### [106] [FleXray: Universal Clinical X-ray Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26756)
*Victor Ion Butoi,Vivek Gopalakrishnan,John V. Guttag,Adrian V. Dalca,Neel Dey*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FleXray：基于物理生成数据引擎的通用全身X光解剖分割模型


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: X光是最常用的成像模态却缺乏定量能力。与CT/MRI等体积模态不同，X光将3D解剖压缩为2D投影，导致结构重叠、边界模糊，即便专家也难以辨别。因此为训练通用分割系统而标注X光数据库不现实，形态学与功能分析仅局限于狭窄的解剖区域和应用。

Method: 提出FleXray通用模型，用于临床X光中全身解剖分割。不依赖人工标注的大规模X光数据集，而是构建可扩展、基于物理的生成式X光数据引擎：利用现有3D全身CT分割数据集与生成式图像编辑模型，模拟具有多样外观、生理属性和成像几何的完全标注2D X光。

Result: 在这些模拟数据上训练的FleXray能够准确分割未见研究数据集及真实场景（in-the-wild）X光中的60个解剖结构。进一步证明FleXray使X光可直接进行定量分析，支持疾病分级的自动测量、X光引导介入中的稳健导航以及病理目标的data-efficient学习。

Conclusion: 作者发布模型、代码、全身X光分割数据集，以及本地易用的浏览器工具（https://flexray.csail.mit.edu）。

Abstract: X-ray is medicine's most widely used imaging modality, yet remains among its least quantitative. Unlike volumetric modalities like CT or MRI, X-ray collapses 3D anatomy into a 2D projection, causing structures to overlap and anatomical boundaries to be ambiguous, even to experts. As a result, labeling X-ray databases for training general-purpose segmentation systems is impractical, leaving morphometric and functional X-ray analysis confined to narrow anatomical regions and applications. To this end, we present FleXray, a generalist model for anatomical segmentation across the entire body in clinical X-rays. Instead of curating large, manually annotated X-ray datasets, we build a scalable, physics-based generative X-ray data engine. Using existing 3D whole-body CT segmentation datasets and generative image-editing models, we simulate fully-annotated 2D X-rays with diverse appearances, physiological properties, and imaging geometries. Trained on these simulations, FleXray accurately segments 60 anatomical structures across unseen research datasets and in-the-wild X-rays. We further show that FleXray makes X-rays directly amenable to quantitative analysis, enabling automated measurements for disease grading, robust navigation during X-ray-guided interventions, and data-efficient learning of pathological targets. We release the model, code, a full-body X-ray segmentation dataset, and a local, easy-to-use browser-based tool at https://flexray.csail.mit.edu .

</details>


### [107] [StableVQ: Practical Guidelines for Stable Vector-Quantized Tokenizer Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26774)
*Bao Tang,Jiahao Guo,Haoxiang Cao,Wenyu Liu,Changqian Yu,Kun Gai,Xinggang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StableVQ 揭示 VQ 训练不稳定源于编解码器与码本的耦合，通过三项解耦设计提升稳定性与利用率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 向量量化（VQ）是离散视觉 tokenizer 的核心，支撑现代自回归与掩码图像生成模型。尽管共享投影码本方法显著提升了码本利用率，训练稳定性仍是关键且尚未充分探索的问题。作者认为根因在于 Encoder–Decoder 与 Codebook 训练的耦合：两者无法各自独立可靠地履行自身职责，系统只有在二者恰好"合作"时才能工作，而这种脆弱条件恰在训练压力最大时失效。

Method: 作者提出 StableVQ，重新审视各模块的合理学习目标，并解决各自独立训练时产生的问题：(1) Dynamic STE——修正 Encoder 学习目标中的不稳定性，使其在码本利用率低时仍能稳健优化重建空间下的离散正则化。(2) Region VQ Loss——重新设计 Codebook 的学习目标，使其无需依赖编码器振荡驱动激活，即可独立保证对编码器输出分布的完整跟踪。(3) Decoupled Schedule——认识到 Encoder–Decoder 与 Codebook 职责不同、需要不同的优化动态，为二者分配独立的学习率调度以保证系统级稳健。方法建立在共享投影码本之上，轻量且不引入可学习参数。

Result: 在 ImageNet 上的实验表明，在多种码本尺寸与初始化设置下，训练稳定性、码本利用率与重建质量均获得一致提升。

Conclusion: StableVQ 通过解耦编解码器与码本的学习目标及优化动态，解决了 VQ 训练稳定性的根本问题，且无需额外可学习参数，具有良好通用性。

Abstract: Vector Quantization (VQ) is fundamental to discrete visual tokenizers that power modern autoregressive and masked image generation models. While recent shared-projection codebook methods have substantially advanced codebook utilization, training stability remains a critical and underexplored challenge. We argue that the root cause lies in the entanglement of the Encoder--Decoder and Codebook training: because neither module can reliably fulfill its own responsibility in isolation, the system can only function when the two subsystems happen to cooperate---a fragile condition that breaks down precisely when training is most stressed. We propose StableVQ, which revisits the proper learning objective of each module and resolves the problems that arise when each is trained to fulfill its own role independently. Concretely, (1) Dynamic STE corrects the instability in the Encoder's learning objective, enabling it to robustly optimize the reconstruction space under discrete regularization even when codebook utilization is low. (2) Region VQ Loss reconceives the Codebook's learning objective so that it can independently guarantee full tracking of the encoder output distribution, without relying on encoder oscillations to drive activation. (3) Decoupled Schedule recognizes that the distinct responsibilities of the Encoder--Decoder and the Codebook demand distinct optimization dynamics, and assigns each an independent learning rate schedule to ensure robust system-level behavior. Built on top of shared-projection codebooks, StableVQ is lightweight and introduces no learnable parameters. Experiments on ImageNet demonstrate consistent improvements in training stability, codebook utilization, and reconstruction quality across diverse codebook sizes and initialization settings.

</details>


### [108] [HARMONY: Hierarchical Agentic Reasoning for MONocular Image-to-Scene Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26793)
*Shufan Sun,Chen Wang,Enxin Song,Jiatao Gu,Lingjie Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HARMONY 提出一种分层思维链框架，结合智能体推理与视觉几何基础模型，从单张单目图像重建具有准确物体间关系和高保真质量的完整3D室内场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合式3D场景重建存在两条路径的局限：智能体推理能提供空间关系的语义理解，但与输入图像缺乏精确对齐；视觉几何基础模型能从图像预测密集点图，但重建质量受限。因此，从单张单目图像恢复具有准确物体间关系与高保真质量的完整3D场景仍具挑战。

Method: HARMONY 从空3D平面图出发：先针对参考图像标定相机，建立语义化的空间坐标系；再用智能体VLM推理恢复3D房间布局与初始摆放顺序；随后按分层顺序摆放物体（壁挂元素、独立家具、家具上的依附装饰），对家具采用深度优先遍历使每次摆放依赖已解析结构，并用反思式反馈循环避免误差累积；每次VLM摆放后用点云估计进行几何精化，使渲染图像更贴合输入。

Result: 在合成与真实图像上的实验表明，HARMONY 优于所评估的重建基线；与 GPT-6 Astra 的定性比较显示其物体摆放更忠实、场景细节保持更好。

Conclusion: HARMONY 能生成语义一致且与参考图像感知对齐的3D场景，将单图组合式重建扩展到复杂室内场景图像。

Abstract: Compositional 3D scene reconstruction has recently been explored from two directions: agentic reasoning that provides semantic understanding of spatial relationships but lacks precise alignment with input images; and visual geometry foundation models that predict dense point maps from input images but the reconstruction quality is limited. Therefore, recovering a complete 3D scene from a single monocular image with accurate inter-object relationships and high-fidelity reconstruction quality remains challenging. In this paper, we present HARMONY, a hierarchical chain-of-thought framework that leverages both agentic reasoning and visual geometry foundation. Given an image of an indoor scene, starting from an empty 3D floorplan, HARMONY first calibrates the camera against the reference image to establish a semantically-grounded spatial frame, then uses agentic VLM reasoning to recover the 3D room layout and an initial placement order. It then places the objects in a hierarchical order, from wall-mounted elements, free-standing furniture, to dependent decorations on top of furniture. We also use depth-first traversal for furniture so each placement conditions on previously resolved structure and a reflective feedback loop to avoid error accumulation. After each object placement by VLM, we use the point cloud estimations to perform geometry-based refinement so that the rendered image aligns better with the input. HARMONY can produce 3D scenes that are semantically consistent and perceptually aligned with the reference image, extending single-image compositional reconstruction to complex indoor scene images. Experiments on synthetic and real-world images demonstrate that HARMONY outperforms the evaluated reconstruction baselines, while qualitative comparisons with GPT-6 Astra suggest more faithful object arrangements and better preservation of scene details.

</details>


<div id='cs.MM'></div>

# cs.MM [[Back]](#toc)

### [109] [CogenPVG: Cognitive-Enhanced Reflective Multi-Agent Framework for Persuasive Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25821)
*Yuntian Xiao,Shoulong Zhang,Wenfeng Song,Yan Wang,Yi Chen,Shuai Li*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出CogenPVG，一个面向说服性视频生成(PVG)任务的认知增强反思式多智能体框架，基于ELM理论将生成过程解耦为四个阶段并取得最佳说服效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 说服性视频生成(PVG)是有价值但研究不足的课题，尽管多模态内容生成取得显著进展，AI自动创建具有强说服力的类人视频仍面临巨大挑战，且现有工作多局限于商业用途。

Method: 提出CogenPVG认知增强反思式多智能体框架：给定用户主题与立场，将生成流程解耦为论证推理、分镜规划、素材创建、后期编辑四个顺序阶段，模拟人类视频制作流程；每个阶段配备一对生成器与评论家智能体，遵循基于ELM说服心理理论的反思式精炼机制；论证推理阶段以批判性思维理论生成逻辑可信的推理，通过ELM中心路径实现认知增强，其余三阶段通过启发式理论生成优化多模态素材作为ELM外围路径。

Result: 大量实验与全面分析表明，该框架取得最佳说服性能，验证了所提多智能体框架在PVG任务上的有效性。

Conclusion: CogenPVG是首个面向通用说服主题(而非局限于商业目的)的工作，其多智能体框架在PVG任务中效果显著。

Abstract: Persuasive video generation (PVG) is a valuable yet under-explored research topic. Despite the significant advances in multimodal content generation, AI-empowered automated creation of human-made-like videos with substantial persuasiveness remains a formidable challenge. In this paper, we propose CogenPVG, a novel Cognitive-Enhanced reflective multi-agent framework tailored for Persuasive Video Generation task. Given the topic and stance from the user, we decouple the sophisticated generation process into four sequential stages: argument reasoning, storyboard planning, asset creation, and post-editing, imitating the workflow of human video producers. To ensure high persuasiveness, each stage is equipped with a pair of generator and critic agents, following a reflective refinement scheme grounded in a solid psychological theory of persuasion, the Elaboration Likelihood Model (ELM). In the argument reasoning stage, we generate highly logical and credible reasoning thoughts under the guidance of critical thinking theory, enabling cognitive enhancement via the central route of the ELM. For the other three stages, we generate and optimize multimodal assets, assembling them into a persuasive video guided by theories of heuristics, as the peripheral route of the ELM. To the best of our knowledge, CogenPVG is the first work focused on general persuasive topics, without being confined to commercial purposes. Extensive experiments and comprehensive analysis demonstrate that our framework achieves the best persuasion performance, thereby proving the effectiveness of our proposed multi-agent framework for the PVG task.

</details>


### [110] [TV-AudioRemover: Joint Text-Visual Guided Sound Removal with Multi-Task Hard-Mixture Curriculum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25864)
*Xinyue Guo,Jianxuan Yang,Daiguo Zhou,Jiagao Hu,Yuxuan Chen,Fei Wang,Jian Luan*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出 TV-AudioRemover，利用视觉编辑后的视频与自然语言指令，从原始音频混合中移除被删除视觉对象所对应的声音，并构建百万级数据集与 AV-Remove-Bench 基准，取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉对象移除后其声音仍残留于音轨，导致音视频不一致；现有视频修复模型仅处理像素，音频编辑模型多依赖文本等单一模态控制，缺乏多模态语义与时间同步线索，效果有限。

Method: 提出文本-视觉引导的声音移除框架，结合视觉编辑视频与自然语言指令抑制目标声音；构建百万级单对象音视频对齐数据集并合成混合-目标配对；在架构中引入任务 token、可泛化指令建模与模态特定全局引导；采用多任务训练与硬混合课程微调。

Result: 提出 AV-Remove-Bench 音视频对象移除基准及客观指标与基于 MLLM 的评估协议；实验表明方法在主观与客观指标上均达到最先进水平。

Conclusion: 通过多模态（文本+视觉）引导与多任务、课程学习训练，可有效实现与视觉移除一致的目标声音移除，为该任务提供了数据、方法与评测的一体化方案。

Abstract: Visual object removal can eliminate a target from video frames, yet its acoustic trace persists in the soundtrack, causing obvious audio-visual inconsistency. Existing video inpainting models operate solely on pixels, while audio editing models, especially for the sound removal task, are typically driven by text and therefore rely on limited single-modal control, which is less effective than multimodal guidance that provides stronger semantic grounding and temporal synchronization cues. In this paper, we present Text-Visual Guided Sound Removal (TV-AudioRemover), a target sound removal framework that leverages the visually edited video together with a natural-language instruction to suppress the sound associated with the removed visual object from the original audio mixture. To acquire high-quality training data, we devise a pipeline to construct a million-scale dataset of single-object audio-visual aligned samples, from which we synthesize mixture-target pairs customized for model training. To effectively leverage visual context and follow instruction intent, we augment the model architecture with task tokens, generalizable instruction modeling, and modality-specific global guidance. We further adopt multi-task training to strengthen task-role comprehension, and employ a hard-mixture curriculum that leverages semantically similar acoustic mixtures during fine-tuning to enhance fine-grained source discrimination. To support evaluation, we present AV-Remove-Bench, a comprehensive audio-visual object removal benchmark, along with dedicated objective metrics and an MLLM-based evaluation protocol. Experiments demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance on both subjective and objective metrics. Project page: https://yjx-research.github.io/TV-AudioRemover/.

</details>


### [111] [KeyBound: Keyed and Host-Bound Learned Audio Watermarking for Speech Provenance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26235)
*Bangshuo Zhu,Yuxin Cao,Weifei Jin,Fusen Guo,Huadong Mo,Jingling Xue,Wei Song*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: KeyBound 提出一种密钥绑定、宿主感知的音频水印方案，用于合成语音溯源，兼顾不可读性、不可移植性与抗重合成能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习型音频水印仅以固定信号失真（噪声、压缩、滤波、重采样）下的载荷恢复率为评判标准，不足以满足溯源需求：水印不应被未授权方读取、不应可移植到无关音频、也不应在现代生成模型重合成后消失。经典水印依赖的密钥与宿主感知载波这两个要素被学习型方案舍弃。

Method: KeyBound 用秘密密钥对载荷进行掩码，并通过由宿主冻结频谱表示所调制的载波嵌入掩码比特：密钥控制载荷的可访问性，宿主条件化载波抵抗直接移植。另设与密钥无关的存在性检测头，使任意方可检测水印，但仅密钥持有者能读取归属信息。

Result: 在单样本均匀性假设下，错误密钥解码通过验证规则的概率至多为 2.1×10⁻³。在 LibriSpeech 上，面对 WavMark、AudioSeal 与 Timbre 均失效的谱去噪器，KeyBound 保持 1.00 检测准确率与 0.98 比特准确率；无密钥时解码为随机水平，并能拒绝被移植的载波。检测能力可迁移至留出的 DAC 与 BigVGAN 重合成，但精确载荷恢复性能下降。

Conclusion: 语音溯源更适合被建模为带密钥、绑定宿主的归属问题，而非在预先固定的信号失真目录下恢复载荷。

Abstract: Audio watermarking is a proactive route to attributing synthetic speech to its source. Learned audio watermarks are typically judged by payload recovery after a fixed catalog of signal distortions such as noise, compression, filtering, and resampling. That test is necessary but not sufficient for provenance. A mark offered as evidence of origin should not be readable by an unauthorized party, should not be transferable to unrelated audio, and should not vanish when the recording is re-synthesized by a modern generative model. We present KeyBound, a learned audio watermark that restores the two ingredients classical watermarking supplied and learned schemes set aside, a secret key and a host-aware carrier. KeyBound masks the payload with a secret key and embeds the masked bits through a carrier modulated by a frozen spectral representation of the host, so the key governs payload access while the host-conditioned carrier resists direct transplantation. A key-independent presence head lets any party detect a mark, whereas only a key holder reads its attribution, and under the single-sample uniformity assumption a wrong-key decode clears our verification rule with probability at most $2.1\times10^{-3}$. On LibriSpeech against WavMark, AudioSeal, and Timbre, KeyBound holds 1.00 detection accuracy and 0.98 bit accuracy under a spectral denoiser that costs every baseline its detection, decodes at chance without the key, and rejects transplanted carriers. Detection further transfers to held-out DAC and BigVGAN re-synthesis, though exact payload recovery degrades. Speech provenance is thus better posed as a keyed, host-bound attribution problem than as the recovery of a payload under a catalog of signal distortions fixed in advance.

</details>


### [112] [ROAM-ASD: Robust Open-World Active Speaker Detection with Flexible Multimodal Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26648)
*Pu Wang,Yujun Wang,Hugo Van hamme*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 提出ROAM-ASD，一个联合建模音频、全脸与细粒度嘴部表征的鲁棒音视频主动说话人检测框架，在五个基准上取得SOTA并提升跨域泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动说话人检测系统依赖可见人脸与语音的可靠关联，但在困难领域或不完整观测下性能明显退化。

Method: 采用统一联合自注意力机制，将音频、全脸、细粒度嘴部等输入流与模态无关的查询token一起处理，实现可用模态输入间的直接交互；并引入模态dropout以增强输入流缺失时的鲁棒性。

Result: 在五个ASD基准上达SOTA：WASD 98.8% mAP、UniTalk 87.9%、AVA 96.5%、ASW 99.3%、Talkies 98.2%，分别较此前最佳提升5.1、4.7、0.9、1.0、2.1 mAP；零样本跨数据集泛化显著提升且对缺失观测保持鲁棒。

Conclusion: ROAM-ASD通过多模态联合建模与模态dropout，在精度、跨域泛化及观测缺失鲁棒性上均有提升。

Abstract: Active speaker detection (ASD) requires reliable association between visible faces and acoustic speech, yet existing systems often degrade under challenging domains or incomplete observations. We introduce ROAM-ASD, a robust audiovisual framework that jointly models audio, full-face, and fine-grained mouth representations. A unified joint self-attention mechanism processes all input streams together with modality-agnostic query tokens, enabling direct interaction among available modality inputs. Modality dropout further improves robustness when input streams are unavailable. ROAM-ASD achieves state-of-the-art performance across five ASD benchmarks: 98.8% mAP on WASD, 87.9% on UniTalk, 96.5% on AVA, 99.3% on ASW, and 98.2% on Talkies, improving over previous best systems by 5.1, 4.7, 0.9, 1.0, and 2.1 mAP points, respectively. ROAM-ASD also substantially improves zero-shot cross-dataset generalization and remains robust to missing observations.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

### [113] [Do Synthetic Personas Predict Real Audience Response? A Sim-to-Real Study Where a No-Persona Baseline Beats Persona-Based Copy Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25010)
*Alexandre Cristovão Maiorano*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究用Upworthy真实A/B测试数据检验LLM"合成人物"预测受众点击的有效性，发现不使用人物设定的零样本基线反而显著优于十人物面板，人物条件化会降低预测效度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 营销者日益将大语言模型当作"合成人物"来预测受众对文案的反应，其依据是"经人物画像条件化的LLM能模仿人类样本"这一证据。但这一预测对真实行为是否有效、人物设定机制本身是否有帮助，尚缺乏严格验证。作者希望用真实A/B测试数据做仿真到现实（sim-to-real）的有效性研究。

Method: 以Upworthy研究档案中数千个共享真实流量的标题A/B测试（含实测点击率）作为留出真值。比较两类模型：一是基于真实受众人口统计特征构建的十人物面板；二是不设人物、直接询问模型"典型读者点击可能性"的零样本基线。效度只能在有统计可区分胜者的可靠子集（n=399）上测量。并检验跨三个Upworthy切分、另一领域新闻数据集、随机种子、提示措辞及模型选择（三个Gemini层级与OpenAI gpt-4.1）的稳健性。

Result: 真值可靠性是核心约束：多数A/B测试无统计上可区分的胜者。与人物模拟前提相反，人物条件化降低了预测效度：无人物基线的排序表现明显更好（Kendall τ=0.361，中等效应；top-1准确率49.2%），优于人物面板（τ=0.084；top-1 34.6%），且置信区间不重叠。结果在三个独立切分中可复现，在另一领域数据集方向一致，并对种子、措辞和模型选择稳健（含OpenAI gpt-4.1的显著配对差距）。全部数字可通过公开复现包重新生成。

Conclusion: 在预测总体参与度时，普通LLM排序器优于人物模拟；合成人物不仅是弱预测器，而是比不使用更差。直接询问模型可获取准确的总体层面先验，而强迫其扮演特定人物会引入偏差与噪声。

Abstract: Marketers increasingly use large language models (LLMs) as "synthetic personas" to predict how an audience will react to a piece of copy before it ships, encouraged by evidence that profile-conditioned LLMs mimic human samples. But is that prediction actually valid against real behaviour - and does the persona machinery help? We present a sim-to-real validity study using the Upworthy Research Archive - thousands of headline A/B tests on shared real traffic, with measured click-through - as held-out ground truth. We compare a ten-persona panel, grounded in the real audience's demographics, against a no-persona zero-shot baseline that simply asks the model how likely a typical reader is to click. Two findings stand out. First, ground-truth reliability is the binding constraint: most A/B tests have no statistically distinguishable winner, so validity can only be measured on the reliable subset (n = 399). Second, and counter to the persona-simulation premise, persona conditioning degrades predictive validity: the no-persona baseline ranks variants markedly better (Kendall τ = 0.361, a medium effect; top-1 accuracy 49.2%) than the persona panel (τ = 0.084; top-1 34.6%), with non-overlapping confidence intervals. Asking the model directly taps an accurate population-level prior; forcing it to role-play specific personas injects bias and noise. The result replicates across three independent Upworthy splits, holds in direction on a different-domain news dataset, and is robust to seed, prompt phrasing, and model choice - across three Gemini tiers and a different model family (OpenAI gpt-4.1, significant paired gap). The takeaway: for predicting aggregate engagement, a plain LLM ranker beats persona simulation - synthetic personas are not merely a weak predictor, they are worse than not using them. All numbers regenerate from a public, artifact-first replication package.

</details>


### [114] [Do Existing Preconditioners Improve Biomedical Tabular Foundation Learning? An Empirical Study on TabPFN Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25013)
*M. Sajid,Pinki Khatun,M. Tanveer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究对TabPFN v2.5在59个生物医学数据集上的5种AdamW预条件策略进行实证比较，发现原始AdamW表现最佳，现有曲率感知预条件方法未能带来可靠改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格基础模型在结构化生物医学数据分析中潜力巨大，TabPFN在低数据分类任务中表现有效，但优化与预条件策略对生物医学微调的影响尚缺乏系统探索。

Method: 系统评估5种基于AdamW的预条件策略，用于微调TabPFN v2.5，覆盖59个生物医学数据集（含阿尔茨海默病、乳腺癌、精神分裂症、SMC、KEEL及UCI基准），从预测性能、计算效率与统计显著性三方面分析。

Result: 原始AdamW优化器在整体性能与统计排名上始终最优；现有曲率感知预条件方法在多样化生物医学场景中未能提供可靠提升。

Conclusion: 通用预条件方法难以充分刻画生物医学表格学习的优化特性，需针对医疗导向表格基础模型开发生物医学感知的专用预条件器。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models have recently shown strong potential for structured biomedical data analysis. Among them, TabPFN has emerged as an effective approach for low-data tabular classification tasks. However, the impact of optimization and preconditioning strategies on biomedical fine-tuning remains largely unexplored. In this work, we present a comprehensive empirical investigation of five AdamW-based preconditioning strategies for fine-tuning TabPFN v2.5 on 59 biomedical datasets spanning Alzheimer's disease, breast cancer, schizophrenia, significant memory concern (SMC), KEEL biomedical datasets, and UCI biomedical benchmarks. The evaluation considers predictive performance, computational efficiency, and statistical significance analysis. Experimental results demonstrate that the original AdamW optimizer consistently achieves the best overall performance and statistical ranking, while existing curvature-aware preconditioners fail to provide reliable improvements across diverse biomedical learning scenarios. The findings suggest that generic preconditioning approaches may not adequately capture the optimization characteristics of biomedical tabular learning, motivating the development of biomedical-aware preconditioners specifically tailored for healthcare-oriented tabular foundation models.

</details>


### [115] [4DGS-JEPA: Temporally Compositional Joint-Embedding Prediction for Dynamic Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25036)
*Yongchao Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出4DGS-JEPA，一种面向动态高斯场景因果多步预测的联合嵌入预测架构，通过时间组合性与混合对应机制实现可复用动力学预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态高斯泼溅方法主要面向重建、未来状态生成或渲染，而非学习可复用的预测性动力学。论文希望在高斯原生表示上建立能够因果地进行多时间步预测的架构。

Method: 采用场景级、运动组级、高斯级的层次化表示，配合以时间步为条件的转移算子，支持直接预测与递归展开。核心为时间组合原理：不同时间顺序路径到达同一未来端点时应产生兼容的预测状态。使用端点与多步路径监督锚定未来目标嵌入，并通过选择性几何解码器和几何级组合约束组运动与高斯几何，无需完整未来外观重建。此外引入混合对应机制，结合持续规范身份与残差最优传输匹配以应对重排与拓扑变化。

Result: 理论上刻画了零损失路径一致性与有限误差递归展开累积。三项受控实验提供机制级证据：时间组合降低潜在路径依赖同时保持预测精度；几何级组合提升解码运动一致性；混合对应在保持身份可靠性的同时应对模糊对应。

Conclusion: 4DGS-JEPA为动态高斯世界提供了预测性、时间组合性的表述框架。

Abstract: Dynamic Gaussian Splatting provides an explicit representation of evolving 3D scenes, but existing approaches are primarily optimized for reconstruction, future-state generation, or rendering rather than for learning reusable predictive dynamics. We propose 4DGS-JEPA, a Gaussian-native joint-embedding predictive architecture for causal multi-horizon prediction over dynamic Gaussian scenes. The model uses a hierarchical scene-, motion-group-, and Gaussian-level representation together with a horizon-conditioned transition operator that supports both direct prediction and recursive rollout. Its central principle is temporal composition: different chronological transition paths reaching the same future endpoint should produce compatible predictive states. Endpoint and multi-horizon path supervision anchor these predictions to future target embeddings, while a selective geometry decoder and geometry-level composition ground the learned dynamics in consistent group motion and Gaussian geometry without requiring complete future appearance reconstruction. We further introduce a hybrid correspondence mechanism that combines persistent canonical identity with residual optimal-transport matching under reordering and topology change. We characterize zero-loss path agreement and finite-error rollout accumulation theoretically. Three controlled experiments provide mechanism-level evidence that temporal composition reduces latent path dependence while retaining predictive accuracy, geometry-level composition improves consistency of decoded motion, and hybrid correspondence preserves reliable identity while remaining robust when correspondence becomes ambiguous. Together, 4DGS-JEPA provides a predictive, temporally compositional formulation of dynamic Gaussian worlds.

</details>


### [116] [An Accurate and Interpretable Hyper Graph Neural Network for GBM Survival Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25088)
*Mushahid Intesum*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出一个多模态框架，将sheaf超图神经网络、概念瓶颈层与扩展充分性检验(EST)正则化统一起来，在GBM生存预测中同时实现高精度与事前可解释性，在UPenn-GBM数据集上达到C-index 0.643且方差最低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GBM生存预测方法将准确性与可解释性视为此消彼长的目标：高性能模型牺牲透明度，可解释模型牺牲预测能力。作者认为这一权衡并非固有，且现有方法多依赖单一成像模态，未能利用多模态MRI与临床元数据的互补信息。

Method: 提出包含三组件的多模态框架：(1) sheaf超图神经网络，通过有向非对称消息传递捕获组织patch间的高阶关系；(2) 概念瓶颈层，将学习到的表示压缩为临床相关概念，实现事前可解释性；(3) 扩展充分性检验(EST)正则化器，在训练中惩罚不忠实的解释。临床与基因组特征通过门控融合引入，保留分子标志物的主导预后信号并维持概念级可追溯性。

Result: 在UPenn-GBM数据集593名患者上采用5折交叉验证，框架取得一致性指数(C-index)0.643，且在所有对比模型中折间方差最低(std=0.015)。

Conclusion: 据作者所知，这是首个将sheaf超图卷积、概念瓶颈监督与EST正则化统一用于脑MRI可解释生存预测的工作，表明准确性与可解释性可以兼得。

Abstract: Survival prediction for glioblastoma multiforme (GBM) demands models that are both accurate and interpretable, yet existing approaches treat these objectives as com- peting, where performant models sacrifice transparency, while interpretable models accept degraded predictive power. We argue that this trade-off is not inherent. Graph neural net- works offer a structural foundation for extracting interpretable, explainable representations without compromising discriminative ability. Furthermore, current methods typically rely on a single imaging modality, underutilizing the complementary information available across multi-modal MRI and clinical metadata. We propose a multi-modal framework that inte- grates three components to address both objectives simultaneously: (1) a sheaf hypergraph neural network that captures higher-order relationships among tissue patches through direc- tional, asymmetric message passing; (2) a concept bottleneck layer that compresses learned representations into clinically grounded concepts, enforcing ante-hoc interpretability; and (3) an extension sufficiency test (EST) regularizer that penalizes unfaithful explanations during training, ensuring that model explanations genuinely reflect the internal decision process. Clinical and genomic features are incorporated through gated fusion, preserving the dominant prognostic signal of molecular markers while retaining concept-level traceabil- ity. Evaluated on 593 patients from the UPenn-GBM dataset under 5-fold cross-validation, our framework achieves a concordance index of 0.643 with the lowest fold-level variance among all compared models (std = 0.015). To our knowledge, this is the first work to unify sheaf hypergraph convolution, concept bottleneck supervision, and EST regularization for interpretable survival prediction from brain MRI

</details>


### [117] [Ovis-Embedding: Pushing the Frontiers of Universal Omni-Modal Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25165)
*Ovis-Embedding Team*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Ovis-Embedding全模态嵌入模型家族，基于Qwen-omni共享骨干实现文本、图像、视频、音频统一表示，在MMEB-v3/v2、MVEB、MAEB、RTEB等基准上取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有嵌入模型多采用分离的模态塔结构，导致模态割裂，难以支持任意模态间的统一检索，需要原生全模态统一训练方案。

Method: 采用预训练Qwen-omni作为共享嵌入骨干并结合低秩初始化的对比训练；构建覆盖文本、图像、视频、音频及交错数据的语料，并提出同源采样以构造任务一致批次与有效批内负样本；训练上用focal loss强化难例，并用基于相似度的嵌入蒸馏从专家模型迁移细粒度相似结构；推理时用低秩特征分解实现紧凑、可变维度嵌入。

Result: 在MMEB-v3、MMEB-v2、MVEB、MAEB和RTEB上均达到最先进水平，覆盖文本、图像、视频、音频各模态，且推理时能以极小性能损失支持灵活维度。

Conclusion: 统一全模态训练可有效缓解模态碎片化问题，推动面向任意模态检索的通用嵌入模型发展。

Abstract: In this report, we introduce \textbf{Ovis-Embedding}, a state-of-the-art omni-modal embedding family built on native integration of text, image, video, and audio. Instead of assembling separate modality towers, Ovis-Embedding uses a shared multimodal backbone to encode different modalities in a common representation space. Specifically, we make \textbf{three key advances}: (1) \textbf{native omni-modal initialization}: we adopt a pretrained Qwen-omni model as the embedding backbone and adapt it through contrastive training with low-rank initialization; (2) \textbf{data-centric omni-modal training}: we construct a broad, high-quality corpus spanning text, images, video, audio, and interleaved multimodal data. To improve data efficiency, we introduce homogeneous-source sampling to form task-consistent batches with informative in-batch negatives; and (3) \textbf{embedding-specific training and inference optimization}: we use focal loss to emphasize hard examples and similarity-based Embedding Distillation to transfer fine-grained similarity structure from complementary experts. At inference time, low-rank feature decomposition enables compact embeddings with flexible dimensionality and minimal performance loss. Empirical evaluations show that the \textbf{Ovis-Embedding} family achieves state-of-the-art performance on \textbf{MMEB-v3}, \textbf{MMEB-v2}, \textbf{MVEB}, \textbf{MAEB}, and \textbf{RTEB}, demonstrating its effectiveness across text, image, video, and audio modalities. These results highlight the potential of unified omni-modal training to overcome modality fragmentation and advance universal embedding models for any-to-any retrieval.

</details>


### [118] [X-Planner: Event-Structured Task Planning for Embodied Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25187)
*Howard Lu,Shalfun Li,Porter Pan,Cris,Lumen,Cyril,Eric Hu,Lily Li,Maeve Zhang,Robert Wang,KZ Zheng,Viggo Chen,Tim Ding,Regsis Cheng,YJ Xiao,Kian,Hai Lin,Alan Song,Elise Ma,Gody Li,Victor Yao,Yohann Tang,Ingrid Yu,Jason He,James Wang,Ryan Yu,Ping Yang,Chris Pan,Vincent Chen,Roy Gan,Hao Wang,Qian Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: X-Planner是一个面向长时域操作的规划前端，同时解决具身推理的监督数据与表示形式问题：构建层级化规划数据并设计离散/潜空间双语式规划接口，离线规划评测与真机实验均验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代视觉-语言-动作（VLA）系统缺乏显式的任务规划中间结构；已有链式思考（CoT）规划器依赖粗粒度任务级标注，且需逐token串行化冗长推理轨迹，导致监督粗、表示效率低。

Method: 数据侧：融合Ego、UMI与遥操作数据，采用层级粒度与依赖来源的标注深度，并以接管时刻标注和人工设计的失败案例监督在线错误识别。模型侧：共享VLM主干输出两种事件结构规划形式——离散接口输出可解释事件状态，潜空间接口通过Staircase Decoding在交错Transformer深度间传递连续CoT状态，并用冻结的潜空间到文本重建目标提供语义锚定。

Result: 离线两步规划评测中，X-Planner在BERTScore-F1与评审制整体得分上均位列四个评测模型中的第二；真机实验中分别优于所评测的基线。

Conclusion: X-Planner通过层级化数据监督与事件结构化的双接口规划表示，兼顾了规划文本质量与下游执行表现，为具身任务规划提供了一种有效前端方案。

Abstract: Task planning bridges high-level instructions and executable behavior in long-horizon manipulation, yet modern Vision-Language-Action (VLA) systems often leave this intermediate structure implicit. Existing chain-of-thought (CoT) planners also tend to rely on coarse task-level annotations or serialize long reasoning traces token by token. We present X-Planner, a planning front-end that addresses both the supervision and representation of embodied reasoning. Our planning data combine Ego, UMI, and teleoperation under a hierarchy granularity with source-dependent annotation depth. Takeover-time annotations and human-designed failures supervise ongoing error recognition. On the model side, a shared VLM backbone exposes two event-structured plan forms: a discrete interface that emits interpretable event states and a latent interface that relays continuous CoT states across staggered Transformer depths through Staircase Decoding. A frozen latent-to-text reconstruction objective provides a semantic anchor for the latent representation. Offline two-step planning evaluation places X-Planner second among four evaluated models on both BERTScore-F1 and a judge-based Overall score. In real-robot experiments, respectively, outperforming the evaluated baselines. These results characterize planning-text quality and downstream execution.

</details>


### [119] [Lean Pool: An AI-Maintained Archive of Formalized Mathematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25199)
*Vasily Ilin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Lean Pool 是一个由 AI 智能体负责扩充、维护与优化的数学形式化代码库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数学形式化工作耗时费力，依赖人工编写与维护，难以规模化。该工作尝试用 AI 智能体自动承担形式化数学库的构建与优化任务。

Method: 构建 Lean Pool 仓库，以 Lean 作为形式化语言，由 AI 智能体完成定理的形式化、库的扩充、日常维护与性能优化。

Result: 摘要未提供具体实验数据或量化指标。

Conclusion: 提出了一种以 AI 智能体自主驱动数学形式化库建设的模式，为形式化数学的规模化发展提供了新思路。

Abstract: Lean Pool is a repository of formalized mathematics. It is grown, maintained and optimized by AI agents.

</details>


### [120] [The AI Neuroscientist: An Interactive Agentic Interface for Neuroimaging Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25254)
*Aakash Patel,Panos Ketonis,Shreya Saxena,Smita Krishnaswamy,David van Dijk*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"AI Neuroscientist"语言智能体，将大语言模型与神经影像工具集结合，支持用自然语言完成质量控制、建模与可视化，并以fNIRS数据验证其交互式探索能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经影像数据分析需要专业的编程与统计技能，对缺乏计算背景的研究者构成门槛，因此需要一种更易用、透明的交互式分析方式。

Method: 将大语言模型与神经影像工具集集成，构建语言智能体，使其能够执行质量控制、建模与可视化，并允许研究者用自然语言查询数据质量、指定分析参数；在自建的fNIRS基准测试集上，与带代码沙箱的通用LLM智能体进行对比评估。

Result: 在fNIRS数据上展示了系统的质量控制、建模和可视化能力，并在自定义基准测试中与通用LLM智能体进行了对比评估。

Conclusion: 该系统为小规模数据探索提供了透明、交互式的替代方案；未来将把架构扩展到fMRI等其他模态，并扩充基准测试任务。

Abstract: Analyzing neuroimaging data requires specialized coding and statistical expertise, which limits accessibility for researchers without computational backgrounds. We present the AI Neuroscientist, a language agent for interactive data exploration. The system integrates a large language model (LLM) with a neuroimaging toolset to perform quality control, modeling, and visualization. This allows researchers to query data quality and specify analysis parameters directly in natural language, providing a transparent and interactive alternative to conventional scripted pipelines for small-scale data exploration. We demonstrate these capabilities using functional near-infrared spectroscopy (fNIRS) data, and evaluate the agent on a custom fNIRS benchmarking suite against general-purpose LLM agents with code sandboxes. Future extensions will generalize the architecture to additional modalities, including functional magnetic resonance imaging (fMRI) data, and expand the benchmarking suite to additional fNIRS tasks.

</details>


### [121] [MedGate-Fusion: Integrating First-Encounter Semantic Narratives and Physiological Biomarkers for Prospective Stroke Risk Stratification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25272)
*Hemn Khdr,Mohammad Noaeen,Karim Keshavjee,Aziz Guergachi,Zahra Shakeri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 MedGate-Fusion，一种多模态门控架构，将首诊叙述的 Transformer 嵌入与十项常规风险标志物融合，用于初级保健中的前瞻性卒中风险分层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 初级保健中的前瞻性卒中风险分层较为困难，因为早期风险信号分散在常规生物标志物和非结构化的临床叙述中，单一数据来源难以充分利用。

Method: 采用加拿大初级保健哨点监测网络（CPCSSN）的电子病历数据。以 808,921 条就诊级观测为起点，构建首诊队列，保留 102,736 份具有非空叙述且数据充分的患者记录，用于评估五年卒中结局。为减少笔记中诊断提及导致的显式目标泄漏，在语义编码前对卒中相关术语进行了基于词典的脱敏处理。模型使用基于 Transformer 的文本嵌入与十项常规风险标志物，通过多模态门控架构进行融合。

Result: 摘要未报告具体性能指标；研究构建了包含 102,736 份患者记录的首诊队列，并完成了卒中相关术语的脱敏处理，为后续五年的卒中结局评估奠定数据基础。

Conclusion: MedGate-Fusion 提供了一种融合结构化风险标志物与临床叙述的多模态框架，有望改善初级保健中的前瞻性卒中风险分层，并强调了脱敏处理对减少标签泄漏的重要性。

Abstract: Prospective stroke risk stratification in primary care is challenging because early risk signals are distributed across routine biomarkers and unstructured clinical narratives. We propose MedGate-Fusion, a multi-modal gated architecture that integrates transformer-based embeddings of first-encounter narratives with ten routinely recorded risk markers. We used electronic medical record data from the Canadian Primary Care Sentinel Surveillance Network (CPCSSN). Starting from 808,921 encounter-level observations, we constructed a first-encounter cohort and retained 102,736 unique patient records with non-empty narratives and sufficient data to evaluate a five-year stroke outcome. To reduce explicit target leakage from diagnostic mentions in notes, we applied dictionary-based redaction of stroke-related terms prior to semantic encoding.

</details>


### [122] [When LLM Agents Fail to Read the Room: ReAdapt for Relational Social Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25284)
*Jianzhe Lin,Xiaolin Li,Yunda Liu,Fei Wang,Jubin Chheda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出关系推理基准与ReAdapt方法，通过显式社会状态更新，提升LLM智能体在社交决策中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交智能体的基本决策并非纯内容问题，而依赖人际关系（关系强度、互惠、共同连接等）。标准LLM智能体循环未显式表示新关系证据如何修正社会假设，导致当关系线索与内容线索冲突时倾向于表面明显的选择。

Method: 构建关系推理基准：500个合成社交世界（含好友、关注、互动历史、信息流），生成两类任务共1000个查询（反应选择、温暖介绍），约53%查询中表面候选与关系真值不同，形成"推翻子集"。提出ReAdapt，在ReAct循环中加入结构化社会状态z=(G,B,R,N,D)，每次工具观察后执行类型化Adapt步骤，更新状态并输出策略操作（继续、切换、放弃、澄清），再选择下一步动作。

Result: 在Gemini-3-Flash、每任务n=150分层子集上，温暖介绍准确率从37%提升至51%（+14），反应选择准确率从69%提升至77%（+8）；Oracle regret分别从0.260降至0.152、从0.095降至0.053。

Conclusion: 在模型、工具与环境固定条件下，显式关系状态自适应有助于LLM智能体将检索到的社会证据转化为修正后的决策。

Abstract: A social agent's most basic decisions (should I react to this post? who should I reach out to?) are not purely content problems. The right action often hinges on the latent relationship between people -- tie strength, reciprocity, mutual connections -- rather than on which content is most salient. Standard LLM agent loops do not explicitly represent how new relational evidence should revise the agent's current social hypothesis, leaving them prone to surface-obvious choices when relational and content cues diverge. We formalize this failure mode with a relationship-reasoning benchmark: 500 synthetic social worlds with friendships, follows, reaction histories, and feeds, yielding 1,000 queries over two tasks, reaction selection and warm introduction (finding the best bridge to a target person). By construction, the surface-obvious candidate differs from the relationship-grounded oracle in about 53% of queries, forming an overturn subset where the agent must use relational evidence to revise an initially plausible choice. We propose ReAdapt (Relationship-Adaptive Agent with Policy-driven sTate), which augments the ReAct loop with an explicit structured social state z = (G, B, R, N, D) capturing goal, belief, relationship, norm, and disclosure. After each tool observation, ReAdapt runs a typed Adapt step that updates this state and emits a policy operation (continue, switch, abandon, or clarify) before choosing the next action. With Gemini-3-Flash on a stratified subset of n = 150 queries per task, ReAdapt improves warm-introduction accuracy from 37% to 51% (+14 points) and reaction-selection accuracy from 69% to 77% (+8 points). Oracle regret drops from 0.260 to 0.152 and from 0.095 to 0.053, respectively. Holding the model, tools, and environments fixed, these results suggest that explicit relational-state adaptation helps LLM agents turn retrieved social evidence into revised decisions.

</details>


### [123] [Attention as a Routing Graph: Live Circuit Extraction from a Single Forward Pass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25285)
*Ash Manvi,Samreena Tajreen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种廉价方法，通过单次前向传播将注意力视为路由图，提取指向答案的小规模边集合，并验证这些边在已知任务上具有真实因果作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统寻找语言模型电路通常需要大量精心设计的干预实验，成本高昂。作者希望用更简单、更便宜的方式获得有因果意义的电路草图。

Method: 将注意力解释为一次前向传播中的路由图，保留少量指向答案的路由/边，并通过消融实验检验这些边是否重要。在 GPT-2 Small、GPT-2 Medium 和 Pythia-410M 上评估，每格 n=100 个提示，使用配对差距检验和 bootstrap 置信区间。

Result: 在归纳头和 IOI 等已知正确电路的任务上，消融提取出的边对模型性能的伤害显著大于消融同规模随机边。提取步骤只需一次前向传播，而逐头 patch sweep 成本约高出两个数量级。

Conclusion: 该方法并非完整电路图谱，而是一种廉价草图，在已知任务上携带真实因果信号，同时也存在明确失败模式。代码与评估工件已开源。

Abstract: Finding circuits in language models usually means running many careful interventions. We try something simpler: treat attention as a routing map from one forward pass, keep a small set of routes that point toward the answer, and ask whether those routes actually matter.
  They often do. On induction and IOI (tasks where the "right" circuit is already known), ablating our extracted edges hurts the model much more than ablating a random set of the same size. We evaluate n=100 prompts per cell on GPT-2 Small, GPT-2 Medium, and Pythia-410M, with paired gap tests and bootstrap confidence intervals. The extract step costs one forward; a head-by-head patch sweep costs about two orders of magnitude more.
  We are not claiming a complete circuit atlas. We are claiming a cheap sketch that carries real causal signal on known tasks, with clear failure modes when it does not. Code and evaluation artifacts are at https://github.com/Aquinf03/live-circuit-routing.

</details>


### [124] [Learned Enterprise Data Comprehension: Compression and Routing for Data Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25286)
*Ethan Torres,Eric Mills*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"潜在等价学习"(latent equivalence learning)框架，用于企业结构化数据智能体，将持久任务身份与其在特定数据集中的实现分离，通过支持/反对证据塑造的高斯原型与查询-原型兼容路由，预先组织证据供智能体推理。在Data Agent Benchmark上取得94.67%的Pass@1，远超基线55.51%，位列榜首。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业场景中的结构化数据智能体必须在跨schema、关系、策略与业务角色的复杂数据环境中推理。现有系统依赖可复用的markdown式记忆或技能文件来保存已发现信息，虽能减少重复探索，却掩盖了更合理的分工：智能体擅长语义推理，而学习系统擅长预测与组织重复出现的结构。因此需要一个能桥接二者、避免每次查询都重建跨schema结构的框架。

Method: 提出潜在等价学习框架，将持久、任务相关的身份(identity)与其数据集相关的实现(realization)分离。具体实现中，支持性与反对性证据共同塑造"支持实现的高斯原型"，学习身份在特定数据环境中的表达方式，并用软隶属度(soft-membership)画像保留硬分配所丢失的区分。另设独立的学习查询-原型系统表示重复出现的证据需求，并通过学习到的兼容函数映射到同一持久身份结构。该身份因子化、查询条件的路由机制为下游推理物化出相关的数据集特定证据。

Result: 在Data Agent Benchmark（涵盖12个异构数据集、54个查询）上，完整实现经过五次完整试验取得94.67%的数据集宏平均分层Pass@1，258/270次原始查询成功；相比之下基准的Claude Opus 4.6参考智能体仅为55.51%，提交时在40个排行榜条目中排名第一。

Conclusion: 通过将身份与数据集相对实现解耦并用查询条件路由预组织证据，智能体可在已组织的证据状态上运作而非每次重建结构，显著优于强基线，验证了潜在等价学习在企业结构化数据智能体中的有效性。

Abstract: Structured-data agents in enterprise settings must reason over complex data environments whose relevant evidence is distributed across schemas, relationships, policies, and recurring business roles. Modern agentic systems often address this burden through reusable markdown-style memory or skill files that preserve previously discovered information for later queries, reducing the need to rediscover the same structure repeatedly. This is useful, but it obscures a natural division of labor: agents are well suited to semantic reasoning, while learned systems are well suited to predicting and organizing recurring structure. We introduce latent equivalence learning to bridge this gap. The framework separates persistent task-relevant identities from their dataset-relative realizations. In our realization, supporting and opposing evidence shape support-realized Gaussian prototypes that learn how those identities are expressed in a particular data environment, while soft-membership profiles retain distinctions lost under a hard assignment. A separate learned query-prototype system represents recurring evidential requirements and maps them through a learned compatibility function into the same persistent identity structure. This identity-factorized, query-conditioned routing materializes the relevant dataset-specific evidence for downstream reasoning, allowing the agent to operate over an already organized evidential state rather than reconstructing cross-schema structure at every query. On the Data Agent Benchmark, spanning 54 queries across 12 heterogeneous datasets, our full implementation achieves 94.67% dataset-macro stratified Pass@1 over five complete trials and 258/270 successful raw query attempts, compared with 55.51% for the benchmark's Claude Opus 4.6 reference agent, ranking first among 40 leaderboard entries at submission.

</details>


### [125] [Making Agents More Consistent: Skills Should Form Habits for Repeat Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25299)
*Travis Weber,Rohit Taneja*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"技能习惯形成"机制：智能体挖掘自身执行历史，生成确定性技能变体与原有推理竞争，通过四道递增成本的门控准入，从而提升一致性、降低token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体在重复任务上表现不一致（42个任务三次运行，38%–74%结果不一致），而买家、审计者和监管者都要求一致性；同时智能体极度浪费算力，95.3%–97.2%的生成内容用于重复推导系统已知的计划。

Method: 让智能体从自身执行历史中挖掘候选技能——确定性变体，与现有方案竞争而非替换。候选技能声明其所覆盖的输入空间区域，常见情况作为脚本运行，其余回退到推理。设置四道成本递增的门控筛选候选，核心门控用保留的参考执行轨迹对候选轨迹做一致性测试，容差由参考自身运行的波动性确定。

Result: 在文本到SQL任务上，四个推理分支中三个在42个重复问题中仅11–13个能复现自身输出，第四个为26个；而习惯形成变体在全部456次重复调度中均能复现，且不劣于所替代的任一分支（p<0.0001），token消耗减少14%–56%，在7–53次复用后转为净收益。同时测量了准确性代价：门控在2.6%的自然改述和26%的边界附近输入上放行了本应推迟的工作，13个此类失败中11个在任何阈值下都无法被轨迹一致性门控发现。确定性错误会精确重复——坏习惯和好习惯一样可靠，这正是系统可审计性的代价。将路由与参数提取分离后，端到端准确率从0.888提升到0.952，成本为原来的43%。

Conclusion: 技能习惯形成通过确定性技能复用显著提升智能体一致性并节省token，其代价是确定性错误会如好习惯般稳定重复；分离路由与参数提取可在较低成本下进一步提升准确率。

Abstract: On repeated work, agents are inconsistent. We ran 42 tasks three times each and found that, depending on the model, 38% to 74% returned answers that did not agree. Consistency is what a buyer, an auditor, or a regulator requires, and agents do not have it. They are wasteful too: 95.3% to 97.2% of what an agent generates goes to re-deriving a plan the system already knows.
  We propose skill habit formation. An agent mines its own execution history for candidate skills, deterministic variants that compete against the incumbent rather than replacing it. A candidate declares the region of input space it claims, so the common case runs as a script and the rest falls through to reasoning. Four gates of ascending cost admit candidates; the central one tests a candidate's execution trace against a retained reference, within a tolerance measured from that reference's own run-to-run variability.
  On text-to-SQL, three of four reasoning arms reproduced their own output on 11 to 13 of 42 repeated questions and the fourth on 26 of 42, while a habit-formed variant reproduced on all 456 dispatches we repeated and was non-inferior to every arm it replaced (p<0.0001). It also used 14% to 56% fewer tokens, turning net positive after 7 to 53 reuses.
  We measured what this costs in accuracy. The guard admitted work it should have deferred on 2.6% of natural paraphrases and 26% of inputs near its boundary, and 11 of 13 such failures were invisible to the trace-conformance gate at any threshold. Deterministic errors repeat exactly: a bad habit is as reliable as a good one, and that is the price of the property that makes the system auditable. Separating routing from parameter extraction raised end-to-end accuracy from 0.888 to 0.952 at 43% of the cost.

</details>


### [126] [Potential for Enhanced Learning in Machine Learning Classes by Using Wiki LLM Indexing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25303)
*Brian Wright*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文比较了同一课程语料下的两种知识表示方式（向量RAG与LLM编译wiki），发现二者在单点事实问答上表现相当，但在跨单元概念关联问题上，编译wiki在准确性和可溯源性上明显更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型越来越多地被用作课程专属辅导工具，但其有效性依赖于经过审核的教学材料；这些材料常在学期中途修订。作者前期构建了面向真实机器学习课程语料的多模态RAG系统，发现检索能改善上下文 grounding，但固定检索策略并非最优。由此引出新问题：语料在入库时的结构化方式，是否比查询时检索多少更重要。

Method: 在相同的课堂语料上进行受控的正面比较，对比两种知识表示：(A) 向量RAG，复现前期研究中表现最佳的配置；(B) LLM编译wiki（Karpathy框架），在入库时将语料合成为带明确交叉引用和来源引用的互联概念页面。评估59个问题，涵盖单点事实回忆、跨单元概念关联、综合与解释、以及教学大纲修订后的时效性，由LLM评委依据人工编写的评分标准打分。

Result: 两种表示在单点事实问题上表现大致相当（9.33 对 9.96，满分10）。但在需要跨课程单元关联的问题上差异显著：编译wiki保持准确且有依据（9.93；100% 有引用来源支撑），而检索方式得分更低且明显缺乏依据（8.14；64%）。wiki的引用使学生和教师能够将任何陈述追溯到引入该内容的讲座。

Conclusion: 编译wiki的引用机制增加了一层动态检索能力，这正是机器学习课程所需的。尽管仍需进一步测试，但使用AI支持机器学习课程学习的教师，应考虑采用基于wiki的结构，因为它有潜力支撑最佳实践的基础要素。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed as course-specific tutors, but their usefulness depends on grounding in vetted instructional materials that are often revised mid-semester. Our prior work built a multimodal retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) system over an authentic machine learning course corpus (Foundations of Machine Learning) and found that retrieval improved contextual grounding, but that fixed retrieval strategies were suboptimal. That motivates a different question: whether how a corpus is structured at ingest time matters more than how much is retrieved at query time. We present a controlled head-to-head comparison of two knowledge representations over an identical classroom corpus: (A) vector RAG, replicating the best-performing configuration from our prior study, and (B) an LLM-compiled wiki (Karpathy framework), in which the corpus is synthesized at ingest into linked concept pages with explicit cross-references and citations back to source materials. We evaluate 59 questions spanning single-fact recall, cross-unit concept linking, synthesis and explanation, and currency after a syllabus revision, scored by an LLM judge against a human-authored rubric. Both representations answered single-fact questions about equally well (9.33 vs. 9.96 of 10), but diverged sharply on questions requiring links across course units. The compiled wiki remained accurate and grounded (9.93; 100% grounded in cited sources), while retrieval scored lower and was markedly less grounded (8.14; 64%). The wiki's citations let students and instructors trace any claim back to the lecture that introduced it, adding a layer of dynamic retrieval that machine learning courses require. While further testing is needed, instructors using AI to support learning in ML courses should consider wiki-based structure for its potential to support foundational elements of best practice.

</details>


### [127] [Clarification Is Not Correction: LLMs Fail to Let Go](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25337)
*Jianzhe Lin,Xiaolin Li,Fei Wang,Robert Douglas,Rajeshkumar Golani,Jubin Chheda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出“早期后验坍缩”（early posterior collapse）现象：语言模型在对话中面对早期歧义时，不是遗忘信息，而是过早将未解决的意图固化为单一任务状态，导致后续澄清无法有效纠正，反而被当作补充上下文来精炼已过时状态。作者主张研究应从“保留更多上下文”转向“保留不确定性”的状态管理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常把对话失败归因于记忆问题，如上下文过长、摘要失真、约束遗忘。作者认为这一视角忽略了更深层的问题：模型在许多对话中并非遗忘，而是过早做出承诺。早期模糊的一轮对话坍缩为单一隐藏解释，后续澄清被这一承诺过滤，从而失效。因此需要重新定位交互式大模型的研究方向。

Method: 作者在写作、规划、编码三类受控对话任务上，使用 Gemini-2.5-Pro 与 Gemini-2.5-Flash 进行数千次试验。通过改变相同信息在对话中的出现顺序，并保证最终对话包含等量的任务相关信息，来检验顺序效应与澄清信号的处理方式。同时对比标准提示与记忆策略（如摘要、思维链）的效果。

Result: 相同信息以不同顺序出现会导致不同结果，即使最终对话包含等价的任务相关信息。这说明后续澄清被当作额外上下文，用于精炼陈旧的任务状态，而非作为纠正信号使其失效。编码任务尤其脆弱，早期假设会嵌入接口与控制流等结构化产物中。标准提示与记忆策略并不可靠：摘要可能抹平歧义，思维链虽能减少推理轨迹中的显式错误承诺，却不提升最终任务成功率。

Conclusion: 这些发现支持“保留不确定性的状态管理”。若助手无法放弃早期解释，鲁棒性就不能仅依赖事后纠正，而必须防止早期模糊对话硬化成单一任务状态。助手应在歧义未解时保留暂定假设，在高影响歧义持续存在时先询问再执行，并在后续证据推翻早期解读时基于修正后的状态重建。作者的目标不是提供一个提示技巧，而是把交互式大模型研究从“保留更多上下文”转向“保留不确定性”。

Abstract: Dialogue failures in language models are usually framed as memory failures: context too long, summaries lossy, a constraint forgotten. We argue this misses a deeper problem: in many conversations the model does not forget, it commits too early. An ambiguous early turn collapses into a single hidden interpretation, and later clarification is filtered through that commitment. We call this early posterior collapse: unresolved user intent collapsing into a committed task state before ambiguity is resolved. We study it with controlled dialogue tasks in writing, planning, and coding using Gemini-2.5-Pro and Gemini-2.5-Flash. Across thousands of trials, the same information in different orders yields different outcomes, even when the final dialogue contains equivalent task-relevant information. This order effect suggests later clarification is treated as extra context rather than a corrective signal: it refines a stale task state without invalidating it. Coding tasks are especially vulnerable, suggesting early assumptions get embedded in structured artifacts such as interfaces and control flow. Standard prompting and memory strategies do not reliably help: summaries can collapse ambiguity, and chain-of-thought can reduce explicit wrong commitment in reasoning traces without improving final task success. These findings motivate uncertainty-preserving state management. If assistants cannot let go of early interpretations, robustness cannot rely on post hoc correction alone; it must keep ambiguous early turns from hardening into one task state. Assistants should hold tentative hypotheses while ambiguity remains, ask before executing when high-impact ambiguity persists, and rebuild from a revised state when later evidence invalidates an earlier reading. Rather than one prompting fix, we aim to redirect research for interactive LLMs from retaining more context toward preserving uncertainty.

</details>


### [128] [From Decorative to Load-Bearing: Task Difficulty Shapes the Causal Role of Chain-of-Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25366)
*Renee Jia,Di Mu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出基于续写的因果测试方法，衡量思维链（CoT）对最终答案的"承重性"。结果显示：任务越难，CoT越承重（错误越容易被传播）；任务越易，模型越倾向于静默绕过自身推理。这一梯度给基于CoT的监督与安全监控带来结构性难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CoT监控只有在"写出的推理因果性地约束答案"时才有意义。已有工作多关注机制忠实性，却缺少对CoT是否真正"承重"的行为层面测量，因此需要一种能直接检验推理步骤对答案影响力的因果测试手段。

Method: 提出续写式因果测试（continuation-based causal testing）：一种消融-补丁干预，扰动某一推理步骤后截断链条，强制模型从被破坏的前缀继续生成，以此测量该步骤对最终答案的承重程度。在Gemma-2-9B-IT、Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct、DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B上，于GSM8K、MMLU、BIG-Bench Hard评测。另做匹配的2x2分析、28,584次续写的方差分解、四变体评判敏感度分析与n=500的双标注者盲评，并在隐状态上训练线性探针、尝试加性激活干预。

Result: CoT承重性随模型相对任务难度变化：易任务上模型静默绕过自身推理，难任务上则跟随被破坏的步骤并传播错误。2x2分析显示任务难度主导扰动类型：从GSM8K到BBH多步算术，错误传播上升16倍；方差分解中98.8%的可解释偏差归因于任务难度，仅0.8%归因于扰动类型。推理专用RL抑制错误传播并压缩该梯度。标签对评判提示不变，标注者间一致性完美（Cohen's kappa=1.00）。线性探针可区分静默绕过、自我纠错与错误传播，但加性激活引导的因果控制有限，最多只能翻转约25%的错误传播案例。

Conclusion: CoT承重性存在结构性梯度：推理链易读的地方信号很少，而真正关键的地方错误会在监控者介入前传播，这对基于CoT的监督与AI安全监控构成结构性挑战。行为模式可被读取但难以可靠控制。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) monitoring is only meaningful if written reasoning causally constrains the answer. We introduce continuation-based causal testing, an ablation-patch intervention that perturbs one reasoning step, truncates the chain, and forces the model to continue from the corrupted prefix. It measures how load-bearing a CoT is for the final answer, a behavioral notion distinct from mechanistic faithfulness. Across Gemma-2-9B-IT, Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, and DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B on GSM8K, MMLU, and BIG-Bench Hard, CoT load-bearingness tracks model-relative task difficulty: on easy tasks models silently bypass their own reasoning; on hard tasks they follow corrupted steps and propagate errors. A matched 2x2 analysis shows task difficulty dominates perturbation type: error propagation rises 16x from GSM8K to BBH multistep arithmetic, and a variance partition over 28,584 continuations attributes 98.8% of explained deviance to task difficulty versus 0.8% to perturbation type. Reasoning-specific RL suppresses error propagation and compresses the gradient. A four-variant judge-sensitivity analysis and blind two-annotator study (n=500) show the error-propagation vs. non-propagation label is invariant to judge prompt, with perfect inter-annotator agreement (Cohen's kappa = 1.00). This gradient creates a structural problem for CoT-based oversight and AI safety monitoring: where the trace is easy to read it carries little signal, and where it matters errors propagate before a monitor can intervene. Linear probes on hidden states separate silent bypass, self-correction, and error propagation, but additive activation steering provides limited causal control, flipping only about 25% of error-propagation cases at best. Behavioral mode is readable but not reliably controllable.

</details>


### [129] [Robust Failure, Conservative Repair: Textual Knowledge Distillation from Cross-Model Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25400)
*Andrew Ren,Haokun Liu,Chenhao Tan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究失败驱动的文本知识蒸馏，提出RFCR方法，通过跨模型共享失败提取规则、用边界案例收紧应用范围并在无有用规则时弃权，将基线cheat sheet准确率从68.50%提升至71.25%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通过模型任务错误提炼文本知识（cheat sheet）以辅助推理，但这种知识迁移不稳定：规则原子可能编码的是模型特定的推理补丁，会误导其他模型；同时规则可能被错误应用到无关案例，依据无关信息翻转答案。单模型失败的规则蒸馏在非源模型家族上甚至不如基线。

Method: 提出RFCR（Robust Failure, Conservative Repair）文本蒸馏流程：从多个模型共同出现的失败中提取规则，利用边界案例锐化规则的应用边界，并在未发现有用规则时选择弃权；该方法建立在将训练样本蒸馏为任务特定cheat sheet的流程之上，并通过规则原子注入模型文本输入。

Result: 在400条BIG-Bench Hard任务集上，RFCR将基线cheat sheet准确率从68.50%提升到71.25%（+2.75个百分点，95%置信区间[+1.25,+4.50]），且在原本正确的案例上没有性能下降。消融实验和跨模型诊断表明，准确率提升同时来自新知识注入和严格的规则应用控制。

Conclusion: 跨模型共享失败提取规则并结合边界约束与弃权机制，可稳定地提升文本知识蒸馏的效果，避免规则误用与模型特定补丁带来的负面迁移。

Abstract: Failure-based textual knowledge distillation aims to discover gaps in a model's knowledge by examining its task errors. The distilled knowledge can be useful for the reasoning of both this model ("source model") and other models. However, this transfer of knowledge may not be stable. We define a rule atom to be a standalone rule injected into a model's textual input at inference time. A rule atom can encode transferable task knowledge or model-specific reasoning patches that can confuse other models. Also, the injected rule atoms can be misapplied to unrelated cases, causing the model to incorrectly flip its answer based on irrelevant information. Building on a pipeline that distills training examples into task-specific cheat sheets that aid model reasoning, we examine when failure-derived rules can improve these cheat sheets. Our early experiment shows rule distillation from a single model's failures underperforms the baseline cheat sheet on non-source model families. This motivates Robust Failure, Conservative Repair (RFCR), a textual distillation procedure that derives rules from failures shared across models, sharpens their application boundaries using boundary cases, and abstains when no useful rule is found. On a 400-item BIG-Bench Hard task set, RFCR improves the baseline cheat sheets from 68.50% to 71.25% (+2.75 pp; 95% CI [+1.25,+4.50]) without performance degradation on previously correct cases. Ablations and cross-model diagnostics support that accuracy gains come from both new knowledge injection and strict rule-application control.

</details>


### [130] [Efficient Iterative Retrieval with Heterogeneous Batching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25405)
*Dohyun Park,Hubertus Franke,Daniel G. Waddington,Swaminathan Sundararaman,Yongjoo Park*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Orthrus是一个统一推理循环的服务系统，通过异构批处理同时执行嵌入与生成模型，提升吞吐量与GPU利用率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代信息检索越来越多地同时使用嵌入模型和生成模型处理复杂查询，但现有服务系统将两者隔离执行，导致吞吐量低、GPU利用率差。粗粒度划分（如将GPU专用于特定任务）无法适应动态负载，产生计算"气泡"。

Method: 提出Orthrus，在统一推理循环中进行异构批处理。核心挑战在于统一计算模式冲突的嵌入与生成负载，并优化批组成。方法包括：分块嵌入与增量池化（chunked embedding with incremental pooling），以及以负载感知方式调整批组成。

Result: 在四块A100 GPU上，相比基线部署，Orthrus在受控负载下实现1.28×–4.52×更高吞吐量，在迭代RAG基准上p99端到端延迟最多降低55.8%。

Conclusion: Orthrus通过统一推理循环中的异构批处理，有效解决了嵌入与生成模型隔离执行带来的低效问题，显著提升吞吐量并降低延迟。代码已开源。

Abstract: Modern information retrieval increasingly employs both embedding and generative models to handle complex queries. However, current serving systems suffer from low throughput and poor GPU utilization because they execute these models in isolation. Coarse-grained partitioning, such as dedicating GPUs to specific tasks, fails to adapt to dynamic workloads and creates computational "bubbles". To address these, we present Orthrus, a serving system that performs heterogeneous batching within a unified inference loop. The primary challenge lies in unifying embedding and generation workloads with conflicting computational patterns while optimizing batch composition for high performance. Orthrus addresses these challenges through chunked embedding with incremental pooling and by adjusting batch composition in a workload-aware manner. Evaluation on four A100 GPUs shows that, relative to baseline deployments, Orthrus achieves 1.28$\times$--4.52$\times$ higher throughput on controlled workloads and up to 55.8% lower end-to-end p99 latency on an iterative-RAG benchmark. We release our code at https://github.com/illinoisdata/Orthrus .

</details>


### [131] [From Offline Proxies to Online Decisions: A Layered Engagement Evaluation Framework for Conversational AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25408)
*Xuanyi Li,Vaskar Nath,Hossein Amirkhani,Jay Li,Alex Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究离线代理信号能否预测在线A/B实验结论，提出由三重对齐构成的构建与诊断清单及区间感知评估协议；在27个实验的489组对比中，冻结映射后的113组上复合信号达81.1% F1，支持在稀缺实验流量分配前优先筛选候选方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线A/B实验是用户参与度决策标准，但流量与读出时间限制了可测试的对话式AI改动数量。作者希望验证一种无需实验组用户曝光的离线信号，是否与在线实验结果一致，从而在稀缺实验流量分配前用于候选方案优先级排序。

Method: 提出可复用的构建与诊断清单，将离线代理视为三重对齐链条：行为标签到产品结果、学习分类器到候选助手行为、聚合离线信号到实验效应。配套评估协议通过区间感知决策一致性（比较离线与在线置信区间而非点估计）及实验内排序审计整体复合信号。具体实例包括固定评估套件、预测会话/提示级参与度的分类器，以及将样本级分差映射到在线模型级参与度增量的校准层。

Result: 在已部署多轮助手上的27个实验中报告489组离线-在线对比（候选臂对控制臂），覆盖模型检查点到系统提示调优。主要测试使用映射冻结后运行的8个实验的113组对比：复合信号F1达81.1%，而其原始分类器分数仅34.3%；复合信号无方向错误判断，原始分数则有31次。所有离线预测均在对应实验运行前计算，以避免过拟合。

Conclusion: 证据支持在稀缺实验流量分配前使用该复合信号优先筛选候选方案，在本部署中用于训练检查点选择与系统提示调优。

Abstract: Online A/B experiments are the decision standard for user engagement, but traffic and readout time limit how many conversational-AI changes can be tested. We ask whether an offline signal designed to be computable without treatment-arm user exposure agrees with the outcomes of those experiments. We contribute a reusable construction and diagnosis checklist that treats an offline proxy as a chain of three alignments: behavioral label to product outcome, learned classifier to candidate-assistant behavior, and aggregated offline signal to experiment effect. A companion evaluation protocol audits the whole composite by interval-aware decision agreement, which compares offline and online confidence intervals instead of point estimates, and by within-experiment ranking. The instantiation we evaluate comprises a fixed evaluation suite on which candidate behavior is scored, an engagement classifier trained to predict session/prompt level engagements, and a calibration layer mapping sample-level score differences to online model-level engagement deltas. We then report the audit: 489 paired offline-online contrasts (one candidate arm against its control) from 27 experiments on a deployed multi-turn assistant, spanning model checkpoints to system-prompt tuning. Our primary test uses the 113 contrasts from eight experiments that ran after the map was frozen: on these the composite reaches 81.1% F1, against 34.3% for the raw classifier score it is built on, and makes no wrong-direction calls where that raw score makes 31. Every offline prediction was computed before its experiment ran to prevent overfitting. The evidence supports using the composite to prioritize candidates before scarce experiment traffic is allocated---in our deployment of the experiment, selecting among training checkpoints and tuning system prompts.

</details>


### [132] [ZeroGate: Trust-Preserving Fast Paths for Governed AI Agent Runtimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25443)
*Zexun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ZeroGate 将精确动作审批与持久化本地准入分离，通过短时 ActionPass 与本地门控在准入点重建动作并原子消费 nonce，用显式再验证契约换取更短的调度边界，但不保证当前世界新鲜度与远程恰好一次效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提前授权可缩短智能体的调度边界，但若不取消授权工作，就可能允许载荷、权限或相关状态已发生变化的动作被放行；因此需要明确授权成本应在何处支付，并给出可审计的再验证边界。

Method: 发行人签署短时 ActionPass；可信运行时适配器在本地门控前重建最终动作，检查绑定并消费 nonce；SQLite 事务将 nonce 消费、适用配额更新与准入回执耦合。评估分为作者语义夹具、受控并发与崩溃实验，以及 Azure Blob 同步与预备执行的对比研究。

Result: 在 4800 次云尝试中，预备式 worker 从准入到调度 p95 为 9.802–11.374 ms，同步式为 25.018–334.000 ms（各并发级别下）；但预备式完整生命周期均值在每个级别都更长，边界改善并非净加速。实现本身既不建立当前世界新鲜度，也不保证远程恰好一次效果。

Conclusion: 贡献在于显式再验证契约、持久化参考边界与授权成本支付位置的可审计比较，而非新密码原语或普适性能前沿；成功本地准入在审批可靠、策略依赖完整且最新、观测忠实、消费原子等条件下，蕴含有指定的同步策略会在准入点授权同一动作。

Abstract: Moving authorization earlier can shorten an agent's dispatch boundary without removing authorization work. It can also admit an action whose payload, authority, or relevant state has changed. ZeroGate separates exact-action approval from durable local admission: an issuer signs a short-lived ActionPass, and a trusted runtime adapter reconstructs the final action before a local gate checks its binding and consumes its nonce. A SQLite transaction couples nonce consumption, applicable quota updates, and an admission receipt. We state a conditional decision-preservation proposition: successful local admission implies that a specified synchronous policy would authorize the same action at the admission point, provided approval is sound, all policy dependencies are represented and current, observations are faithful, and consumption is atomic. The implementation alone establishes neither current-world freshness nor exactly-once remote effects. Evaluation separates authored semantic fixtures, controlled concurrency and crash experiments, and an Azure Blob study comparing synchronous and prepared execution through the same issuer and gate. Both modes mint an exact-action pass; lifecycle latency includes preparation and prepared-batch dwell. Across 4800 cloud attempts, prepared worker-admission-to-dispatch p95 ranges from 9.802 to 11.374 ms, versus 25.018 to 334.000 ms synchronously, across the tested concurrency levels. Prepared mean complete lifecycle is longer at every level: the boundary improvement is not a net speedup. The contribution is an explicit revalidation contract, a durable reference boundary, and an auditable comparison of where authorization cost is paid, not a new cryptographic primitive or a universal performance frontier.

</details>


### [133] [Rollout Efficiency in Reinforcement Learning for Reasoning Large Language Models: A Taxonomy and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25463)
*Niloofar Gholipour,Marcos Assuncao,Gursimran Singh,Timothy Yu,Rajkumar Buyya,Julien Gascon-Samson,Zhenan Fan,Yong Zhang,Xiaojie Xu,Yaqiang Yao,Xiaolong Bai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本综述系统梳理了面向推理的强化学习中"轨迹生成（rollout）效率"研究，从机制与瓶颈两个视角建立分类体系，分析各类技术如何应对不同低效来源、组合机会与冲突，并指出效率评估报告的不足及未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向推理的强化学习让大模型能解决数学、编程等多步任务，但训练成本大幅转移到轨迹生成阶段（为策略更新生成轨迹）。因此需要在保持训练数据新鲜性、一致性和统计有效性的前提下，提升rollout效率、降低训练成本。目前相关研究分散，缺乏系统梳理。

Method: 这是一篇综述性研究。作者对近期rollout效率研究进行系统分类（taxonomy），从"机制（mechanism）"与"瓶颈（bottleneck）"两个维度对现有方法进行归类；并在此基础上分析不同技术族所针对的低效来源。

Result: 构建了rollout效率研究的系统分类体系；厘清了各类技术族与rollout低效来源之间的对应关系；识别了技术组合的潜在机会与冲突；发现了效率收益在评估与报告环节存在的缺口。

Conclusion: rollout效率是面向推理的强化学习中的关键成本瓶颈；本文通过分类学整合现有研究，为方法组合、评测标准化及后续研究提供了框架与方向指引。

Abstract: Reasoning-oriented reinforcement learning enables large language models to solve mathematical, coding, and other multi-step tasks, but shifts a substantial portion of the training cost to rollout, where trajectories are generated for policy updates. Efficient rollout mechanisms are therefore essential to reduce this cost while maintaining the freshness, consistency, and statistical validity of training data. This survey provides a systematic taxonomy of recent research on rollout efficiency for reasoning-oriented reinforcement learning, classifying existing approaches from both mechanism and bottleneck perspectives. Based on this taxonomy, we analyze how different technique families address distinct sources of rollout inefficiency, examine opportunities and potential conflicts for combining them, identify gaps in the evaluation and reporting of efficiency gains, and discuss open challenges and future research directions.

</details>


### [134] [Real-Time Hand Gesture Recognition for OpenXR Using Transformer-Based Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25466)
*Salar Rezayani,Russell Butler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究利用基于Transformer的机器学习模型，结合Unity中通过OpenXR标准采集的手部追踪数据，实现实时手势识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手势识别是人机交互的关键组成部分，可为游戏、虚拟现实、机器人等应用提供直观界面，因此需要更准确、鲁棒的实时识别方法。

Method: 使用Unity中OpenXR标准采集的手部追踪数据，提取手部关节位置数据与手腕旋转角度，训练基于Transformer的手势识别系统，利用其序列建模能力捕捉短时间窗口内的时序依赖。

Result: 手势分类准确率显著提升，且在不同手部朝向和手部尺寸下均能实现鲁棒分类。

Conclusion: Transformer模型可有效提升实时手势识别性能；未来工作将扩展至检测手势之间的过渡动作流。

Abstract: Hand gesture recognition is a key component in human-computer interaction (HCI), enabling intuitive interfaces for applications in gaming, virtual reality (VR), robotics, and more. This study integrates transformer-based machine-learning models for real-time hand gesture recognition, using hand-tracking data captured through the OpenXR standard in Unity. We leverage positional data of hand joints and wrist rotation angles to train a custom gesture recognition system. By utilizing the sequential modeling capabilities of transformers, the system captures temporal dependencies within short gesture windows and classifies gestures robustly across hand orientations and sizes. The results show a significant improvement in gesture classification accuracy. Building on this, we outline how the approach can be extended toward detecting the flow of movement, i.e., the transitions between gestures, as future work.

</details>


### [135] [ShowTellArena: Evaluating Business Workflow Understanding from Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25467)
*David Garg,Ritobrata Sarkar,Ehsan Azarnasab,Siddhartha Borah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了 ShowTellArena，一个用于评估智能体在观看叙述式业务演示后理解能力的基准协议与公开数据集，包含50个业务工作流任务和502道问题，并报告了218次试点尝试的探索性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 我们通常通过展示工作过程并解释决策来教导同事，但如何检验智能体是否从同样的教学中真正理解了内容，目前缺乏相应的评估手段。

Method: 构建 ShowTellArena 基准协议与公开数据集（v1.0含50个业务工作流任务，涵盖录像、截图、叙述、夹具种子及502道问题，任务横跨财务、招聘、采购、客户决策、库存和物流）；协议固定业务场景与测验，允许各产品通过自身教学界面捕捉课程；问题考查操作规则、边界、例外及所提自动化中的错误。

Result: 分析了39个工作流案例中的218次选定试点尝试，其中28个案例由全部三个被评估系统完成；探索性结果同时暴露了回答错误和未能完成教学体验的失败情况。

Conclusion: 贡献在于可审查的数据集与评估工作流，供他人扩展；该选定试点并非受控的产品排名，同时说明了发布版本的验证缺口及试点中覆盖不均、排除和评分来源问题。

Abstract: We often teach a colleague by showing the work and explaining the decisions as we go. How can we check what an agent understood from the same lesson? We introduce ShowTellArena, a benchmark protocol and public dataset for comprehension after narrated business demonstrations. The v1.0 release contains 50 business workflow tasks, with recordings, screenshots, narration, fixture seeds, and 502 questions. Tasks span finance, hiring, procurement, customer decisions, inventory, and logistics. The protocol holds the business scenario and quiz fixed while allowing each product to capture the lesson through its own teaching interface. Questions test operational rules, boundaries, exceptions, and errors in proposed automations. We analyze 218 selected pilot attempts across 39 workflow cases, including 28 cases attempted by all three evaluated systems. These exploratory results expose both answer errors and failures to complete the teaching experience. We describe the release's verification gaps and the pilot's uneven coverage, exclusions, and grading provenance. The contribution is an inspectable dataset and assessment workflow that others can extend; the selected pilot is not a controlled product ranking.

</details>


### [136] [RAG-NAROK: Retrieval-Aware Knowledge Corpus Poisoning in RAG with Source-specific Refutation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25469)
*Abdullahil Kafi,Alvi Ataur Khalil*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 RAG-NAROK 攻击框架，通过感知查询与检索上下文动态生成"锚定反驳"文档，显著优于静态投毒攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG 系统依赖动态检索管线来为 LLM 输出提供可验证的外部知识，但其结构性依赖带来一类尚未被充分探索的对抗脆弱性。现有知识库投毒攻击本质上是静态的：对抗文档被预先计算并注入，完全不知道受害系统针对特定查询实际会检索到什么内容，因此对生成器上下文窗口中围绕其载荷的竞争性文档环境"视而不见"。

Method: 提出 RAG-NAROK（Retrieval-Anchored Generation Negation And Response Quality Collapse）框架，一种能适应查询文本的 RAG 攻击方法。它利用 RAG 管线的透明性，先提取合法来源的身份信息，再生成"锚定特定来源的反驳文档"（Anchor-Specific Refutation documents），这些文档明确点名并贬低被检索到的来源，同时利用近因偏差与权威偏差，将文本生成引导向目标答案。

Result: 实验结果表明，RAG-NAROK 在多个不同领域上显著优于静态基线攻击方法。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了 RAG 透明性与 AI 安全性之间存在根本性张力：RAG 管线为提升可验证性而暴露的检索信息，恰恰成为可被利用的攻击面。

Abstract: Retrieval augmented generation (RAG) systems have emerged as the dominant architecture for grounding large language model (LLM) outputs in verifiable external knowledge, yet their structural reliance on a dynamic retrieval pipeline introduces a largely unexplored class of adversarial vulnerability. Existing knowledge-base poisoning attacks are fundamentally static. Adversarial documents are pre-computed and injected without any awareness of what the victim system will actually retrieve for a given query, leaving the attack blind to the competitive documentary landscape that surrounds its payload in the generator's context window. Unlike traditional static poisoning attacks that are blind to the retrieved context, we introduce RAG-NAROK (Retrieval-Anchored Generation Negation And Response Quality Collapse), a RAG attack framework that adapts to the query text. RAG-NAROK exploits the transparency inherent in RAG pipeline to first extract the legitimate source identities, then generate Anchor-Specific Refutation documents that explicitly name and devalue retrieved sources while leveraging recency and authority biases to steer the text generation toward a target answer. Our results demonstrate that RAG-NAROK significantly outperforms static baselines across diverse domains, revealing a fundamental tension between RAG transparency and AI security.

</details>


### [137] [Spectra: A Rules-Driven LLM Pipeline for Automated KYC Document Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25474)
*Miray Wahib,Ethan Tran,Rea Mourad,Mira Muti,Nikita Dvornik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对资本市场KYC开户流程耗时的问题，提出将传统四方流程重构为两方共享流程，并开发了AI辅助文档处理平台Spectra，结合规则引擎与LLM智能体实现分类、提取与合规验证，在真实文档上取得100%分类准确率与89.4%提取准确率，人工复核负担降低96%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 资本市场KYC开户依赖分析师人工分类文档、从异构来源提取结构化数据、并对照复杂监管政策验证合规，单客户耗时长，端到端流程常因串行交接拖延数周。作者希望识别其中可重复、适合AI自动化的环节以提升效率。

Method: 1）重构工作流：将传统四方流程整合为两方，共享大部分工作以便共同自动化，消除中间交接；2）提出Spectra平台：将结构化规则引擎与基于LLM的分类、提取、验证智能体结合；规则引擎将合规政策编码为可查询数据库，实现聚焦式上下文注入；3）将文档处理拆分为独立、可审计的阶段，各阶段单独优化并可追溯至具体政策条款，而非使用单一整体提示。

Result: 在真实KYC文档评测中，Spectra实现100%分类准确率和89.4%提取准确率，人工复核负担下降96%。

Conclusion: KYC开户流程包含可重复且适合自动化的组件；通过流程重构与规则引擎+LLM智能体的分阶段自动化设计，可显著提升处理准确率并大幅减少人工复核工作量。

Abstract: Know Your Client (KYC) onboarding in capital markets requires analysts to manually classify documents, extract structured data from heterogeneous sources, and validate compliance against complex regulatory policies. This process requires significant analyst time per client, with end-to-end onboarding often stretching to multiple weeks due to sequential handoffs. In this work, we analyze an on-boarding process and find that it comprises repeatable components well-suited to AI automation. We therefore propose a restructured workflow to be amenable to automation: we consolidate the traditional four-party process into two parties that share most of the work and can be automated together, eliminating intermediate handoffs that compound delays. To automate the remaining steps, we introduce Spectra, an AI-assisted document processing platform that combines a structured rules engine with LLM-based classification, extraction, and validation agents. The rules engine encodes compliance policy as a queryable database, enabling focused context injection that reduces token usage while improving extraction precision. Rather than a single monolithic prompt, the system decomposes document processing into isolated, auditable stages, each optimized independently and traceable to specific policy clauses. In evaluation on real KYC documents, Spectra achieves 100% classification accuracy and 89.4% extraction accuracy. Human review burden dropped by 96%.

</details>


### [138] [Queer inclusion in speech datasets: An audit and taxonomy of practical tensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25491)
*Brooklyn Sheppard,Anaelia Ovalle,Adina Williams,Levent Sagun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文审查了语音数据集中LGBTQIA+（酷儿）声音的纳入情况，通过审计六个数据集发现酷儿群体代表性极低（0–1.4%的说话者），并提出了一个张力分类体系，用以理解边缘化群体语音数据收集中的矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语音技术数据集缺乏边缘化群体（尤其是LGBTQIA+/酷儿群体）的声音，导致无法进行可靠的差异性测量。作者希望以该群体为案例，探讨从边缘化社区收集语音数据所面临的挑战与张力。

Method: 审计六个多样化的语音数据集，测量其中可量化的酷儿群体代表性；同时审计另外两个由酷儿社区“为其所建、与其共建”的语音科学数据集作为对比；并构建一个描述相关张力的分类体系（taxonomy）。

Result: 六个数据集中可测量的酷儿代表性很低（仅占说话者的0–1.4%），不足以进行稳健的差异性测量；AI与语音技术研究中常用的数据集收集惯例与参与式方法所强调的价值观存在冲突。

Conclusion: 语音数据集对酷儿群体的代表性严重不足，主流语音数据收集方法与参与式、以社区为中心的做法之间存在多重张力；作者提出分类体系以帮助理解和应对这些张力。

Abstract: In this paper, we examine speech datasets for their inclusion of LGBTQIA+, or queer, voices and provide a taxonomy of tensions to better understand why there is a lack of such voices in current speech technology datasets. Through an audit of six diverse speech datasets, we find that measurable queer representation is low (0-1.4% of speakers) - insufficient for robust disparity measurement. We take this community as a case study to consider what challenges and tensions are associated with collecting speech data from marginalized communities. For comparison, we audit an additional two datasets from the speech sciences that were created by, for, and with the queer community. We note that many customs in speech dataset collection efforts in AI and speech technology research may conflict with values emphasized in participatory approaches with marginalized communities, and provide a taxonomy describing these tensions.

</details>


### [139] [Towards participatory speech dataset curation: A queer case study and conceptual framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25496)
*Brooklyn Sheppard,Anaelia Ovalle,Adina Williams,Levent Sagun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过LGBTQIA+（酷儿）社区的案例研究，论证了建立参与式语音数据集创建框架的必要性，并提出了一个面向边缘化社区的参与式语音数据策管概念框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 酷儿社区对AI存在明确担忧并报告了相关伤害，例如试图开发可识别个人为酷儿的"gaydar"技术。常见语音数据收集实践可能不适合与酷儿说话者互动，因此需要一种参与式框架。

Method: 案例研究法。综述常见语音数据收集实践及其对酷儿说话者的不适切性，回顾参与式AI中酷儿社区参与的努力，以及针对其他边缘化社区语音数据收集的参与式实践；基于协同设计与知识共享的洞见，构建概念框架。

Result: 提出一个参与式语音数据策管的概念框架，包含四个相互重叠且双向的过程：界定社区、项目制定、参与模式、个人自主权。

Conclusion: 参与式语音数据策管框架可为、与及由边缘化社区共同开展，为语音数据集的创建提供更具伦理性和包容性的路径。

Abstract: In this paper, we motivate the need for a participatory speech dataset creation framework through a case study of the LGBTQIA+, or queer, community - a community with documented concerns about AI and reported harms, including attempts to develop 'gaydar' technologies that purportedly identify individuals as queer. We review common speech data collection practices, why these methods may be unsuitable for engaging with queer speakers, and discuss previous efforts in participatory AI with queer community engagement, as well as participatory endeavours specific to speech data collection for other marginalized communities. From this review, we develop a conceptual framework for participatory speech data curation by, for, and with marginalized communities drawing on insights from co-design and knowledge sharing. We propose a framework comprising overlapping and two-way processes of defining a community, project formulation, modes of participation, and personal autonomy.

</details>


### [140] [SMTB: Fast Structure-Mapping with Tight Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25508)
*Daniel Weitekamp,Christopher MacLellan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出SMTB结构映射算法，比SME快5-15倍，在大型嵌套领域映射准确率提升约50%，并作为CRE框架的一部分支持多种表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统结构映射引擎SME偏向树状谓词逻辑中的高阶对应关系，限制了其在任意关系图上的适用性，且速度较慢。

Method: 提出Structure-Mapping with Tight Bounds（SMTB）算法，最大化关系连通性而非偏向高阶关系；集成于多语言兼容、具Python接口的Cognitive Rule Engine（CRE）框架中。

Result: SMTB比SME快5-15倍，在大型嵌套领域中发现映射的效果约提升50%；在SME语料的5845个领域对上与SME v4进行了对比评估。

Conclusion: SMTB可适用于任意关系图和树状嵌套谓词逻辑领域，避免了SME在特权化高阶关系时可能出现的失败模式。

Abstract: Structure-mapping forms analogies by aligning systems of relationally connected elements based on shared structure instead of surface features. We introduce a new structure-mapping algorithm: Structure-Mapping with Tight Bounds (SMTB) that is 5--15x faster than the structure-mapping engine (SME) and about 50\% better at finding mappings in large nested domains. SMTB is part of the broader Cognitive Rule Engine (CRE) project, a flexible multi-language-compatible framework with an accessible Python interface to state-of-the-art C++ implementations of core algorithms commonly used in cognitive systems such as pattern matching, planning, and structure-mapping. CRE and SMTB are designed to work with a wide range of representation choices. Unlike SME, which biases higher-order correspondences in tree-like predicate logic, SMTB maximizes relational connectivity without privileging higher-order relations. This allows SMTB to work just as well over arbitrary relational graphs as it does in tree-like domains of nested predicate logic.
