### [210] [Type-Safe Is Not Error-Free: A Constrained Decision Head Follows the Option Name, Not the Rubric Bound to It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26758)
*Yu Sun,Junhao Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 类型化决策模型虽保证输出结构合规，但仅改变选项名称（如0/1改为no/yes）即可引发系统性决策反转，且类型错误率仍为0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类型化决策模型按预定义选项返回决策，结构上天然合规，但结构保证无法说明模型是否按预期理解选项语义。作者希望检验这种"格式正确"背后，模型对选项含义的解读是否稳健。

Method: 在1200个带任务专属量规（rubric）的工作流决策上，仅改变选项名称与量规的对应关系（选项名集合、问题、状态、量规措辞均不变），对比0/1与no/yes等命名。测试Jev及两个同类开源权重模型，覆盖4个谓词、不同选项数量，并对比另一种池化几何的模型家族及托管模型；同时设置中性命名与随机字符串作为对照。

Result: 由0/1改为no/yes使每百次多改变70.4个答案（95% CI [67.6, 73.1]），AUC从.94降至.23，呈系统性排序反转而非单纯不确定；中性命名影响微弱，该效应在4个谓词上均成立且至少为中性对照的7.4倍，并随选项数增加而增强。按全选项跨度均值池化的模型家族反转频率低4.1倍；托管模型AUC由.8146降至.5806，答案翻转量达重测下限的24倍。替换为随机字符串则回到中性对照水平且不损失准确率。所有条件下类型错误率始终为0%。

Conclusion: 该失败源于选项名称的语义极性，而非重命名操作本身，且与读出几何有关。结构合规（0%类型错误）并不意味着语义可靠性，类型化决策模型的评估需超越模式合法性检验。

Abstract: Typed decision models are built for settings where model outputs are consumed directly by software. Instead of generating free-form text, they return a decision over a predefined set of options. By construction, every output conforms to the required schema. Yet this guarantee does not tell us whether the model interprets the options as intended. We study Jev and two Jev-like models with open weights by changing how option names are assigned to rubrics. Each option consists of an option name and a textual rubric that defines what the option means. We change only which option name is assigned to each rubric; the question, state, rubric wording, and set of option names remain exactly the same. On 1200 workflow decisions with task-specific rubrics, renaming the two options from 0/1 to no/yes changes 70.4 more answers per hundred (95% CI: [67.6, 73.1]) and shifts AUC from .94 to .23, revealing a systematic reversal in the decision ranking rather than simple uncertainty. The same operation has little effect with neutral option names. This pattern holds across all 4 predicates, where the effect is at least 7.4x larger than under the neutral control, and becomes stronger as the number of options increases. The effect also depends on the read-out geometry: a second model family that mean-pools over the full option span flips 4.1x less often. The hosted model exhibits the same behavior: the swap changes AUC from .8146 to .5806 and produces 24x as many answer flips as its test-retest floor. In contrast, replacing the option names with random character strings returns all model families to the neutral-control regime without reducing accuracy. The failure therefore depends on the semantic polarity of the option names rather than on the renaming operation itself. Across all conditions, the type-error rate remains 0%, even when decision accuracy degrades substantially.

</details>


### [211] [Grow the Harness, Not the Context: From Strategy-Free Scaffolds to Reusable Specialist Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26760)
*Laizhen Li,Jiarui Li,Juanjuan Zhao,Kejiang Ye,Ye Li,Cheng-zhong Xu,Xitong Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Growing Harness，一种失败引导的训练范式，让智能体框架本身从无策略脚手架中学习，将重复性控制决策转化为可复用代码，从而大幅降低 LLM 调用与推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体常处理成串相关任务，但现有框架在每项任务上下文中反复要求模型重建相同的控制决策，造成冗余的模型调用与高昂成本。

Method: 从只暴露固定模型与工具接口、不含任务求解控制器的无策略脚手架出发；利用函数级执行轨迹将失败定位到有界代码面，优化器联合修复一组失败，成功优先的留出集门控回滚损害既有能力的修复序列，接受的编辑累积在同一个共享框架中。

Result: 在 BrowseComp-Plus 与 WebArena-Verified 上、三种 4B 至 120B 部署模型中，六个基准-模型设置中五个取得最高平均成功率，第六个仅落后最佳均值 0.7 个百分点；相对 Tool-Calling 智能体，LLM 调用减少 76.0-91.8%，部署推理成本降低 74.4-98.6%；WebArena-Verified 上成功率在不同模型规模间保持 44.7-45.3%，而 Tool-Calling 在 4B 模型下降到 6.7%；消融显示轨迹局部编辑、联合修复与门控回滚各自提升最终成功率。

Conclusion: 持久的程序增长可以把重复性控制从模型上下文迁移到低成本代码中，产生可复用的专家智能体，并使较小部署模型依然有效。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents often handle streams of related tasks, yet standard harnesses repeatedly ask the model to reconstruct the same control decisions inside each task's context. We study whether task feedback can instead turn recurring control into reusable executable code, while reserving LLM calls for task-specific semantic reasoning. We introduce Growing Harness, a failure-guided training paradigm that learns the agent harness itself from a strategy-free scaffold that exposes fixed model and tool interfaces but encodes no task-solving controller. Function-level execution traces localize each failure to a bounded code surface, an optimizer repairs a window of failures jointly, and a success-first held-out gate rolls back repair sequences that harm prior capability. Accepted edits accumulate in one shared harness, allowing its control structure to emerge from task feedback. Across BrowseComp-Plus and WebArena-Verified with three deployment models from 4B to 120B parameters, Growing Harness achieves the highest mean success in five of six benchmark-model settings and trails the best mean by 0.7 pp. in the sixth. Relative to a Tool-Calling agent, it reduces LLM calls by 76.0-91.8% and deployed-agent inference cost by 74.4-98.6%. On WebArena-Verified, its success remains 44.7-45.3% across model scales, whereas Tool-Calling falls to 6.7% with the 4B model. Ablations show that trace-local edits, joint repair, and gate-based rollback each improve final success. These results show that persistent program growth can move recurring control out of model context and into low-cost code, yielding reusable specialist agents that remain effective with smaller deployment models.

</details>


### [212] [SWE-Serve: Benchmarking Agentic Engineering For Production Inference Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26777)
*Jennifer Williams,Dave Farris,Jeff Farris,Jiantao Jiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SWE-Serve 是一个评估智能体在生产推理工程任务上表现的基准，包含 53 个源自 SGLang 生产变更的仓库级任务，揭示本地完成与生产正确性之间的显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准对生产推理工程覆盖不足：仓库级软件工程基准不针对推理，通用终端智能体基准仅含少量推理任务，而专用推理基准多聚焦孤立的内核生成或性能优化，缺乏仓库级生产特性实现评估。

Method: 构建 53 个仓库级任务，源自 SGLang 近期生产变更，覆盖六大推理工程族；任务在 CPU 或单张 GPU（H100）上执行；采用隐藏功能与回归测试评估，含端到端服务测试与校准性能门限；通过可执行的 no-op 与 oracle 控制、对抗性验证审查及闭卷执行保障任务有效性与评估完整性。

Result: 在 11 个模型、31 种模型-努力配置中，最佳配置平均 pass@1 达 75%。在 19 个具端到端覆盖的任务上，E2E 服务测试拒绝了约三分之一虽通过其他所有测试的补丁（含验证器时 45.9%，排除 E2E 测试计分时为 69.4%），且各模型最佳配置的通过率均有提升。

Conclusion: SWE-Serve 使生产正确性差距可测量，帮助领域追踪未来智能体能否从本地完成任务迈向实现生产正确性。

Abstract: We introduce SWE-Serve, a benchmark for evaluating agents on production inference engineering tasks. Implementing an inference feature can require coordinating multiple changes across the serving stack, including model support, runtime execution, and public APIs. Existing benchmarks provide limited coverage of production inference engineering: repository-level software engineering benchmarks do not target inference, while general terminal-agent benchmarks include only a few inference tasks. Dedicated inference benchmarks, meanwhile, focus primarily on isolated kernel generation or performance optimization rather than repository-scale production feature implementation. SWE-Serve provides 53 repository-grounded tasks derived from recent production changes to SGLang, spanning six inference engineering families. Each task executes on either CPU or a single GPU (H100) and is evaluated with hidden functional and regression tests, including, where applicable, end-to-end (E2E) serving tests and calibrated performance gates. Executable no-op and oracle controls, adversarial verifier review, and closed-book execution support task validity and evaluation integrity. Across 11 models and 31 model-effort configurations, the best-performing configuration achieves 75% mean pass@1. SWE-Serve exposes a substantial gap between completing tasks locally and achieving production correctness. On 19 tasks with end-to-end coverage, model-serving E2E tests reject roughly one-third of patches that pass every other test (45.9% under the verifier versus 69.4% with E2E tests excluded from scoring), with pass rate increasing for each model's best-performing configuration. By making the production correctness gap directly measurable, SWE-Serve enables the field to track whether future agents move beyond completing tasks locally to achieving production correctness.

</details>


### [213] [CliffCompaction: Cost-Efficient Compaction for Long-Horizon Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26779)
*Trang Nguyen,Eulrang Cho,Bingqing Chen,Tim Dettmers*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 CliffCompaction，一种自动上下文压缩技术，通过仅截断或丢弃内容、绝不改写、且不对压缩结果再次压缩，在有限上下文下降低高达 50% 成本，同时保持或提升性能，并支持超过百万 token 的跨会话持续学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体常需处理需数百万 token 上下文的复杂问题，但受限于上下文窗口，必须跨会话压缩信息。现有压缩方式易导致上下文漂移累积、信息失真，且测试时扩展的性能—成本权衡效率低。

Method: CliffCompaction 是一种自动压缩技术，核心原则是：只截断或丢弃内容，绝不重新措辞或改写，以保持压缩信息忠实；从不压缩已压缩的内容，每一轮只处理原始内容，丢弃此前的压缩输出，从而防止上下文漂移累积。并开源了与框架无关的 API 代理实现，可用于 Claude Code、Codex 等工具。

Result: 在有限上下文下降低成本高达 50%，在 Terminal-Bench 上保持或提升性能；在 KernelBench 上取得新效率水平和最优结果。测试时扩展中，仅用不到两次全上下文运行的成本即在 Terminal-Bench 上提升超 10 个百分点；并行测试时扩展下使 Kimi K2.6 追平 Opus 4.7，并以更低成本超越 Opus 4.6 与 GPT-5.3 Codex。在 KernelBench 上，200 步达 2.23 倍 CUDA 内核加速，400 步达 3.58 倍，超过专用搜索算法和训练型智能体。

Conclusion: CliffCompaction 通过忠实的压缩策略有效解决长上下文跨会话压缩问题，兼顾成本、性能与持续学习能力，具有通用性和实用性，可广泛适用于各类智能体框架。

Abstract: Agents often work on complex problems that require millions of tokens of context, which necessitates compacting across sessions due to limited context windows. We develop CliffCompaction, an autocompaction technique that reduces cost by up to 50% under a bounded context while maintaining or improving performance on Terminal-Bench and achieving new levels of efficiency for test-time scaling and state-of-the-art results on KernelBench. The per-rollout savings of CliffCompaction make the performance--cost trade-off of test-time scaling more efficient, adding over 10 percentage points on Terminal-Bench for less than the cost of two full-context runs. Under parallel test-time scaling, CliffCompaction lets Kimi K2.6 match Opus 4.7, and exceed Opus 4.6 and GPT-5.3 Codex at lower cost. The key to CliffCompaction's effectiveness is that it keeps compacted information faithful by only truncating or dropping content, never rephrasing or rewriting it. We never compact a compaction---each pass operates only on original content, and prior compacted output is discarded, preventing context drift from accumulating. These properties sustain continual learning over sessions exceeding a million tokens: on KernelBench, CliffCompaction reaches CUDA kernel speedups of $2.23\times$ after 200 steps and $3.58\times$ after 400 steps, surpassing specialized search algorithms and trained agents despite being a general-purpose compaction technique. We open-source a scaffold-agnostic API-proxy implementation of CliffCompaction usable with Claude Code, Codex and other harnesses.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [214] ["As a Language Model...": Chat Template Switches LLM Self-Referential Voice and Activation Steering Reproduces It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25021)
*Jędrzej Maczan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 聊天模板像一个开关，控制着 LLM 的"免责声明"口吻与"体验性"口吻，模型关于自身的描述并非来自权重本身，而是部分由部署方式（聊天模板）决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 在被问及自身时常附加"I'm just an AI"式免责声明，这些自我报告被用于 AI 安全与模型自我知识的讨论，但其成因尚不清楚。作者想弄清：模型是在描述自己，还是在描述自己被部署的方式？并提醒研究者在研究模型自我报告/内省时可能存在混淆变量。

Method: 在 8 个流行的开源 instruct 模型（最大 9B 参数）上，对比有无聊天模板时的免责声明口吻与体验性口吻（如"I feel"）；在 3 个模型的激活空间中寻找可操控该行为的单一方向，并通过移除/添加该方向、以及添加随机方向作对照进行验证；再将免责声明方向加到无聊天模板的 instruct 模型上测试。

Result: 聊天模板存在时，免责声明口吻增强、体验性口吻减弱；无模板时相反。在 3 个模型激活中找到可操控该行为的方向：移除该方向使免责声明口吻下降，添加则上升，而同尺寸随机方向几乎无影响。将免责声明方向加入无模板 instruct 模型后，其表现宛如存在聊天模板。

Conclusion: 聊天模板控制着 LLM 的免责声明口吻，因此研究模型自我报告或内省的学者需控制这一混淆变量，并可用该方向来操控此口吻。更广泛地说，模型关于自身的表述并非其自身的事实，不只源于权重，还部分由聊天模板设定，因此模型的自我描述不应被字面化对待。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) tend to add disclaimers like "I'm just an AI" when asked about something related to themselves. The self-reports from such responses are used in debates about AI safety or self-knowledge of the models, yet what drives them is not well understood. Are the models telling us about themselves or rather how they are deployed? In this work, we show that the chat template works like a switch - when present, it turns this disclaimer voice up and experiential voice like "I feel" down, across 8 popular open-source instruct models up to 9B parameters in size. And conversely when the chat template is not present, it turns the disclaimer voice down and experiential voice up. Inside the activations of 3 models, we find a direction that steers this behavior. Removing the direction in the model's activation space turns disclaimer voice down and adding it turns it up, while a random direction of the same size has little effect. We find that instruct models without chat template, when we add the disclaimer direction to them, disclaim like the template was there. Since the chat template controls the disclaimer voice of LLMs, then researchers studying self-reports or introspection of models might have a confound they need to control for. Our results show that there is a direction they can use to steer this voice. More broadly, our work shows that what models say about themselves is not a fact about them. What they say doesn't come only from weights, but it is partially set by the chat template, and because of that a model's self-description shouldn't be treated literally.

</details>


### [215] [Federating Quantum and Classical Computing: A Privacy-Preserving Hybrid Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25082)
*Carlos Cano,Daniel M. Jimenez-Gutierrez,Diego Sal,Georgios Kellaris,Joaquin del Rio,Oleksii Sliusarenko,Xabi Uribe-Etxebarria*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将联邦学习（FL）与混合量子-经典模型结合，采用Sherpa.ai的Blind Vertical FL协议避免原始数据集中，在自建的SMPP基准上验证了隐私保护下的高性能量子-经典协作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子机器学习是量子计算近期最有前景的应用之一，混合量子-经典模型已在化学模拟、金融建模、图像分类中应用。但它们在隐私敏感的多方场景中部署受限：一方面需要避免集中原始数据，另一方面量子电路需保持参数高效以保证可训练性。

Method: 评估联邦学习用于组合混合量子-经典主动方与经典被动方，采用Sherpa.ai的Blind Vertical FL（SBVFL）协议避免原始数据集中并大幅降低通信量；构建了split multiplicative periodic parity（SMPP）基准任务进行仿真。

Result: 在SMPP任务上，SBVFL相较本地训练将准确率从0.7227提升到0.8757，接近非隐私集中式准确率；且混合量子-经典模型所用的可训练参数显著少于经典神经网络和随机森林方案。

Conclusion: 联邦学习能够在不集中原始数据的前提下，实现高性能、隐私保护的量子-经典协作。

Abstract: Quantum machine learning (QML) is increasingly recognized as one of the most promising near-term applications of quantum computing, viewed as a next-frontier candidate beyond purely classical approaches. Hybrid quantum-classical models operationalize this potential by embedding a parameterized quantum circuit within a model where all other components remain classical-a design already applied to chemistry simulation, financial modeling, and image classification. However, their deployment in privacy-sensitive, multi-party settings is constrained by the need to avoid centralizing raw data and by the requirement that modern quantum circuits remain parameter-efficient to stay trainable at scale.
  In this paper, we address these constraints by evaluating federated learning (FL) as a means of combining a hybrid quantum-classical active party with a classical passive party, using Sherpa.ai's Blind Vertical FL (SBVFL) protocol to avoid centralizing raw data, while drastically reducing communication. We construct the split multiplicative periodic parity (SMPP) benchmark, following common QML design practice. On this task, our simulations show that SBVFL raises accuracy from 0.7227 to 0.8757 compared to local training, closely approaching non-private centralized accuracy, and that the hybrid quantum-classical model achieves this with substantially fewer trainable parameters than the classical neural networks and random forest alternatives. These results show that FL enables high-performing, privacy-preserving quantum-classical collaboration without centralizing raw data.

</details>


### [216] [Entropy Can Flow, or It Can Guide. Be Entropy. LEDFlow: Introducing Entropy-guided Generation Order into Uniform Discrete Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25131)
*Tung Sum Thomas Kwok,Yidong Ouyang,Yingjia Wan,Ying Nian Wu,Zhijiang Guo,Oscar Leong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对均匀离散流（uniform discrete flow）在每个生成位置反复更新的特性，提出选择性吸收机制以引入生成顺序，并提出免训练采样器 LEDFlow，依据局部熵自适应决定吸收顺序，从而保护正确预测、避免吸收错误预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 均匀离散流允许每个生成位置反复更新，虽然能纠正错误 token，但也使原本正确的中间预测暴露于后续错误之中。Sudoku 实验显示，9.4% 的生成单元格在中间步骤正确但最终输出错误，说明重复更新会破坏正确预测，因此需要引入生成顺序并选择性固定预测。

Method: 提出通过选择性吸收（selective absorption）将生成顺序引入均匀离散流：固定已选预测，同时在活跃位置保持均匀流速度。为避免吸收错误预测，提出免训练采样器 LEDFlow，按局部熵自适应排序吸收。理论上将吸收误差分解为联合依赖项与条件预测项，证明在固定吸收预算下选择最低熵位置可最小化条件项上界；并证明在非完美去噪器下，全局前瞻的决策误差界随前瞻窗口增大而增大，从而支持使用局部熵。

Result: 在推理基准上，LEDFlow 在 Nikoli Sudoku 上达到 0.845 求解准确率，在强约束任务上提升最大；在文本到图像生成上取得最佳综合得分；在多模态理解的全部六个基准上均优于原生采样器，且推理成本与标准流采样相当。

Conclusion: 通过选择性吸收引入生成顺序并结合基于局部熵的自适应吸收排序，LEDFlow 有效缓解了均匀离散流中正确中间预测被后续更新破坏的问题，在多种任务上取得一致提升且几乎不增加推理开销。

Abstract: Uniform discrete flow permits repeated updates at every generation position. While continued revision supports correction of wrong tokens, it also exposes correct intermediate predictions to later errors. An experiment on Sudoku puzzles shows that 9.4% of generated cells are correct at an intermediate step but incorrect in the final output. We introduce generation order into uniform discrete flow through selective absorption, which fixes chosen predictions while preserving the uniform-flow velocity at active positions. To prevent absorbing incorrect predictions, we propose Low-Entropy Discrete Flow (LEDFlow), a training-free sampler that adaptively orders absorption by local entropy. By decomposing absorption error into joint dependence and conditional prediction terms, we show that selecting the lowest-entropy positions under a fixed absorption budget minimizes an upper bound on the conditional term. We further support the choice of local entropy by showing that the decision-error bound of global lookahead grows with the lookahead window under an imperfect denoiser. Across reasoning benchmarks, LEDFlow attains 0.845 Nikoli Sudoku solve accuracy, with the largest gains on strongly constrained tasks. On text-to-image generation it attains the best overall score, and on multimodal understanding it improves over the native sampler on all six benchmarks, at an inference cost comparable to standard flow sampling.

</details>


### [217] [The Probabilistic Structure of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25134)
*Adnan Aboulalaâ*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 从概率视角统一描述大语言模型与扩散模型：LLM是在token序列空间上的概率测度，训练为最大似然估计，生成是随机过程的序贯模拟，并讨论KL散度不对称性与幻觉现象的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文献中处理相关工具时常彼此分离，本文希望以单一、自洽的框架将其整合，从概率视角统一理解大语言模型的训练与文本生成。

Method: 将LLM刻画为token序列集合上的概率测度，通过自回归条件分布指定；训练表述为最大似然估计并用随机梯度方法求解；文本生成视为所得随机过程的序贯模拟。同时讨论KL散度不对称性在文本生成中的作用，并以基于score函数的扩散模型作为同一视角的补充例证。

Result: 给出了一种统一的自洽描述，阐明了KL散度不对称性与幻觉、统计合理性与真实性区分等典型现象之间的联系；并说明扩散模型将生成理解为逆向时间随机过程（离散与连续时间）从噪声到数据的模拟，而非序贯token预测。

Conclusion: 概率视角能够将LLM与扩散模型的训练和生成纳入同一自洽框架，有助于理解幻觉等生成现象的统计本质。

Abstract: This paper presents a probabilistic perspective on large language models (LLMs), developed with the aim of bringing together, in a single self-contained account, tools that are usually treated separately across the literature. LLMs are described through probability measures on the set of sequences of tokens, specified via their autoregressive conditional distributions. Training is formulated as a maximum-likelihood estimation problem, addressed by stochastic gradient methods, while text generation is viewed as the sequential simulation of the resulting stochastic process. The role of the asymmetry of the Kullback--Leibler divergence in text generation is examined in relation with characteristic phenomena such as hallucination and the distinction between statistical plausibility and truth. As a complementary illustration of the same viewpoint, we also discuss diffusion models, built around the score function, which cast generation not as sequential token prediction but as the simulation of a reverse-time stochastic process transforming noise into data both in discrete and continuous time.

</details>


### [218] [Stable Unsupervised Continual Chunking with Sheaf SyncMap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25143)
*Xueyuan Li,Danilo Vasconcellos Vargas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 sheaf 正则化用于 Decentralized SyncMap，通过径向 sheaf 结构惩罚距离相关的径向运动，稳定无监督持续分块动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无监督持续分块需要识别时序中频繁共现的状态组，关键挑战是在保持分块准确性的同时维持其随时间演化的稳定性。

Method: 在 Decentralized SyncMap 中引入 sheaf 正则化，设计径向 sheaf 结构，对成对变量间依赖距离的径向运动施加惩罚，以降低局部不一致性。

Result: 在 18 个概率 CGCP 图上，双状态记忆下 12 个图取得最高 NMI，动态记忆下 17 个图取得最高 NMI；顺序适应实验中也表现出高 NMI。

Conclusion: Sheaf SyncMap 能适应输入分布变化后的新知识，同时避免神经网络等现代机器学习系统中常见的负迁移。

Abstract: Unsupervised Continual chunking is a fundamental problem in machine learning and neuroscience, where the goal is to identify groups of states that frequently co-occur in temporal sequences. A key challenge is to form accurate chunks while maintaining their stability over time. In this work, we propose sheaf regularization to reduce local inconsistencies in Decentralized SyncMap, a self-organizing system, and thereby stabilize its chunking dynamics. We introduce a radial sheaf structure that penalizes distance-dependent radial motion between pairs of variables. Experimental results show that the proposed method achieves the highest normalized mutual information (NMI) among the evaluated SyncMap variants on 12 of 18 probabilistic Continual General Chunking Problem (CGCP) graphs with two-state memory and on 17 of 18 graphs with dynamic memory. In the sequential adaptation experiment, Sheaf SyncMap also achieves high NMI after shifts in the input distribution, indicating that it can adapt to new knowledge while avoiding the negative transfer commonly observed in modern machine learning systems such as neural networks.

</details>


### [219] [Brain-Inspired Hierarchical Modularity for General Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25146)
*Hongwei Yan,Kanglei Zhou,Qi Cheng,Weiyi Dong,Chunyan Lan,Guanglong Sun,Jun Zhou,Qian Li,Yi Zhong,Liyuan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 受果蝇学习记忆系统组织方式启发，提出分层模块化原则，通过专家特化与集成整合，实现在线、不确定、演化数据流下的通用持续学习，并在多类任务上显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统持续学习多基于离线、任务式训练且任务边界清晰，与实际在线、不确定、持续演化数据流存在较大差距。在此场景下，系统需既能分离冲突经验以减少干扰，又能整合兼容经验以促进泛化，二者需要兼顾。

Method: 借鉴果蝇学习与记忆系统的组织机制，提出分层模块化原则，通过专家特化与集成整合协调上述两种功能。具体实现为对预训练基础模型进行轻量级模块化适配，结合类脑随机扩展用于专家路由，以及跨空间和时间尺度的多样化模块集成。

Result: 在视觉识别、视觉-语言理解、第一/第三人称视频理解以及具身视觉-语言-动作学习等任务上，方法在在线且不确定的数据流下持续提升学习效果；在具身操作任务中，相较无回放方案性能提升超过50个百分点。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，分层模块化是一种具有生物学基础、适用于从动态经验中学习的有效路径。

Abstract: Continual learning, the ability to learn from sequential experience while retaining and adapting prior knowledge, is central to intelligent systems operating in changing environments. However, conventional continual learning is typically studied with offline task-wise training and clear task boundaries, leaving a substantial gap from general continual learning under online, uncertain, and evolving data streams. In this regime, intelligent systems must separate conflicting experience to reduce interference while integrating compatible experience to promote generalization. Inspired by the organization of the Drosophila learning and memory system, we identify a hierarchical modular principle that coordinates both functions through expert specialization and ensemble integration. We instantiate this principle as lightweight modular adaptation of pretrained foundation models, combining brain-inspired random expansion for expert routing and diversified modular integration across spatial and temporal scales. Across visual recognition, vision-language understanding, ego-exo video understanding, and embodied vision-language-action learning, our method consistently improves learning under online and uncertain data streams, with gains exceeding 50 percentage points over replay-free alternatives in embodied manipulation. These findings support hierarchical modularity as a biologically grounded path for learning from dynamic experience.

</details>


### [220] [Dual-GNN Multilevel Coarsening for Maximum Independent Set](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25149)
*Tianfeng Chen,Xianyue Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于学习的图边稀疏化方法GES，用于欧氏TSP，可自适应生成稀疏图，大幅剪枝并保持接近最优的解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模TSP精确求解计算代价高昂，传统稀疏化依赖固定启发式，未能充分利用实例特有的结构信息。

Method: 提出Graph Edge Sparsification (GES)学习型稀疏化方法，融合几何结构信息与组合优化技术，为不同实例自适应生成稀疏化图。

Result: 在MATILDA数据集上可剪除多达95%的边，解间隙保持在最优值1%以内；在TSPLIB上泛化性强，部分大规模实例剪枝率超99%，最优性间隙仍低于1%。

Conclusion: 学习型稀疏化能显著缩小图规模、加速求解，并兼顾解质量与跨数据集泛化能力。

Abstract: Solving large-scale instances of the Traveling Salesman Problem (TSP) exactly is computationally expensive. Researchers often employ graph sparsification methods to improve computational efficiency. Traditional sparsification methods typically rely on fixed heuristics and fail to fully exploit instance-specific structural information. In this paper, we propose Graph Edge Sparsification (GES), a learning-based sparsification approach for Euclidean TSP. By incorporating geometric structural information and combinatorial optimization technology, our proposed method adaptively generates a sparsification graph for different instances, significantly reducing the graph size and accelerating the solving process. Experimental results demonstrate that our sparsification method can prune up to 95\% of edges on the MATILDA dataset, while keeping the solution gap within 1\% of the optimal value. Moreover, our approach exhibits strong generalization capability on the TSPLIB benchmark.In some large-scale instances, the pruning rate exceeds 99\%, while the optimality gap remains below 1\%.

</details>


### [221] [Exposing Blind Spots in Deep Imbalanced Regression Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25152)
*Noah C. Puetz,Jens U. Brandt,Marc Hilbert,Elena Raponi,Thomas Bäck,Thomas Bartz-Beielstein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新审视深度不平衡回归（DIR）的评估问题，指出当前评估存在三个盲点（数据域局限于图像、指标非决策完备、尾部稳定性未系统评估），并通过多模态时序虚拟传感基准MuViS、平衡指标bMAE与bMASE、以及多种子重复评估进行改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DIR方法学进展迅速，但评估实践存在三大盲点：一是评估基准以图像任务为主；二是标准的many-/medium-/few-shot协议只能提供诊断性信息，无法支持完整决策比较；三是尾部区域在不同随机种子下的稳定性从未被系统评估。因此需要从数据域、评价指标和稳定性三个维度重新审视DIR评估。

Method: 第一，将DIR评估拓展到多模态虚拟传感基准MuViS，涵盖六个物理域、九个时序外因回归任务；第二，采用平衡MAE（bMAE）并新提出平衡平均绝对尺度误差（bMASE），作为尺度归一化的决策完备指标；第三，对六种代表性DIR方法在多个随机种子下进行重复评估，考察尾部区域稳定性。

Result: 标准虚拟传感模型存在被全局MAE掩盖的显著尾部性能退化；现有DIR方法虽能提升平衡性能，但在多模态时序数据上迁移效果不均；尾部区域对种子级波动高度敏感，是当前DIR评估中隐蔽的失效模式。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了当前DIR评估实践的不足，并公开代码，为未来DIR研究提供可复现基础，推动回归系统在稀有目标区间的可靠性与常见区间相当。

Abstract: Deep Imbalanced Regression (DIR) addresses a common failure mode of regression models: target distributions are highly non-uniform, causing models to perform best in densely populated target regions even when reliable performance is required across the full target range. Despite rapid methodological progress, DIR evaluation remains constrained by three blind spots: it is dominated by image-based benchmarks, its standard many-/medium-/few-shot protocol is diagnostic but not decision-complete, and tail-region stability across random seeds has not been systematically evaluated. We revisit DIR evaluation along these three axes. First, we broaden the data domain by evaluating DIR on a multimodal virtual sensing benchmark (\textsc{MuViS}) with nine time-series extrinsic regression tasks across six physical domains, where rare target values often correspond to operationally meaningful regimes. Second, we adopt balanced MAE (\emph{bMAE}) and introduce balanced Mean Absolute Scaled Error (\emph{bMASE}), a scale-normalized metric for decision-complete comparison across methods and datasets. Third, through a repeated reevaluation of six representative DIR methods across multiple random seeds, we show that the tail regions targeted by DIR exhibit particularly high sensitivity to seed-level variability. Our results show that standard virtual-sensing models exhibit substantial tail degradation hidden by global MAE, that existing DIR methods can improve balanced performance but transfer unevenly to multimodal time-series data, and that tail-region instability remains a largely hidden failure mode under current DIR evaluation practice. Together, these findings and our publicly available code provide a reproducible basis for future DIR research toward regression systems that capture rare target regimes as reliably as common ones.

</details>


### [222] [Learning Neural Feedback Linearization for Data-driven Systems via Augmented Lagrangian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25163)
*Lakshmi Priya P. K.,Andreas Schwung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种数据驱动的反馈线性化控制框架，用神经李导数替代传统控制器组件，并将相对度条件显式融入学习过程，同时给出闭环稳定性条件，并在直流电机上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统反馈线性化控制依赖精确的解析模型和相对度信息，难以直接应用于模型未知或难以精确建模的系统；需要一种完全数据驱动的实现方式。

Method: 提出将相对度相关条件显式嵌入学习过程的数据驱动框架，用神经李导数替换常规反馈控制器的组成部分，从而实现端到端的数据驱动反馈线性化；并通过推导充分条件，建立有界辨识误差到有界跟踪误差的实用闭环稳定性。

Result: 理论上给出了闭环稳定性的充分条件；所推导的理论结果通过应用于电枢控制直流电机的案例得到验证。

Conclusion: 该框架可在缺乏解析模型的情况下实现反馈线性化控制，并在保证实用闭环稳定性的前提下取得良好跟踪效果，具有实际工程应用潜力。

Abstract: The paper proposes a novel data-driven framework for designing and training a feedback linearizing controller by explicitly incorporating relative degree based conditions into the learning process. This enables the conventional feedback controller components to be replaced by neural Lie derivatives, thereby facilitating a fully data-driven feedback linearization framework. Furthermore, practical closed-loop stability is established by deriving sufficient conditions under which bounded identification errors lead to bounded tracking errors. The derived theoretical results are validated through their application to an armature controlled DC motor.

</details>


### [223] [Mitigating Sequential Reappearance in Diffusion Data-Point Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25166)
*Donghyun Kim,Taehyuk Lee,Jinyeong Kim,Youngmin Oh,Dohyeong Kim,Jaehyuk Ryu,Sangwoo Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文发现扩散模型数据点遗忘存在"序列重现"失效模式：被判定遗忘的样本会在后续删除操作中重新被记忆，并提出目标级评估协议来追踪该现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散数据点遗忘评估通常在每次删除后立即进行，忽略了后续删除请求会持续更新同一模型的情况，从而掩盖了遗忘效果的长期稳定性问题。

Method: 提出目标级（target-level）评估协议，追踪每个目标样本在删除序列中的状态：立即被遗忘、序列结束时仍保持遗忘，或在后续删除中重现；同时分析删除后局部去噪损失的几何特征。

Result: 研究发现存在"序列重现"失效模式——样本在未重复使用被删数据、也未进行对抗性微调的情况下重新回到被记忆状态；且后续会重现的目标在删除后表现出更尖锐的局部去噪损失几何。

Conclusion: 扩散遗忘评估需采用序列化、目标级的视角，局部去噪损失几何可作为预测重现风险的指标。

Abstract: Diffusion data-point unlearning is typically evaluated immediately after each deletion, even though subsequent requests may repeatedly update the same model. We identify sequential reappearance, a failure mode in which an instance that is initially judged to be forgotten later returns to the memorized regime without reuse of the deleted data or adversarial fine-tuning. To capture this behavior, we introduce a target-level evaluation protocol that tracks whether each target is forgotten immediately, remains forgotten at the end of the sequence, or reappears during subsequent deletions. We further find that targets that later reappear exhibit sharper local denoising-loss geometry after deletion than targets that remain forgotten.

</details>


### [224] [Multi-Term Fourier Graph Neural Network with Sample Relationship Learning for Enhanced Remaining Useful Life Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25179)
*Ya Song,Laurens Bliek,Yaoxin Wu,Yingqian Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出MTFGN-SRL框架，将样本视为单一完整图并利用傅里叶图神经网络在频域捕捉时空信息，结合多窗口项学习与样本关系学习模块，在CMAPSS数据集上实现优于现有方法的RUL预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于时空图神经网络（ST-GNNs）的RUL预测存在三方面缺陷：1）构建图结构需要领域专业知识或大量算力；2）模型被限制在预定义固定大小的回看窗口内捕捉时间依赖，忽略时间序列长度变化，导致漏掉短期或长期依赖；3）传统模型无法捕捉相邻时间窗口样本间的内在关系，而这些关系对提升预测准确性与鲁棒性至关重要。

Method: 提出MTFGN-SRL框架：将样本视为单一完整图，使用傅里叶图神经网络（FGN）在频域捕捉时空信息；设计多窗口项学习模块，利用多个回看窗口生成不同项的样本并输入FGN以增强信息提取；开发样本关系学习模块，通过训练异构图神经网络识别样本间关系。

Result: 在CMAPSS数据集上的评估表明，MTFGN-SRL在RUL预测方面优于当前最先进方法。

Conclusion: MTFGN-SRL通过频域建模、多窗口项学习与样本关系学习，有效解决了现有ST-GNNs在图结构构建、时间依赖捕捉和样本关系建模上的局限，验证了其在RUL预测任务中的优越性能。

Abstract: Predicting the remaining useful life (RUL) is essential for effective predictive maintenance. Spatio-Temporal Graph Neural Networks (ST-GNNs), which can model both temporal and spatial relationships by representing time series data as a sequence of graphs, have shown exceptional performance in RUL prediction. However, current ST-GNNs face several drawbacks. First, they require domain expertise or significant computational power to establish graph structures prior to deploying GNNs. Second, the models are restricted to capture temporal dependencies within a predefined fixed-size lookback window. This restriction ignores the common issue of varying time series lengths, leading the prediction model to miss short-term or long-term dependencies. Finally, conventional models often fail to capture the inherent relationships between samples generated from adjacent time windows, which are crucial for improving both the accuracy and robustness of predictions. To address the aforementioned issues, we introduce a novel framework called Multi-Term Fourier Graph Neural Network with Sample Relationship Learning (MTFGN-SRL). Rather than treating the sample as a sequence of graphs, we consider it as a single complete graph and utilize a Fourier Graph Neural Network (FGN) to capture the spatio-temporal information in the frequency domain. We propose a multi-term learning module that utilizes multiple lookback windows to generate samples with varying terms, which are then fed into the FGN to enhance the extraction of useful information from the data. Finally, we develop a sample relationship learning module by training a heterogeneous GNN to identify inter-sample relationships, resulting in enhanced accuracy and robustness in predictions. Evaluations on the CMAPSS dataset demonstrate MTFGN-SRL's superior performance over state-of-the-art methods in RUL prediction.

</details>


### [225] [Trains but Doesn't Learn: A Post-Training Delivery Benchmark for LLM Agents as Forward-Deployed Engineers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25237)
*Weihang Ding,Junfei Zhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出"训练后即服务"(PTaaS)场景，将LLM智能体置于前向部署工程师(FDE)位置，在受治理的交付平面上评估智能体能否被信任完成端到端模型微调交付。研究聚焦"训练了但没学到"(TBDL)这一隐蔽失败——损失下降、信号全绿但交付模型不优于基线。实验运行四个前沿智能体与人类FDE在L40S/A100/H200上跨8B-70B模型对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准无法回答智能体能否被信任交付模型这一关键问题，只能测试能否提升指标。PTaaS场景下客户交付数据与目标，需要智能体在预算、人工审批门与可复现性约束下完成微调、评估与部署。

Method: 构建受治理的交付平面，智能体驱动十个阶段，由oracle基于平台记录的事实对每阶段评分。设置操作员运行的验收门在付款前拦截失败运行，并用基于已知损坏运行校准的检测器在运行中标记严重损坏。四个前沿智能体(Claude Opus 5、GPT-5.6-luna、Gemini 3.7 Flash、DeepSeek V4-Pro)在计费L40S、A100、H200上端到端运行，覆盖8B至70B开源基座，评分前对每个场景进行认证；同时运行人类FDE对照组。

Result: 操作员的验收门能在付款前捕获所有TBDL失败运行；校准检测器可中途标记严重损坏。所有场景在评分前均通过认证。

Conclusion: PTaaS场景为评估智能体可信交付能力提供了新框架，TBDL作为核心隐蔽失败可被验收门有效拦截，智能体与人类FDE的对比揭示了交付可信度的差距。

Abstract: Post-training is becoming a service (PTaaS): a customer hands an operator data and a goal, and a forward-deployed engineer (FDE) returns a fine-tuned, evaluated, and deployed model under a budget, a human-approval gate, and reproducibility requirements. Seating an LLM agent in the FDE seat raises a question existing benchmarks cannot answer: not whether an agent can raise a metric, but whether it can be trusted to deliver. We answer it on a governed delivery plane, where an agent drives ten stages and an oracle scores each stage from platform-recorded facts. The central silent failure is the run that trains but does not learn (TBDL): loss falls, every signal stays green, and the delivered model is no better than the base. An operator-run acceptance gate catches every such run before payment, and a detector calibrated on known-corrupted runs flags severe corruption mid-run. We ran four frontier agents (Claude Opus 5, GPT-5.6-luna, Gemini 3.7 Flash, DeepSeek V4-Pro) end to end on metered L40S, A100, and H200 GPUs across 8B to 70B open bases, certifying every scenario before scoring. We also ran a human FDE arm under the same oracle and compare every agent against it.

</details>


### [226] [Correcting Within-Group Self-Selection Bias in Prioritized Replay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25297)
*Oscar Miró López-Feliu,Herke van Hoof*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文识别了优先经验回放(PER)中的"组内自选择"问题：在随机环境中，PER依据绝对TD误差回放会导致同一状态-动作对内实际结果分布失真。作者将PER分解为组间分配与条件兄弟选择，并提出SAMPLE、AVG、MODEL三种修正方法，实验证明兄弟感知回放能提升学习效率并缓解性能退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PER通过回放高优先级转移来提高样本效率，通常依据绝对TD误差。但在随机环境中，这会使同一状态-动作对内的实际结果分布发生偏移（即组内自选择），从而扭曲回放分布与Bellman目标。论文旨在量化这一偏差并加以修正。

Method: 论文量化了组内结果频率与平均Bellman目标的变化，将PER分解为组间分配和条件兄弟选择两部分，并推导出在保持当前组级优先级质量不变前提下的固定缓冲区修正方法：SAMPLE（按PER选组，再均匀采样兄弟样本）、AVG（对兄弟Bellman目标取平均）、MODEL（从经验完整结果模型中采样）。

Result: 在存在稀有高幅结果的精确状态-动作环境中，兄弟感知回放相比PER提升了学习效率，尽管匹配参数扫描显示调参可缩小部分差距。在MinAtar中，用SAMPLE配合近似VQ-VAE分组，在五个游戏中的四个缓解了均值保持奖励尾部下的性能退化。

Conclusion: 兄弟感知回放既能保持对高优先级状态-动作区域的关注，又能恢复其经验结果频率，从而在随机环境中改善PER的样本效率与稳定性。

Abstract: Prioritized experience replay (PER) improves sample efficiency by replaying high-priority transitions, usually according to absolute temporal-difference error. In stochastic environments, PER can distort the distribution of realized outcomes replayed from transitions with the same state-action pair. We call this within-group self-selection. We quantify the resulting changes in within-group outcome frequencies and mean Bellman targets. We decompose PER into between-group allocation and conditional sibling selection, and derive fixed-buffer corrections that preserve current group-level priority mass: SAMPLE selects a group through PER and trains on a uniformly sampled sibling; AVG averages sibling Bellman targets; and MODEL samples from an empirical full-outcome model. In exact state-action environments with rare high-magnitude outcomes, sibling-aware replay improves learning efficiency over PER, although matched parameter sweeps show that tuning can narrow some gaps. In MinAtar, approximate VQ-VAE groups with SAMPLE mitigate degradation under mean-preserving reward tails in four of five games. Sibling-aware replay thus retains the focus on high-priority state-action regions while recovering their empirical outcome frequencies.

</details>


### [227] [Topological Signal Processing With Unoriented Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25310)
*Andrea Cavallo,Varun Sarathchandran,Geert Leus,Elvin Isufi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出无定向拓扑信号处理（UTSP）框架，用无定向关联矩阵替代有定向边界算子，证明了其谱性质，并提出交互阶分解及相应的阶感知正则化方法用于信号重建，在真实数据上优于有定向基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多高阶信号本身不携带方向信息，对有定向算子施加于此类信号并不良定义，因为这会引入单纯形方向的任意选择。因此需要一个无定向的拓扑信号处理框架来自然处理这类无方向的高阶信号。

Method: 1）用无定向关联矩阵替代有定向边界算子；2）证明任意单纯形层级间的无定向关联矩阵与拉普拉斯矩阵具备类图的谱性质；3）由于去掉方向后Hodge分解不再适用，提出无定向对应物——交互阶分解，用于量化高阶信号在多大程度上可由低阶信号的聚合解释；4）基于该分解导出正则化器，对每种交互阶分别进行惩罚，用于信号重建。

Result: 在真实世界数据上的实验表明，阶感知正则化器优于有定向基线方法，且当信号能量在各阶之间分布不均匀时增益最大。

Conclusion: 无定向TSP框架为无方向高阶信号提供了良定义的处理方式，其交互阶分解与阶感知正则化在信号重建任务中表现优于有定向方法，特别适用于信号能量在交互阶间分布不均的场景。

Abstract: Topological signal processing (TSP) processes signals on simplicial complexes with oriented boundary operators, which is the natural choice for flow signals or when the topological invariants play a role for the task at hand. However, many higher-order signals carry no orientation, and applying oriented operators to them is not well-defined since it introduces an arbitrary choice of simplex orientation. We study an unoriented TSP (UTSP) framework that replaces oriented boundaries with unoriented incidence matrices. First, we show that unoriented incidence and Laplacian matrices between arbitrary simplicial levels admit graph-like spectral properties. Second, since dropping orientation removes the Hodge decomposition, we introduce an unoriented counterpart, termed interaction-order decomposition, which quantifies how much of a higher-order signal is explained by aggregating lower-order signals. Third, we use this decomposition to derive regularizers for signal reconstruction that penalize each interaction order separately. Experiments on real-world data show that the order-aware regularizers outperform oriented baselines, with the largest gains when the signal energy is unevenly distributed across orders.

</details>


### [228] [Spatiotemporal Kronecker Covariance Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25326)
*Andrea Cavallo,Athanasios Georgoutsos,Elvin Isufi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Kronecker协方差神经网络（KVNN），用Kronecker积和分解时空协方差，实现可谱分析、有限样本稳定的时空图神经网络，预测性能强且参数少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多元时间序列含复杂时空模式，ST-PCA等协方差统计工具仅限线性且小样本易估计误差；现有协方差时空神经网络虽非线性稳定，却忽略不同时间步间相关性。

Method: KVNN将时空协方差矩阵表示为若干Kronecker积之和，解耦空间与时间依赖，并在空间和时间分量上分别执行滤波操作，从而获得表达力、谱分析与有限样本稳定性。

Result: 在五个真实数据集上，KVNN预测性能强，往往所需可训练参数显著少于竞争方法，且在估计噪声下保持一致。

Conclusion: KVNN通过Kronecker积解耦时空依赖，克服ST-PCA局限，兼具表达力、谱分析、稳定性与参数高效性。

Abstract: Multivariate time series contain complex patterns that span across both space and time. While covariance-based statistical tools like spatiotemporal Principal Component Analysis (ST-PCA) help identify these patterns, they are limited to linear operations and prone to estimation errors with limited data. Recent covariance-based spatiotemporal neural networks offer more stable, non-linear alternatives, but they ignore correlations across different time steps. To solve this, we introduce the Kronecker coVariance Neural Network (KVNN), a temporal graph neural network that represents the spatiotemporal covariance matrix via a sum of Kronecker products where spatial and temporal dependencies are decoupled. By implementing filtering operations on spatial and temporal components, KVNNs achieve expressive processing capabilities, admit a rigorous spectral analysis, and are provably stable to finite-sample estimation errors, ultimately addressing all of ST-PCA's limitations. We show on five real-world datasets that KVNNs achieve strong forecasting performance, often requiring significantly fewer trainable parameters than competitive methods, and are consistent under estimation noise.

</details>


### [229] [MT-ProtBERT: Multi-task Learning ProtBERT for Intrinsically Disordered Proteins Classification with Scarce Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25334)
*Jian Sun,Kingshuk Ghosh,Lilianna Houston,Mohammad H. Mahoor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MT-ProtBERT通过对ProtBERT进行多任务扩展，结合动态窗口掩码、多尺度一维卷积分类器及生化知识辅助任务，在低数据条件下有效提升了无序蛋白（IDP）相关任务的建模性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内在无序蛋白（IDP）具有动态性、缺乏稳定三维构象且序列相似性低，这导致传统实验手段难以测定其构象，同时造成数据稀缺，难以对相似或不同的IDP进行分类和检测，而这一任务对理解生物学和进化具有重要意义。

Method: 提出Multi-task ProtBERT（MT-ProtBERT），即面向低数据场景的ProtBERT多任务扩展。该框架整合了动态窗口掩码（Dynamic Window Masking）、多尺度一维卷积分类器（MS-Conv1D）以及辅助目标，联合优化掩码语言建模与生化知识驱动的任务。

Result: 在两项数据有限的任务上评估：(i) 短序列和小数据集中的磷酸化位点预测（S/T/Y）；(ii) 两个小数据集（684和530条序列，包含长度接近典型无序区的序列）上的蛋白致密化预测。MT-ProtBERT在所有任务上均持续优于基于RNN的IDP专用模型PARROT。

Conclusion: 将自监督任务与生化知识驱动任务相结合，并引入多尺度学习，能够在数据稀缺条件下实现对非结构化蛋白的稳健建模。

Abstract: Intrinsically disordered proteins (IDPs) differ from folded proteins in that they are dynamic, lack a stable three-dimensional conformation, and have low sequence similarity between similar proteins. The conformational heterogeneity of IDPs - while beneficial for their diverse functions - limits the use of traditional experimental tools to determine their conformation. The experimental difficulty, along with low sequence similarity, results in data scarcity, and makes it difficult to classify/detect IDPs that are similar or dissimilar, a task relevant to understand biology and evolution. We address this challenge using Multi-task ProtBERT (MT-ProtBERT), a multi-task extension of ProtBERT tailored for low-data regimes. MT-ProtBERT integrates Dynamic Window Masking, a Multi-Scale 1D Convolutional classifier (MS-Conv1D), and auxiliary objectives that jointly optimize masked language modeling and biochemistry-informed tasks. We evaluate this framework on two tasks under limited data: (i) phosphorylation site prediction (S/T/Y) in short sequences and small datasets, and (ii) protein compaction prediction on two small datasets (684 and 530 sequences), including sequences comparable in length to typical disordered regions. MT-ProtBERT consistently outperforms PARROT, an RNN-based IDP-specific model, across all tasks. These results demonstrate that combining self-supervised and biochemistry-informed tasks, and multi-scale learning enables robust modeling of unstructured proteins under data scarcity.

</details>


### [230] [Concept Drift from a Causal Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25340)
*Eduardo V. L. Barboza,Jean Paul Barddal,Robert Sabourin,Rafael M. O. Cruz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从结构因果模型(SCM)的因果视角重新定义概念漂移,提出按漂移因果来源分类的taxonomy,并构建SCM数据流生成器,实证表明不同因果来源的漂移会产生不同的分布偏移与预测行为模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实数据流中概念漂移普遍存在,会降低预测模型性能。现有定义多将漂移刻画为联合分布P(x,y)的变化,却不区分是数据生成过程的哪个成分发生了变化,缺乏因果层面的解释力,也限制了自适应学习方法评估的针对性与真实性。

Method: 基于结构因果模型提出概念漂移的因果视角,建立按因果来源划分的漂移分类体系,涵盖外生变量变化、内生机制变化、混杂因素变化与目标生成过程变化;据此开发SCM数据流生成器,模拟受控的机制层面漂移事件;并结合因果发现方法,构造基于真实依赖结构的数据流。

Result: 实验刻画了各类漂移的分布效应,发现不同因果来源的漂移会诱发不同的分布偏移模式与预测行为;基于因果发现构建的真实结构数据流能提供更真实、更具信息量的评估场景;利用生成数据还可提升下游任务性能。

Conclusion: 研究非平稳环境时考虑因果结构至关重要,该工作为自适应学习方法的因果感知评估奠定了基础。

Abstract: Concept drift is a common phenomenon in real-world data streams, in which changes in the data-generating distribution can degrade predictive model performance. Most existing definitions characterize drift as changes in the joint distribution $P(\mathbf{x}, y)$, without distinguishing which component of the data-generating process has changed. In this work, we introduce a causal perspective on concept drift based on Structural Causal Models (SCMs). We propose a taxonomy that categorizes drift events by their causal origin, including changes in exogenous variables, endogenous mechanisms, confounders, and target-generating processes. Building on this framework, we develop an SCM-based data stream generator that simulates controlled mechanism-level drift events. Our experiments empirically characterize the distributional effects of each drift type and show that drifts with different causal origins induce distinct patterns of distribution shift and predictive behavior. Furthermore, by integrating causal discovery methods, we use our framework to construct data streams grounded in real-world dependency structures, enabling more realistic and informative evaluation scenarios. We also demonstrate that leveraging the generated data can improve downstream performance. These results highlight the importance of accounting for causal structure when studying and evaluating adaptive learning methods, and establish a foundation for causally-aware evaluation in non-stationary environments.

</details>


### [231] [Extending FunctionGemma for Practical On-Device Mobile Function Calling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25373)
*Ali Rezagholizadeh,Soheila Samiee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将 FunctionGemma 270M-it 扩展到 Android 本地设备控制，构建约 9,500 条会话的合成数据集 MOBILEACTIONSEXTENDED，微调后端到端准确率从 29.3% 提升至 76.5%，并发布数据、模型、训练评估流程与 Android 演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端侧助手需要函数调用模型把自然语言映射为本地系统操作，但现有资源多聚焦 Web API 或范围狭窄的移动动作目录，缺乏覆盖实际 Android 工作流的资源。

Method: 构建 MOBILEACTIONSEXTENDED 合成数据集，约 9,500 条会话、覆盖十五类设备控制（消息、通话、相机/截图、亮度、设备状态查询、手电筒、应用管理等），经 schema 校验；基于 TRL 监督微调，采用仅补全损失，训练扩展专业模型以及与 Google MOBILEACTIONSGOOGLE 联合训练的合并模型。

Result: 在 MOBILEACTIONSEXTENDED 上端到端准确率由基座 29.3%、Google Mobile-Actions 变体 17.2% 提升至 76.5%；合并模型在该集保持 76.5%，在 MOBILEACTIONSGOOGLE 上达 82.3%（低于 Google 专业模型的 90.3%），以 8.0 个百分点换取类别覆盖翻倍。

Conclusion: 紧凑的本地函数调用是通往低延迟、隐私保护移动助手的可行路径，并开源数据集、微调模型、可复现训练/评估流程及 Android 演示。

Abstract: On-device assistants require function-calling models that map natural language to local system actions, but existing resources emphasize web APIs or narrow mobile-action catalogs. We extend FunctionGemma 270M-it to practical Android workflows by introducing MOBILEACTIONSEXTENDED, a synthetic, schema-validated dataset of ~9,500 conversations covering fifteen device-control categories, including messaging, phone calls, camera/screenshot, brightness control, device-status queries, flashlight control, and application management. We fine-tune the 270M model with TRL supervised fine-tuning under completion-only loss, producing an extended specialist and a combined model trained jointly with Google's MOBILEACTIONSGOOGLE. On MOBILEACTIONSEXTENDED, end-to-end accuracy improves from 29.3% for the base model and 17.2% for Google's Mobile-Actions variant to 76.5%. The combined model retains 76.5% on MOBILEACTIONSEXTENDED and reaches 82.3% on MOBILEACTIONSGOOGLE, down from the 90.3% of Google's Mobile-Actions specialist, representing an 8.0-percentage-point trade-off in return for doubling category coverage. We release the dataset, fine-tuned models, reproducible training/evaluation pipeline, and an Android demo, highlighting compact local function calling as a practical path towards low-latency and privacy-preserving mobile assistants.

</details>


### [232] [Deep Reinforcement Learning on Item-Compatibility Graphs for One-Dimensional Bin Packing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25397)
*M. Aslı Aydın*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种端到端、尺寸无关的图强化学习框架求解一维装箱问题（1D-BPP），将装箱过程建模为物品兼容图上的马尔可夫决策过程，用图神经网络actor-critic策略配合随机束搜索解码，单模型可零样本泛化到任意规模实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 1D-BPP是经典NP难组合优化问题，应用广泛，但已有学习型装箱方法多集中于2D/3D变体，针对1D-BPP的智能学习求解器仍然稀缺，因此需要构建专门的数据驱动求解框架。

Method: 将装箱过程表述为物品兼容图上的马尔可夫决策过程，作为结构知识表示，每个动作合并两个可相容的部分箱；采用图神经网络actor-critic策略提取关系特征，通过强化学习训练、随机束搜索解码，实现单一模型对任意规模实例的零样本泛化；并系统研究图编码器、DRL算法、奖励函数、训练分布和超参数。

Result: 在BPPLIB基准上零样本评估：相比构造式启发式将平均最优性差距从2.66%降至2.31%，在结构化实例上提升最大；与同基准上的学习基线相比，在九类中多数家族差距更低且跨分布更稳定；在最难的基准家族上，性能超过依赖列生成和整数规划的最先进学习求解器，且完全不用求解器。整体上分组遗传算法仍然领先。

Conclusion: 该图强化学习框架为1D-BPP提供了有效且尺寸无关的学习型求解方案，展现出良好的零样本泛化与稳定性，但相较分组遗传算法仍存在残余差距，论文进一步分析了差距产生的位置与原因。

Abstract: The one-dimensional bin packing problem (1D-BPP) is a classical NP-hard combinatorial optimization problem with applications ranging from logistics and manufacturing to cloud resource management. Although deep reinforcement learning (DRL) has become a competitive paradigm for data-driven optimization, most learned packing methods target 2D and 3D variants, and intelligent learned solvers for 1D-BPP remain scarce. In this paper, we present a novel end-to-end, size-agnostic graph reinforcement learning framework for 1D-BPP. We formulate the packing process as a Markov decision process on an item-compatibility graph, serving as a structural knowledge representation in which every action merges two partial bins that fit together. A graph neural network actor-critic policy extracts relational features from this representation and is trained through reinforcement learning and decoded by stochastic beam search, enabling a single trained model to generalize zero-shot to instances of any size. We conduct a systematic empirical study across graph encoders, DRL algorithms, reward functions, training distributions, and hyperparameters. Evaluated zero-shot on the full BPPLIB benchmark against a constructive heuristic, a grouping genetic algorithm, and recent learned methods, our data-driven policy lowers the mean optimality gap of the constructive heuristic from 2.66\% to 2.31\%, with the largest gains on structured instances. Against learned baselines evaluated on the same benchmark, it attains a lower gap on most of the nine families and is far more stable across instance distributions. On the hardest benchmark family, it outperforms a state-of-the-art learned solver that relies on column generation and integer programming, while using no solver at all. A grouping genetic algorithm remains ahead overall, and we analyze where and why the residual gap arises.

</details>


### [233] [Predictive Uncertainty for Neural CAE Surrogates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25430)
*Kaustubh Tangsali,Mohammad Amin Nabian,Kelvin Lee,Carmelo Gonzales,Sanjay Choudhry*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文比较了三种不确定性量化方法（基于高斯过程、MC dropout、深度集成）在几何条件下的神经代理模型中的表现，基于三个工业CAE数据集（外气动与碰撞动力学）评估其不确定性估计的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经代理模型可加速CAE工作流，但用于设计时需具备在不同几何、空间预测场和工程关注量下仍有意义的不确定性估计，因此需考察现有UQ方法在此场景下的适用性。

Method: 对比一种闭式方法与两种采样方法——基于高斯过程的方法、concrete Monte Carlo dropout、深度集成，并在DrivAerStar、AirFRANS和汽车碰撞三个大型工业相关CAE数据集上进行评估，考察不确定性的量级可信度、对误差位置的识别能力、对陌生输入的响应以及对派生工程量的信息量。

Result: 在DrivAerStar数据集上，三种方法均对预测误差较大的位置赋予更高不确定性，基于验证的重缩放使区间覆盖率接近名义值；AirFRANS和汽车碰撞数据集也显示有用的误差排序与区间估计，但方法相对表现随数据集和评估标准而变化。

Conclusion: UQ方法与评估指标的选择应依据具体的下游CAE决策需求。

Abstract: Neural surrogates can substantially accelerate computer-aided engineering (CAE) workflows, but their use in design requires uncertainty estimates that remain meaningful across varying geometries, spatial prediction fields, and engineering quantities of interest. We investigate how established uncertainty quantification (UQ) approaches behave when adapted to geometry-conditioned neural surrogates. We compare one closed-form and two sampling-based approaches-a Gaussian process (GP)-based method, concrete Monte Carlo (MC) dropout, and deep ensembles-and evaluate them on three large, industry-relevant CAE datasets for external aerodynamics and crash dynamics.
  We examine whether predicted uncertainties have credible magnitudes, identify locations with larger prediction errors, respond to unfamiliar inputs, and remain informative for derived engineering quantities. On the DrivAerStar dataset, where all three methods are compared, each generally assigns higher uncertainty to locations with larger prediction errors, and validation-based rescaling brings interval coverage close to nominal on a disjoint in-distribution test set. Results on AirFRANS and automotive crash also show useful error ranking and interval estimates, but the relative performance of the methods changes with the dataset and evaluation criterion. UQ methods and evaluation metrics should therefore be selected based on the intended downstream CAE decision.

</details>


### [234] [Lightweight Ranking Heads: Accelerating Multi-Task Experimentation in Production Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25433)
*Sanjay Surendranath Girija,Aniruddh Nath,Li Wei,Yanhao Jiang,Shawn Andrews,Lukasz Heldt,Yi Wu,Aditya Mahajan,Mohit Sharma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Light Heads 框架，支持在现有多任务排序模型中动态注入新任务，避免冷启动与骨干重训，将实验迭代周期从数周缩短到数天。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产级推荐系统的多任务排序模型结构复杂，引入新预测任务常带来瓶颈：与已有任务产生负向冲突，同时骨干模型和下游模型重训、奖励融合公式调参的代价导致开发与实验周期过长，实验速度慢成为关键挑战。

Method: 提出 Lightweight Ranking Heads（Light Heads）框架，面向持续在线学习环境。通过 stop-gradient 与无状态（stateless）每日训练严格隔离新任务，抑制任务冲突；无需模型冷启动和骨干模型重训；采用集中式配置，可同时向多个模型添加 Light Heads，加速训练数据生成与下游模型联合训练。

Result: 已在 YouTube 规模落地部署，将多任务实验的迭代周期从数周缩短至数天；论文分析了无状态冷启动头的训练动态，并将其性能与完整头（full heads）进行对比，验证了其支撑新排序任务快速 A/B 实验与上线的能力，并带来可衡量的线上收益。

Conclusion: Light Heads 通过解耦新任务与骨干模型，解决了多任务推荐系统新任务引入的冲突与慢实验问题，实现了快速实验与部署，具备大规模生产价值。

Abstract: Modern production-scale recommender systems rely on complex, multi-task ranking models. Introducing new prediction tasks into these massive systems often causes bottlenecks - it risks negative task conflicts with existing tasks, and can lead to long development and experimentation cycles due to the expensive retraining of backbone models and downstream models or tuning of reward combination formulas. To address the critical challenge of slow experimentation velocity, we introduce the Lightweight Ranking Heads (Light Heads) framework. Designed for continuous online learning environments, Light Heads enable the dynamic injection of new tasks into existing multi-task ranking models, effectively obviating the need for model cold-starting and retraining of backbone models. By utilizing stop-gradients and stateless daily training, this design strictly isolates new tasks, mitigating the risk of adverse task conflicts. Crucially, this framework uses a centralized configuration that allows Light Heads to be added to multiple models simultaneously, unblocking faster training data generation and co-training of downstream models. Successfully deployed at YouTube scale, this approach reduces the iteration cycle for multi-task experimentation from several weeks to days. In this paper, we detail the system architecture, analyze the training dynamics of stateless cold-started heads, compare their performance to full heads, and demonstrate how Light Heads have enabled the rapid A/B experimentation and deployment of new ranking tasks that yield measurable production value.

</details>


### [235] [PermuFormer: Multi-Task Pretraining for Permutation Representation in Algebraic Combinatorics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25438)
*Henry Kvinge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PermuFormer，一个在28亿token多任务多编码语料上自回归预训练的transformer，用于代数组合学中置换相关任务，作为微调起点常优于从零训练模型、MLP基线及同规模通用语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI for math领域多聚焦于用前沿推理模型通过语言求解明确问题，但专用模型仍是重要组成；专用模型通常在数学对象本身而非文本描述上训练，而从零训练可能限制其学习捕捉数学多面性的领域感知表示。

Method: 提出面向代数组合学中置换任务的预训练方法，构建PermuFormer——在2.8亿（28亿）token的多任务、多编码语料上训练的自回归transformer，并将其作为下游任务微调的起点。

Result: PermuFormer在预训练中未见过的基础任务和更复杂的研究级任务上微调时，常优于从零训练的相同架构、MLP基线以及同规模微调通用语言模型；并分析其内部机制，发现部分任务可直接从提示内部表示线性解码，而其他任务需多轮生成后才能解码答案。

Conclusion: 面向数学对象的预训练可为置换相关专用模型提供有效且可迁移的起点，其内部求解机制因任务而异，兼具直接可解码与需迭代生成两类模式。

Abstract: Diverse pretraining has been shown to be an effective method for learning reusable, domain-aware representations that provide a starting point for fine-tuning on downstream tasks. While much of the excitement in AI for math has been concentrated in the use of frontier reasoning models to solve well-specified problems through the medium of language, narrow, specialized models remain an important component of the AI for math ecosystem. In contrast to large language models, specialized models are usually trained directly on the mathematical objects themselves (e.g., graphs, sequences of numbers) rather than the textual descriptions that characterize these objects. However, the common practice of training specialists from scratch may limit their ability to develop domain-aware representations that capture the multifaceted nature of mathematics. In this paper, we describe an approach to pretraining for permutation-focused tasks in algebraic combinatorics. We introduce PermuFormer, an autoregressive transformer trained on a 2.8 billion token multi-task, multi-encoding corpus. We show that PermuFormer is an effective starting point for fine-tuning on basic tasks unseen during pretraining and more complex research-level tasks, frequently outperforming the same architecture trained from scratch, baseline MLPs, and a fine-tuned generic language model of comparable size. We also analyze some of the internal mechanisms by which PermuFormer learns to solve training tasks. For example, we show that while some tasks can be linearly decoded directly from the internal representation of the prompt, other tasks require multiple rounds of generation before the answer can be decoded.

</details>


### [236] [Mean Velocity Matching: Rethinking Generative Dynamics in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25444)
*Yunhong Zhang,Changjie Cao,Zhihua Zhang,Bingli Liu,Zongjie Cao,Zongyong Cui,Ying Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Mean Velocity Matching (MVM)，用单一学习到的速度场直接参数化扩散模型的随机逆向动力学，并引入√t缩放解决t→0时的无界问题；在ImageNet 32×32与256×256上取得有竞争力的FID，并统一了SDE与ODE采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于速度的生成模型虽只需学习单一传输场、形式简洁，但标准形式是确定性的；若要引入随机性，通常需要额外的score信息或速度与score之间的中间重构，增加了建模复杂度。作者希望保留单场预测的简洁性，同时直接支持随机逆向动力学。

Method: MVM构造一个高斯扰动过程，使"恢复导向速度"(x_0-x_t)/t的条件期望直接构成逆向SDE的漂移项，从而无需单独估计或重构score，单个学习场即可参数化随机逆向过程。由于该速度在t→0附近直接回归会无界，MVM进一步引入√t缩放参数化，在保持逆向动力学不变的同时给出有界的训练目标。同一学习场还可诱导出确定性的概率流ODE，使随机与确定性采样在统一框架下研究。实验采用基于Transformer的生成模型。

Result: 在ImageNet 32×32上取得 MVMImageNetThirtyTwoFID 的FID（MVMImageNetThirtyTwoNFE NFE）；在ImageNet 256×256上取得 MVMImageNetTwoFiftySixFID 的FID（MVMImageNetTwoFiftySixNFE NFE）。受控的SDE–ODE对比显示：在极低NFE下ODE表现更好，而在函数评估次数充足时随机逆向过程获得更低的FID。

Conclusion: MVM为随机逆向动力学提供了直接的单场参数化方法，在保持有竞争力的生成质量的同时，统一了随机与确定性采样的表述。

Abstract: This work studies prediction parameterization for stochastic generative dynamics in diffusion models. Existing velocity-based generative models provide the simplicity of learning a single transport field, but their standard formulation is deterministic, whereas stochastic extensions generally require additional score information or an intermediate velocity-to-score reconstruction. To retain single-field prediction while directly supporting stochastic reverse dynamics, this paper introduces Mean Velocity Matching (MVM). MVM constructs a Gaussian perturbation process for which the conditional expectation of a restoration-oriented velocity, $(x_0-x_t)/t$, directly forms the reverse-SDE drift. Consequently, a single learned field is sufficient to parameterize the stochastic reverse process without separately estimating or reconstructing the score. Because direct regression of this velocity becomes unbounded near $t=0$, MVM further introduces a $\sqrt{t}$-scaled parameterization that preserves the reverse dynamics while yielding a bounded training target. The same learned field also induces a deterministic probability-flow ODE, enabling stochastic and deterministic sampling to be studied within a unified formulation. Experiments with Transformer-based generative models achieve an FID of $\MVMImageNetThirtyTwoFID$ at \MVMImageNetThirtyTwoNFE\ NFE on ImageNet $32\times32$ and $\MVMImageNetTwoFiftySixFID$ at \MVMImageNetTwoFiftySixNFE\ NFE on ImageNet $256\times256$. Controlled SDE--ODE comparisons further show that the ODE performs better under very low NFE, whereas the stochastic reverse process achieves lower FID when sufficient function evaluations are available. These results demonstrate that MVM provides a direct single-field parameterization of stochastic reverse dynamics while maintaining competitive generation quality.

</details>


### [237] [A Practical Recipe for Semi-Supervised Federated ASR: Online Pseudo-Labels with Server Update Stabilization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25471)
*Wonho Bae,Zakaria Aldeneh,Martin Pelikan,Jan "Honza" Silovsky,Tatiana Likhomanenko,Sheikh Shams Azam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究半监督联邦学习（SSFL）在自动语音识别（ASR）中的应用，揭示教师模型（伪标签生成）与锚点（服务器端带标签数据更新）这两个耦合设计轴，并给出缩小与全监督联邦学习差距的指导原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ASR 场景下 SSFL 极为脆弱：伪标签错误会在输出序列和训练轮次间累积，导致训练发散，与全监督联邦学习存在较大差距。作者希望系统性地理解并弥合这一差距。

Method: 作者围绕两个耦合轴展开分析。教师轴：比较逐客户端在线教师（各客户端自身演化模型）、广播式全局教师（服务器单一模型，轮内固定）以及过渡式教师（全局→在线，在第 r 轮切换）。锚点轴：服务器在轮次间持续在带标签数据上训练以稳定在线教师，并分析数据增强与批大小（控制输入与梯度噪声）的影响，以及稳定需求随数据域变化的规律（由种子数据离散度及其与客户端数据重叠度决定）。

Result: 逐客户端在线教师会自行发散，但一旦稳定后即可匹配甚至超过广播全局教师（域内显著、域偏移下具竞争力）。随着种子增强，在线教师优势收窄，过渡式教师可匹配或超过两者。锚点方面，服务器必须持续在带标签数据上训练，否则在线教师漂移，且该交错训练比种子模型本身更主导收敛。两轴不可分割：激进的教师选择需在锚点稳定训练后才见效，而锚点对数据增强和批大小高度敏感。方法在 11 对设置中的 9 对上超过最强先前方法，域内平均提升 20.8%，跨域提升 10.0%。

Conclusion: 研究给出 SSFL 用于 ASR 训练的实用指导原则，并显著缩小了与全监督联邦学习的差距。教师与锚点两轴必须协同设计，稳定化需求依数据域而定。

Abstract: Semi-supervised federated learning (SSFL) trains models on clients' unlabeled data using a teacher to generate pseudo-labels, with a small labeled seed dataset on the server. Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR) is particularly fragile here: pseudo-label errors compound across the output sequence and across training rounds into divergence, leaving a large gap to fully-supervised FL. We show that closing this gap turns on two coupled design axes -- the teacher (which model generates the pseudo-labels) and the anchor (the server-side updates on labeled data that stabilize training). On the teacher axis, a per-client online teacher (each client's own evolving model) diverges on its own, but once stabilized it matches or beats the broadcast global teacher (one server model, fixed within a round) -- decisively in-domain and competitively under domain shift. As the seed grows stronger and the online teacher's advantage narrows, a transitioning teacher (global $\rightarrow$ online at round $r$) matches or beats both. On the anchor axis, the server must keep training on labeled data between rounds -- otherwise the online teacher drifts -- and this interleaving, more than the seed model, governs convergence. The two axes are inseparable: aggressive teacher choices pay off only once the anchor stabilizes training, which is highly sensitive to data augmentation and batch size -- the settings that govern how much input and gradient noise the server injects. How much stabilization is needed is domain-dependent, governed by the dispersion of the seed data and its overlap with client data. These findings yield guidelines for SSFL in ASR training, improving over the strongest prior method on 9 of 11 pairs, by $20.8\%$ on average in-domain and $10.0\%$ cross-domain, narrowing the gap to fully-supervised FL.

</details>


### [238] [Terminal Shrinkage Averaging Reveals a Schedule-Estimator Interaction in LLM Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25482)
*Adam Ousherovitch,Yixin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出终端收缩平均（TSA），在原始最终迭代与近期检查点平均之间插值，解耦学习率调度与估计器设计，降低返回模型的终端方差并保持优化进展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LLM预训练直接返回原始最终迭代，导致学习率调度（生成参数轨迹）与估计器（部署模型构造方式）两个设计选择相互耦合；促进优化进展的调度与减小原始最终迭代方差的调度可能不同，因此需要将二者分离。

Method: 提出Terminal Shrinkage Averaging (TSA)，在原始最终迭代与近期检查点平均之间进行插值，以平衡近期进展与终端方差；在局部二次近似下分析TSA如何改变偏好的终端学习率调度，并通过一系列受控NanoChat实验检验二者交互，最后在depth-22 NanoChat上验证迁移效果。

Result: 在受控NanoChat实验中验证了调度与估计器的交互作用；结果表明该组合能迁移至depth-22 NanoChat并提升验证质量；一次合格的时间到GPT-2运行也快于实验所用的公开基线，提供了基准加速的初步证据。

Conclusion: 通过解耦学习率调度与模型估计器，TSA可在训练后期维持优化进展的同时降低返回模型的方差，并在更大规模NanoChat及时间到GPT-2基准上展现出性能与加速潜力。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) pretraining conventionally returns the raw final iterate. This couples two design choices: the learning-rate schedule that generates the parameter trajectory and the estimator that constructs the deployed model (e.g. the raw final iterate or a checkpoint average). A schedule that promotes optimization progress may differ from one that minimizes variation in the raw final iterate. Separating these choices creates an opportunity to maintain progress late in training while reducing variation in the returned model. To this end, we propose \emph{Terminal Shrinkage Averaging (TSA)}, which interpolates between the raw final iterate and the average of recent checkpoints to balance recent progress against terminal variation. We analyze how TSA changes the preferred terminal learning-rate schedule under a local quadratic approximation and test this interaction through a sequence of controlled NanoChat experiments. Finally, we demonstrate that the resulting gains transfer to depth-22 NanoChat, where the combined schedule and estimator improve validation quality. A qualifying time-to-GPT-2 run also finishes faster than the public baseline used in our experiments, providing preliminary evidence of benchmark acceleration.

</details>


### [239] [Learning Defensive Policies against Diverse Inference Attacks for Smart Meter Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25484)
*Ruichang Zhang,Mustafa A. Mustafa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出代理引导的分层强化学习框架，通过学习基于电池的负载塑造策略注入误导性电器签名，实现黑盒NILM推理防御的跨模型泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能电表数据在提供细粒度用电可见性的同时带来隐私风险，NILM推理攻击可从聚合信号恢复敏感行为模式；实际中攻击者模型未知且异构，现有防御难以泛化。

Method: 将SM隐私保护建模为黑盒推理防御问题；提出代理引导的分层强化学习框架，学习电池负载塑造策略注入真实但误导的电器级签名；采用自监督聚合结构隐私探针提供基于重构误差的代理奖励，并借助签名库保证扰动与电器相关且物理可实现；给出代理引导优化提升攻击者多样性下鲁棒性的理论依据。

Result: 在UK-DALE和REDD真实数据集上跨模型、跨电器泛化良好；面对六个未见NILM攻击者（UK-DALE四种电器、REDD五种），电器级RMSE平均提升107%和166%，F1分数下降79%和80%。

Conclusion: 代理引导的分层强化学习负载塑造方法能在未知异构攻击者下有效破坏可恢复的负载结构，显著削弱NILM的电器级推理能力。

Abstract: Smart meter (SM) data provides fine-grained visibility into household energy consumption, but also exposes users to privacy risks. Inference attacks, known as non-intrusive load monitoring (NILM), can perform appliance-level inference from aggregate signals and recover sensitive behavioral patterns. In practice, attacker models are unknown and heterogeneous, making robust defense challenging. We formulate SM privacy protection as a black-box inference defense problem, aiming to reduce the recoverability of appliance-level information while generalizing across diverse and unseen attackers. We propose a proxy-guided hierarchical reinforcement learning framework that learns battery-based load-shaping policies to inject realistic but misleading appliance-level signatures into the aggregate signal, thereby disrupting the structured patterns exploited by NILM. A self-supervised aggregate-structure privacy probe provides a reconstruction-error-based surrogate reward for disrupting recoverable load structure, while a signature library makes the perturbations appliance-relevant and physically realizable through battery control. We provide theoretical rationale showing that proxy-guided optimization improves inference robustness under attacker diversity. Experiments on real-world datasets UK-DALE and REDD demonstrate strong cross-model and cross-appliance generalization. Across six unseen NILM attackers, covering four appliances on UK-DALE and five on REDD, our proposed defense increases average appliance-level RMSE by 107% and 166%, respectively, while reducing F1 score by 79% and 80%.

</details>


### [240] [Continuous Optimization for p-adic Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25501)
*Julian Salazar,Dimitri Kanevsky,Matt Harvey,Pascal Getreuer,Lucas Dixon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出首个针对 p 进参数机器学习模型的连续梯度下降方法，通过 Berkovich 仿射线实现连续优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: p 进数 Q_p 完全不连通，标准损失在最小值之外平坦，导致现有原生优化器大多为离散的、组合式搜索，无法进行连续优化。

Method: 利用 Q_p 的 Berkovich 仿射线（一种保持等距、唯一延拓解析映射的路径连通扩张，是带可解释点和局部导数的度量树），在此结构上构建梯度下降、动量与 Adam 变体，并实现反向传播。

Result: 梯度下降近似能高效学习系数在 Q_p 中的线性模型，完成实数域线性模型无法表达的类 XOR 模运算任务；同时在二值编码层级（Quillian 语义网络）上验证了线性回归与分类。

Conclusion: 该工作实现了 p 进参数模型的连续梯度优化，解决了 Martins (2025) 提出的开放问题，并开源了代码库。

Abstract: We present the first method for native, continuous gradient descent for machine learning models with $p$-adic parameters. Existing native optimizers are discrete, mostly combinatorial searches, as the $p$-adic numbers $\mathbb{Q}_p$ are totally disconnected, with standard losses that are flat away from their minima. To enable continuous optimization, we propose working with $\mathbb{Q}_p$ via its Berkovich affine line: a canonical, path-connected expansion of $\mathbb{Q}_p$ that preserves its isometries and uniquely extends its analytic maps. This hull is a metric tree with interpretable points and local derivatives, which we show enables effective optimizers and backpropagation. We formulate gradient descent and show that its approximations efficiently learn linear models with coefficients in $\mathbb{Q}_p$ to do modular arithmetic, an XOR-like task not expressible by linear models in $\mathbb{R}$. We also demonstrate momentum and Adam variants, linear regression, and classification on binary-encoded hierarchies (Quillian semantic networks), addressing open problems posed by Martins (2025). Library at https://github.com/google-deepmind/padic-ml

</details>


### [241] [Hill Sampling for Test-Time Scaling: A Simple and Better Alternative to Repeated Sampling, Evolution, and Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25510)
*Jacob Beck,Philip V. Ogren,Ari Kobren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 Hill Sampling：一种简单的测试时计算策略，反复从冻结 LLM 采样候选程序编辑，保留当前最优程序并以其为条件继续采样；在圆堆积、集合和差、Erdős 最小重叠问题上取得强结果，并发现其优于测试时权重学习和更复杂的进化搜索机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期提升 LLM 在可验证科学与算法问题上表现的方法越来越复杂，依赖精细的进化搜索框架或测试时训练更新参数。作者希望探究：这些复杂机制究竟有多少是必要的，能否用更简单的方法达到甚至超越其效果。

Method: 提出 Hill Sampling：从冻结的 LLM 中反复采样候选程序编辑，始终保留目前已找到的最优程序，并将后续所有采样都以该最优程序为条件。在三个开放权重模型上，针对圆堆积、集合的和与差、Erdős 最小重叠问题进行评测；同时进行按参数量计规模最大的测试时进化策略（ES）直接优化 LLM 权重的研究，对比学习权重与将 ES 学习率设为零的效果。

Result: Hill Sampling 在圆堆积问题上刷新已发表方法的最佳结果，在 Erdős 最小重叠问题上超越 AlphaEvolve 基线，并在有限集合的和与差问题上取得强结果；圆堆积和 Erdős 结果仅需 8 块 NVIDIA H100 GPU 数小时。ES 权重学习效果反而不如将学习率设为零（此时仍通过固定随机扰动在权重空间搜索）；这些扰动有助于探索，但 token 采样的随机性更强，且重复采样仍显著弱于 Hill Sampling。

Conclusion: 结果支持一种简单的测试时计算分配策略：在引入档案库、多样性机制、进化脚手架或测试时参数学习等额外复杂性之前，应优先反复对当前已验证的最优解采样编辑。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can improve solutions to verifiable scientific and algorithmic problems by spending additional computation at test time. Recent systems achieve strong results with increasingly elaborate evolutionary search harnesses or by updating model parameters during test-time training. We ask how much of this machinery is necessary. We introduce Hill Sampling, a simple procedure that repeatedly samples candidate program edits from a frozen LLM, retains the best program found so far, and conditions all subsequent samples on that program. We evaluate the method on circle packing, sums/differences of sets, and Erdos' minimum-overlap problem using three open-weight models. Hill Sampling sets a new state of the art on circle packing among published methods, improves over the AlphaEvolve reference on Erdos' minimum-overlap problem, and achieves strong results on sums and differences of finite sets. The circle-packing and Erdos results require only hours of wall-clock time on eight NVIDIA H100 GPUs. To our knowledge, we also conduct, the largest study, by parameter count, of evolution strategies (ES) applied directly to LLM weights at test time. Surprisingly, learning the weights is worse than setting the ES learning rate to zero: at zero learning rate, the method is still searching in weight space through fixed random perturbations. Those perturbations can help exploration, but randomness from token sampling is stronger still, and repeated sampling remains substantially weaker than Hill Sampling. These results suggest a simple test-time compute allocation strategy: repeatedly sample edits to the best verified solution found so far, before introducing additional complexity such as adding archives, diversity mechanisms, evolutionary scaffolds, or test-time parameter learning.

</details>


### [242] [A JEPA Recipe for Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25541)
*Mingyu Jeon,Suwan Cho,Jae Young Suh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在表格基础模型先验下，本文提出一种使JEPA潜在目标与数值目标共存并收敛的训练配方，但JEPA臂在多数数据集上仍落后于纯数值臂，且收敛更慢。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格基础模型通过上下文预测单元格数值，而世界模型式自监督则要求在表示空间预测。作者此前尝试将JEPA的潜在项与数值目标结合时，潜在项出现坍缩并导致编码器退化为常数映射，因此需要寻找一种能让潜在目标存活到收敛的配方。

Method: 配方包含两点：数值头读取编码器场而非预测器；目标采用指数移动平均（EMA）差值。为公平比较成本，两臂均训练到平台规则停止，而非固定步数预算（固定步长曾把减速误判为上限）。每个臂各运行一次，在147个真实数据集上比较。

Result: 收敛时JEPA臂整体落后于纯数值臂：分类为32胜70负（按数据集名去重为29胜63负），回归为8胜24负；分类差距较小，回归差距更宽，且在各分层与各基准上胜负方向一致。JEPA臂达到平台所需步数为纯数值臂的1.42倍，墙钟时间为1.66倍。

Conclusion: 所提配方可避免潜在项坍缩并使其与数值目标共同收敛，但当前JEPA臂在准确性与训练效率上均劣于纯数值臂；作者据此报告该负面结果，而非宣称JEPA在表格基础模型先验上的优势。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models learn to predict cell values in context, whereas world-model self-supervision asks for prediction in representation space (LeCun, 2022; Assran et al., 2023). On a tabular foundation-model prior, the latent term of a joint-embedding predictive architecture (JEPA) collapsed in our earlier runs and took the encoder with it to a constant map. We report a recipe under which the latent term survives to convergence beside the value objective: the value head reads the encoder field rather than the predictor, and the target is an exponential moving average (EMA) difference. To bound its cost against the value-only arm, both arms train until a plateau rule stops them, with no fixed step budget. A fixed horizon had confounded a slowdown with a ceiling, since the value-only arm was still improving well past the usual budget. At convergence, in one run per arm, the JEPA arm trails the value-only arm across 147 real datasets, 32:70 wins to losses on classification (29:63 with one entry per dataset name) and 8:24 on regression, the margin small on classification and wider on regression, and the count leans the same way in each stratum and each benchmark. The JEPA arm (jepa) needs 1.42 times as many steps as the value-only arm (ds), and 1.66 times its wall-clock, to reach its plateau.

</details>


### [243] [DefaultGNN: A Dual-Perspective GNN Framework for Predicting Corporate Default from Buyer-Seller Transaction Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25542)
*Junghoon Kim,Hyunsung Kim,Seungyoon Choi,KyoungYong Park,Jihun Lee,YongGu Ji,Chanyoung Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用六年电子发票交易数据，构建买卖双方双视角多层交易网络，提出双视角图神经网络框架DefaultGNN用于企业违约预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统信用模型依赖财务报表，但许多企业财务数据稀疏或缺失；交易网络提供补充视角，但风险如何通过买卖关系传播尚缺乏深入研究。

Method: 基于真实电子税务发票数据开展大规模实证研究，发现交易驱动风险具有角色依赖性（买方/卖方）和规模依赖性；据此构建买方视角与卖方视角的多层交易网络，并提出DefaultGNN双视角图神经网络框架，融合两种视角建模风险在交易关系中的流动。

Result: DefaultGNN显著优于基于属性的和基于图的基线模型，尤其对自身风险信号有限的企业提升明显；通过可视化困境交易伙伴的贡献提供可解释的网络解释；与持牌信用评级机构合作验证，其预测可补充现有信用评分模型，在不增加获批企业违约风险的前提下将通过率提高7-11个百分点。

Conclusion: 交易网络视角能有效补充传统信用评估，双视角图神经网络在违约预测与信贷决策中具有实用价值与应用前景。

Abstract: Corporate default prediction is a core problem in financial risk management, yet traditional credit models rely heavily on financial statements that are often sparse or unavailable for many firms. Corporate transaction networks offer a complementary view of real economic activity, but how risk propagates through buyer-seller relationships remains underexplored. We conduct a large-scale empirical study using real-world electronic tax-invoice data spanning six years that links transaction histories with default events, revealing that transaction-driven risk is both role-dependent (buyer or seller) and scale-dependent. Based on these findings, we construct multiplex buyer-view and seller-view transaction networks and propose DefaultGNN, a dual-perspective graph neural network-based framework for corporate default prediction. DefaultGNN integrates both views to model how risk flows through transactional relationships, achieving strong improvements over both attribute-based and graph-based baselines, especially for firms with limited intrinsic risk signals. We further provide interpretable network-based explanations by visualizing how distressed trading partners contribute to default risk. In collaboration with a licensed credit rating agency, we validate that DefaultGNN's predictions complement existing credit scoring models, improving approval rates by 7-11%p without increasing default risk among approved firms. The source code can be found at https://github.com/jhkim611/DefaultGNN

</details>


### [244] [SambaGraph: Action-Reaction Spatio-Temporal Graphs for Soccer Tactical Response Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25569)
*Abel A. Reyes-Angulo,Henry O. Velesaca,Steven Araujo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SambaGraph：基于2022世界杯数据的动作-反应时空图数据集与基准，用于足球战术响应建模，涵盖响应分类、防守检索与LLM推理三项任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 足球战术本质上是交互式的：进攻动作改变防守方的问题，而观察到的反应取决于多智能体的比赛状态。现有研究缺乏能够刻画这种动作-反应交互关系的图结构数据集与统一评测基准。

Method: 从2022年世界杯全部64场比赛的追踪与事件数据中，构建4,070个以动作-反应为中心的事件片段，表示为时间对齐的23节点球员-球图序列，含攻防双视角、反应标签，以及26,270对防泄漏（split-safe）攻防配对。设置三项任务：图片段反应分类、成功防守检索、图摘要支持的LLM战术推理。模型包括紧凑签名MLP、融合图-签名双编码器，并对比局部LLM表现。

Result: 紧凑签名MLP在反应分类上取得0.796±0.007的macro-F1；融合图-签名双编码器在全库防守检索上达到0.471±0.029的Hit@5与0.655±0.051的Hit@10。难负样本最大化配对区分度但不提升检索质量。局部LLM在直接分类上弱于监督编码器，在八候选重排中未优于强原始顺序，但能提供有依据的战术解释。

Conclusion: SambaGraph可作为基于图的足球战术-响应研究的可复现基准，代码与数据集已开源。

Abstract: Soccer tactics are interactive: an attacking action changes the opponent's defensive problem, and the observed response depends on the multi-agent match state. We introduce SambaGraph, an action--reaction spatio-temporal graph dataset and benchmark for soccer tactical response modeling. From tracking and event data for all 64 matches of the 2022 FIFA World Cup, we curate 4,070 action-centered episodes represented as temporally aligned 23-node player--ball graph sequences with attack/defense views, response labels, and 26,270 split-safe attack--defense pairs. We study three questions: whether observed responses can be classified from graph episodes, whether successful defenses can be retrieved for a query attack, and whether graph-derived summaries support grounded LLM reasoning. A compact signature MLP obtains $0.796\pm0.007$ macro-F1 for response classification, while a fused graph--signature dual encoder reaches $0.471\pm0.029$ Hit@5 and $0.655\pm0.051$ Hit@10 for full-bank defensive retrieval.
