Abstract: Probabilistic time series foundation models (TSFMs) provide coordinate-wise predictive distributions, but these marginals do not determine a joint distribution over multivariate future trajectories. We study training-free coupling of frozen TSFM marginals into multivariate forecast sample paths. Our primary evaluation fixes the empirical marginal sample multiset at every channel--horizon coordinate across methods, isolating the effect of coupling alone. Historical temporal and channel relations substantially improve their corresponding dependence diagnostics. The same pattern persists when the fixed-marginal constraint is removed and paths are sampled directly, and remains present under native multivariate backbone inference. These results support treating dependence reconstruction as a distinct post-processing problem for probabilistic TSFMs.

</details>


### [281] [Theory for groupoid equivariant neural networks: an approach for steerable CNNs on bounded domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25987)
*Alberto Ibort,Maria Jimenez-Vazquez,Juan M. Perez-Pardo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出群胚等变神经网络理论，用群胚、局部双截面的伪群、测度及表示丛构成对称数据，并证明双截面等变核定理；在有界平面域上实现离线零空间基与稀疏 gather–transform–scatter 架构，数值实验显示在对称性无法整体实现为群作用时具有显著优势，精度至少提升一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统等变卷积网络依赖群对信号空间的整体作用，这一假设不适用于有界或分层域：环境刚体运动可能仅在域的一部分上可行，边界还引入了传递群作用无法察觉的几何类型。因此需要更一般的等变理论来处理局部对称性。

Method: 作者发展群胚等变神经网络理论，对称数据由群胚、选定的局部双截面伪群、测度以及输入输出表示丛组成。针对对象空间上的积分通道，证明双截面等变核定理：等变性等价于两点核上的传输约束，其解由每对轨道上的一个联合稳定子交织算子分类。以有界平面域为案例，通过离线零空间基和稀疏 gather–transform–scatter 操作实现架构；另用 Poisson–Dirichlet 核研究评估边界感知归纳偏置，并分析精确逆的对称性保持性质。

Result: 理论结果给出双截面等变核的完整分类；案例研究表明精确逆保持矩形的整体对称性，但不保持一般真局部双截面。数值结果显示，当对称性无法通过群作用整体实现时，所提架构具有显著优势，相比测试模型精度至少提升一个数量级。

Conclusion: 群胚等变框架将等变网络从整体群作用推广到局部双截面，为有界与分层域提供了原则性设计工具，并在数值上验证了其在局部对称场景中的有效性。

Abstract: Equivariant convolutional neural networks are usually built from a group acting globally on the space of signals. This hypothesis is inappropriate for many bounded or stratified domains: an ambient rigid motion may be admissible only on part of the domain, and the boundary introduces geometric types that are invisible to a transitive group action. We develop a theory of groupoid-equivariant neural networks in which the symmetry datum consists of a groupoid, a selected pseudogroup of local bisections, a measure, and input and output representation bundles. For integral channels on the object space, we prove a bisection-equivariant kernel theorem: equivariance is equivalent to a transport constraint on the two-point kernel, and its solutions are classified by one joint-stabilizer intertwiner on each orbit of pairs. As a case study we apply the theory to bounded planar domains.
  The resulting architecture is implemented through offline nullspace bases and sparse gather--transform--scatter operations. A Poisson--Dirichlet kernel study is used separately to assess boundary-aware inductive bias; the exact inverse is shown to preserve the global symmetries of the rectangle but not general proper local bisections. The numerical results show that the proposed architectures provide significant advantages when symmetries cannot be globally implemented by group actions and provide an accuracy improvement of at least one order of magnitude with respect to the models tested.

</details>


### [282] [The Dynamics of Quasiregular Neural Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26018)
*Matthia Sabatelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文在受控的“准规则回归”任务中研究神经网络如何同时学习主导规律与系统性例外，发现网络会先部分习得例外、随后回退到主导规律（过度规则化），最终再恢复，且例外越少过度规则化越强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多学习问题都由一个主导规律加上系统性例外构成，语言习得中的U型学习便是典型例子。作者希望在一个规律与例外都明确已知的受控环境中，隔离并刻画神经网络学习过程中两者之间的竞争机制。

Method: 作者设计了“准规则回归”（quasiregular regression）问题，其中规则解与例外解均被显式定义，从而可以精确追踪模型对两者的掌握程度；并在此受控设定下训练神经网络，观察其学习动态如何随例外数量（稀有程度）及不同规则类型而变化。

Result: 神经网络能够部分习得例外，但随后会回退到主导规律，最后又重新恢复例外——即出现过度规则化现象。当例外较为稀有（数量少）时，过度规则化显著增强，尽管这些例外在早期就被习得；此外，这一现象并非在所有被考察的规则上同等出现。

Conclusion: 研究结果揭示并隔离出神经网络学习中规律与例外之间一种简单形式的竞争关系，为理解语言习得等场景中的U型学习现象提供了受控证据。该结论有助于更一般地认识规则性与例外性知识在神经学习中的动态交互。

Abstract: Many learning problems combine a dominant regularity with systematic exceptions. Motivated by U-shaped learning in language acquisition, we study this interaction in controlled quasiregular regression problems where regular and exceptional solutions are explicitly known. Neural networks can partially acquire exceptions, subsequently regress toward the dominant regularity, and finally recover. This overregularization becomes substantially stronger when exceptions are rare, despite their early acquisition, but does not emerge equally across all regularities considered. Our results isolate a simple form of competition between regularities and exceptions during neural learning.

</details>


### [283] [BOBA: Dynamic Bayesian Optimization through Bayesian Active Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26021)
*Merlin Angel Kelly,Rishan Patel,Alexander Thomas,Ziyue Zhu,Zikun Quan,Tom Carlson,Youngjun Cho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出BOBA，一种基于主动推断自由能原理的动态贝叶斯优化采集函数，通过前瞻性不确定性量化提升对移动最优点的跟踪能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态黑盒优化中目标函数随时间变化，最优点持续移动。现有动态贝叶斯优化（DBO）方法（如基于UCB）未显式考虑时间变化，导致样本分配次优、难以跟踪移动最优解。

Method: 提出BOBA（Bayesian Optimization through Bayesian Active Inference），受主动推断中自由能原理启发，扩展传统采集函数，引入对函数变化不确定性的前瞻性量化，从而在非平稳环境中更合理地权衡探索与利用；并分析不同探索策略下的BOBA变体。

Result: 在合成动态基准上与当前最先进DBO方法对比，BOBA在查询受限场景下显著改善regret，在时间受限场景下保持竞争力；不同探索策略的变体可针对不同类型动态函数调节探索-利用平衡。

Conclusion: 本文贡献了一个面向DBO的基于自由能的采集函数，并揭示了主动推断原理如何增强非平稳环境下的优化，对需要持续适应的实时应用具有启示意义。

Abstract: Dynamic black-box optimization presents significant challenges for Bayesian Optimization (BO), as the objective function evolves over time, causing optimal locations to shift continuously. Existing dynamic BO (DBO) methods using standard acquisition functions such as Upper Confidence Bound (UCB) fail to explicitly account for temporal variations, leading to suboptimal sample allocation and poor tracking of moving optima. Here, we propose BOBA (Bayesian Optimization through Bayesian Active Inference), a novel acquisition function inspired by free energy principles from active inference that explicitly minimizes predictive uncertainty about future states in dynamic environments. BOBA extends traditional acquisition functions by incorporating a forward-looking uncertainty quantification that estimates uncertainty in function changes, enabling more informed exploration-exploitation trade-offs in non-stationary settings. We evaluate BOBA on synthetic dynamic benchmarks, comparing against state-of-the-art DBO methods. Our experiments demonstrate that BOBA significantly improves regret in query-restricted settings, while remaining competitive in time-limited settings. We further analyze variants of BOBA with different exploration strategies, showing how the exploration-exploitation balance can be tuned for different types of dynamic functions. This work contributes both a free energy-based acquisition function for DBO and insights into how active inference principles can enhance optimization in non-stationary environments, with implications for real-time applications requiring continuous adaptation.

</details>


### [284] [MICRO: Multi-Fidelity Active Search for Severe Error Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26025)
*Orlando Leone,Niclas Pokel,Pehuén Moure,Yingqiang Gao,Roman Boehringer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出MICRO主动搜索框架，联合建模评分与标注损失并借助聚类与rollout分配预算，在WMT20英德任务上以有限成本最大化严重错误发现数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类反馈在成本与信息量上存在差异：强反馈能揭示严重错误但代价高昂，而低成本质量评分可辅助判断哪些样本值得标注。因此需要一种能在不同反馈类型间分配共享预算、以最大化已确认严重错误发现的方法。

Method: 提出MICRO（Multi-Fidelity Impact Clustered Rollout）主动搜索框架：联合建模以样本特征为条件的评分与标注损失以引导采样；对采样样本按其对严重性概率的预测影响进行聚类以选取多样化候选；再用rollout估计其发现价值。

Result: 在WMT20英德数据上，评分同时改善损失重建与严重性预测。在四种预算与评分成本设定下，MICRO取得最高平均发现数；其中一种设定与适配的MF-ENS相当，另外三种显著优于全部六种对比策略（含两种rollout对照，p<.001）。

Conclusion: MICRO能有效结合低成本评分与高成本标注，在有限预算下提升严重错误发现效率，并稳健优于现有对比方法。

Abstract: Human feedback can vary in cost and informativeness. Strong feedback can reveal severe errors but is costly, so cheaper quality ratings can help decide which items to annotate. We propose MICRO (Multi-Fidelity Impact Clustered Rollout), an active search framework that allocates a shared budget to these feedback types to maximise confirmed severe error discoveries. MICRO jointly models ratings and annotation losses conditional on item features to steer acquisition. It clusters acquisitions by their predicted impact on severity probabilities to select diverse candidates, then uses rollout to estimate their discovery value. Experiments on WMT20 English-German show that ratings improve both loss reconstruction and severity prediction. MICRO achieves the highest mean discovery count across four budget and rating cost settings, with similar performance to adapted MF-ENS in one and significant gains over all six comparison policies, including two rollout controls, in the other three $(p<.001)$.

</details>


### [285] [xWhyL: Causal Interactive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26037)
*Nicholas Tagliapietra,Florian Peter Busch,Moritz Willig,Matej Zečević,Lavdim Halilaj,Juergen Luettin,Kristian Kersting*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出xWhyL框架，从解释（溯因信号）中学习因果模型，证明其在特定条件下可拒绝错误解释，并实现Causal Interactive Learning（CIL）以高效支持因果发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 认知科学表明人类解释的驱动力本身是学习因果的机制，但AI领域基本忽略了从这类溯因信号中学习。现有可解释AI（XAI）虽借助因果模型生成解释，却很少反向探索解释能为因果性做什么，这一空白亟待填补。

Method: 提出xWhyL形式化框架，将因果性与XAI连接，从解释中学习因果模型；建立数学理论，把解释转化为与观测数据互补的学习信号；针对“因果拔河”（Causal Tug-of-War，即解释可能源于错误信念并与数据冲突）证明在何种条件下框架会拒绝而非吸收错误解释；给出实际实例Causal Interactive Learning（CIL）。

Result: 理论上证明解释可作为互补学习信号，帮助突破观测式因果发现的局限；证明框架在特定条件下能拒绝错误指定的解释；CIL表明专家解释能有效支持因果发现，并区分正确与错误的解释。

Conclusion: 解释作为溯因信号可被形式化用于因果学习，补充观测数据并克服观测因果发现的局限；在满足所证条件时框架可抵御错误解释，专家解释能有效提升因果发现并甄别解释正误。

Abstract: Explanations are central to causal reasoning, and cognitive science has long established that the human drive to explain is itself a mechanism for learning about causality. Despite this, learning from those abductive signals is largely ignored in artificial intelligence. While explainable AI (XAI) increasingly draws on causal models to generate explanations, the converse direction about what explanations can do for causality remains largely unexplored. To fill this gap, we propose xWhyL, a formal framework connecting causality and XAI by learning causal models from explanations. We develop a mathematical theory that translates explanations into a learning signal complementary to observational data, and demonstrate how it enables overcoming the limits of observational causal discovery. As explanations can be derived from incorrect beliefs and clash with data, a tension we call the Causal Tug-of-War, we prove conditions under which our framework rejects misspecified explanations rather than absorbing them. Our practical instantiation, Causal Interactive Learning (CIL), shows how expert explanations can efficiently support causal discovery and distinguish correct from incorrect explanations.

</details>


### [286] [Towards Adaptive Federated Graph Clustering: A Global Community-aware Contrastive Learning-based Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26063)
*Yinlin Zhu,Di Wu,Wang Luo,Guocong Quan,Miao Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 AdaFGC，一种基于全局社区感知对比学习的自适应联邦图聚类框架，解决聚类数目预设不合理与社区间分离不充分的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦图学习多聚焦监督任务，而现实图往往大规模且无标签，联邦图聚类尚不成熟。客户端间子图异质性导致社区结构各不相同，现有方法存在两个关键局限：(1) 不现实的预定义聚类数目假设；(2) 社区间分离不彻底。

Method: AdaFGC 引入一组过完备的全局社区锚点来建模全局社区结构，并通过跨客户端锚点细化自适应估计聚类数目；同时采用全局社区感知的对比学习方案，以共享锚点作为对比原型，在跨客户端显式强化社区级吸引与排斥，并辅以节点级和拓扑级目标以稳定本地表示。

Result: 在八个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，AdaFGC 在多个聚类指标上持续优于现有的有监督与无监督联邦图学习基线。

Conclusion: AdaFGC 通过全局社区锚点与社区感知对比学习，有效缓解子图异质性带来的挑战，为无标签联邦图聚类提供了更优方案。

Abstract: Federated graph learning (FGL) enables multiple clients to collaboratively train graph models without sharing their private graph data, providing a promising paradigm for mining knowledge from distributed graph repositories. While most existing FGL methods focus on supervised tasks, real-world graphs are often massive and unlabeled, making federated graph clustering an important yet still immature research direction. Notably, this task is particularly challenging due to the inherent subgraph heterogeneity across clients, which leads to client-specific community structures. In this work, we identify two critical limitations in existing federated graph clustering methods: (1) unrealistic pre-defined cluster cardinality assumptions and (2) incomplete inter-community separation. To address these challenges, we propose AdaFGC, an Adaptive Federated graph clustering framework based on Global community-aware Contrastive learning. AdaFGC introduces an over-complete set of global community anchors to model the global community structure and adaptively estimate clustering cardinality via cross-client anchor refinement. In addition, it employs a global community-aware contrastive learning scheme that uses the shared anchors as contrastive prototypes to explicitly enforce community-level attraction and repulsion across clients, complemented by node-level and topology-level objectives that stabilize local representations. Extensive experiments on eight benchmark datasets demonstrate that AdaFGC consistently outperforms existing supervised and unsupervised FGL baselines across multiple clustering metrics.

</details>


### [287] [FuncCode: Compressing Kolmogorov--Arnold Networks in Function Space with Hardware-Aware Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26067)
*Kazi Ahmed Asif Fuad,Lizhong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FuncCode：一种基无关的KAN压缩方法，通过共享码本分别编码基分支与base分支，实现高压缩率且精度损失极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KAN用可学习的一元函数替代标量边权重，提升灵活性但导致参数内存增大：每条边需存储多个系数，通常还附带独立的base分支。

Method: 从采样边响应构建共享码本，分别独立编码基分支与base分支，并以量化、位打包格式导出码本与逐边索引；方法基无关，适用于样条与多项式KAN。

Result: 采样边响应的有效秩比系数表示低13–35%；函数空间聚类与系数空间聚类统计上持平，精度增益来自保留两分支的差异化共享结构。十种子MNIST上样条KAN与GRAM KAN分别压缩31.6×和17.6×，精度仅降0.31和0.34个百分点；6.1M边卷积KAGN压缩19.9×，CIFAR-10/CIFAR-100分别仅差0.54/1.89个百分点；压缩后逐边索引占存储权重位高达99.4%，呈索引受限；在九个位精确FPGA加速器上，相对dense INT4将SplineKAN布线后权重内存减少3.87×，且不增加周期数与延迟。

Conclusion: FuncCode证明在函数空间进行基无关码本压缩可大幅降低KAN参数量与硬件内存，同时保持精度，且收益关键在保留两分支的独立共享结构。

Abstract: Kolmogorov--Arnold Networks (KANs) replace scalar edge weights with learnable univariate functions, increasing flexibility but also parameter memory because each edge stores multiple coefficients, often together with a separate base branch. We introduce FuncCode, a basis-agnostic compression approach that forms shared codebooks from sampled edge responses, codes the basis and base branches independently, and exports the resulting codebooks and per-edge indices in a quantized, bit-packed format. Across spline and polynomial KANs, sampled edge responses exhibit $13$--$35\%$ lower effective rank than their coefficient representations. Further replicated controls show that function-space clustering alone is statistically tied with coefficient-space clustering; the consistent accuracy gain comes from preserving the distinct sharing structure of the two branches. On a ten-seed MNIST benchmark, FuncCode compresses spline and GRAM KANs by $31.6\times$ and $17.6\times$ with only $0.31$ and $0.34$ pp accuracy loss. On a 6.1M-edge convolutional KAGN, it achieves $19.9\times$ compression while remaining within $0.54$ pp of dense accuracy on CIFAR-10 and $1.89$ pp on CIFAR-100. After compression, per-edge indices account for up to $99.4\%$ of stored weight bits, making the representation index-bound. Across nine bit-exact FPGA accelerators, FuncCode reduces SplineKAN post-route weight memory by $3.87\times$ relative to dense INT4, without increasing cycle count or latency. The FuncCode implementation is available at https://github.com/OSU-STARLAB/FuncCode.

</details>


### [288] [Fast Matrix Multiplication in fp8: Certified Coefficient Optimization and Measured Error](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26077)
*Shuxiao Xie,Shuyang Xie,Yuan Cao,Dezhi Ran,Wei Yang,Tao Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将 Strassen 型算法的基变换选择形式化为 Hadamard 流形上的 Kempf–Ness 问题，用系数泛函 Φ 的全局最优（Φ_min=200/9）认证最优实现，并在 fp8 与大型 LLM 内核上验证其显著降低误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Strassen 型算法在精确乘积与乘法次数相同的前提下有多种实现（通过基变换得到），其 fp8 误差不同，因此需要回答"该运行哪一种"。现有理论无法解决：经典稳定性只控制最坏情况 ℓ1 增长而非期望误差；Dumas–Pernet–Sedoglavic 优化器只能称为"可能最优"，全局最优性未证。

Method: 为每个实现附加一个系数泛函 Φ（系数几何的标量摘要），并在基变换轨道上最小化 Φ。将该 Kempf–Ness 问题置于 Hadamard 流形上，从而可认证全局 Φ 最优而非仅搜索；通过精确矩映射零点定出 Φ_min = 200/9，并用 de Groote 分类将最优性推广到所有精确实 rank-7 2×2 分解。随后引入显式 block-scaled e4m3 模型，其中 Φ 是相对期望均方误差的首阶系数，据此检验 Φ 预测的排序。

Result: 每个精确实 rank-7 实现的 Φ 预测 RMS 常数至少为三次算法在同一噪声系数下的 5/3 倍。排序与重基实验在测试的融合 block-scaled 区间内支持该预测；在四个架构家族的真实 matmul tile 上，Φ 最优实现落在 fp8 低误差区域。在两个约 70B 模型、真实 deep_gemm 内核上，同一实现消除了经典 Strassen 相对干净模型超出 NLL 的 10%–55%。

Conclusion: 算法实现由此从调参选择变为数学上被认证的设计问题：存在具全局 Φ 最优且经实测验证 fp8 相关性的独立低精度维度。

Abstract: A Strassen-type algorithm has many realizations with the same exact product and multiplication count yet different fp8 error because basis changes reshape coefficient geometry, posing the question of which to run. No current account settles this: classical stability controls worst-case $\ell_1$ growth, not the expected-error magnitude, and the Dumas--Pernet--Sedoglavic optimizer could only be called probably optimal, its global optimality unproved. To settle this, we attach to each realization a coefficient functional $Φ$, a scalar summary of its coefficient geometry, which we minimize over the change-of-basis orbit. This Kempf--Ness problem on a Hadamard manifold lets us certify the global $Φ$ optimum rather than merely search for it: an exact moment-map zero fixes $Φ_{\min} = 200/9$, and de Groote's classification extends that optimality to every exact real rank-7 $2\times2$ decomposition. Every exact real rank-7 realization therefore has a $Φ$-predicted RMS constant at least $5/3$ times that of the cubic algorithm, at fixed noise coefficient. We then introduce an explicit block-scaled e4m3 model in which $Φ$ is the leading-order coefficient of relative expected mean-squared error, and we test the resulting $Φ$-predicted ordering against realized fp8 error. Ordering and re-basing experiments support that prediction within tested fused block-scaled regimes, and on real matmul tiles from four architecture families the $Φ$-optimal realization falls in the fp8 low-error region. Across two $\sim$70B models on real deep_gemm kernels, the same realization removes 10 to 55% of classic Strassen's excess NLL over the clean model. Algorithm realization thus becomes a mathematically certified design problem rather than a tuning choice: an independent low-precision axis with a global $Φ$ optimum and measured fp8 relevance.

</details>


### [289] [Margin-Drop Coordinates for Cross-Budget Robustness Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26081)
*Yanliang Huang,Zhen Zhang,Peng Xie,Wenyuan Wu,Sitong Zhu,Zhuoqi Zeng,Amr Alanwar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 浅层固定预算鲁棒性评估无法预测深层攻击下的崩溃，但通过将干净样本的margin-drop分解为干净margin松弛、一步攻击缺口与漂移三个归一化坐标，用浅层漂移中位数排序可将PGD-10到PGD-200崩溃的Spearman相关从-0.006提升到+0.811。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定预算的鲁棒性评估可能选出错误的冻结视觉编码器：在浅层攻击下表现鲁棒的编码器，在同等评估被加强后可能丧失大部分鲁棒性。作者希望探究浅层评估是否包含足够信息来识别这种"预算脆弱性"。

Method: 对每个干净且分类正确的样本，记录干净成对margin、一阶线性化margin下降尺度、干净起点一步攻击的margin下降，以及迭代攻击达到的下降。以该尺度归一化后得到三个margin-drop坐标：干净margin松弛、一步缺口、漂移（迭代攻击超出一步扰动的额外归一化下降）。三者可重构归一化攻击后margin，从而决定通过与否。

Result: 在42个预训练冻结视觉编码器上，浅层存活率对后续PGD-10到PGD-200崩溃几乎无排序信息（Spearman仅-0.006），而浅层漂移中位数排序达到+0.811。结论在留出编码器池和ℓ∞评估下依然成立。在限定11个编码器的深度评估中，按浅层漂移排序可识别17个高崩溃编码器中的11个，而按存活率排序仅识别5个。完整坐标分解还能区分固定预算残差相同但在更深预算下分化的情形，并在干预下将margin修复与漂移修复分离，揭示仅靠端点鲁棒性无法识别的不同修复路径。

Conclusion: 浅层固定预算鲁棒性评估本身不足以判断编码器的深层鲁棒性，但浅层评估所记录的margin-drop坐标（尤其是漂移）能有效预测深层崩溃，并可区分不同的鲁棒性修复机制。

Abstract: Fixed-budget robustness evaluation can select the wrong frozen vision encoder. An encoder that survives a shallow attack may lose most of that robustness when the same evaluation is strengthened. We ask whether the shallow evaluation contains enough information to identify this budget fragility. For each clean-correct sample, the evaluation records the clean pairwise margin, the first-order linearized margin-drop scale, the margin drop from a clean-start one-step attack, and the drop reached by an iterative attack. Normalizing by that scale gives three margin-drop coordinates capturing clean margin slack, one-step shortfall, and drift, where drift is the additional normalized margin drop the iterative attack reaches beyond the one-step perturbation. Together, they reconstruct the normalized post-attack margin and therefore the pass-or-fail outcome. Across 42 pretrained frozen vision encoders, the shallow survival rate carries essentially no rank information about subsequent PGD-10 to PGD-200 collapse, at Spearman -0.006, while the median shallow drift coordinate ranks the same collapse at +0.811. The result persists in a held-out encoder pool and under an $\ell_\infty$ evaluation. With deep evaluation limited to 11 encoders, ranking by shallow drift recovers 11 of the 17 high-collapse encoders, compared with 5 under survival-rate ranking. The full coordinate decomposition further distinguishes cases that share the same fixed-budget residual but diverge at deeper budgets, and separates margin repair from drift repair under interventions, revealing distinct repair paths that endpoint robustness alone does not identify.

</details>


### [290] [CoEvo: Oracle-Grounded Self-Evolution of a Single Model for Multi-Step Causal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26094)
*Jian Zhang,Bingyi Wang,Yizhi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CoEvo，一个基于"oracle"验证的自我进化框架，让单个模型在出题者与解题者间交替，通过组内辩论暴露分歧步骤并由oracle裁决形成过程级监督，从而在无教师蒸馏条件下提升多步因果推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多步因果推理需要逐步约束，早期错误会悄然传播，即使答案正确也可能逻辑有误。在专业领域中，教师LLM会在中间步骤出错，安全约束限制了云端蒸馏，且条件不断变化，因此自我进化成为实际可行的路径。但朴素自我进化会崩溃：仅靠结果奖励会让模型利用分布捷径，弱自我评估会把虚假路径固化为稳定失败模式。

Method: 利用关键不对称性——生成正确链条难，但验证单步容易。许多高风险领域存在确定性的可查询oracle（如物理模拟器或规则引擎），可在不具教师级能力的情况下检查断言步骤，并超出规则时弃权。基于此提出CoEvo：单个模型在Proposer与Solver间交替。作为Solver生成竞争性链条，组内辩论暴露分歧步骤（能力边界的代理），oracle裁决形成过程级监督；作为Proposer在oracle约束内构建渐难场景，推动课程走向深层多跳链。两个角色联合更新，训练压力与模型共同进化。

Result: 在工业、临床和法律多步因果推理基准上，CoEvo使8B LLM持续自我进化，超越蒸馏基线及最强专有参考模型，路径正确率达82.1%（对比71.4%）。训练后的模型可泛化到未见类别与系统，保持根因准确率。

Conclusion: 借助确定性oracle提供的过程级监督，单个模型可通过出题者-解题者协同进化实现稳定的自我提升，克服结果奖励捷径与弱自评问题，并在专业领域多步因果推理中取得优于蒸馏与专有基线的效果与泛化能力。

Abstract: Multi-step causal reasoning requires chaining inferences where each step constrains the next. An early error propagates silently, and a correct answer reached via flawed logic evades outcome-level detection. In specialized domains, teacher LLMs err on intermediate steps, safety constraints restrict cloud distillation, and shifting conditions demand adaptation, leaving self-evolution as the practical route. Naive self-evolution can collapse: outcome-only rewards let the model exploit distributional shortcuts, and weak self-evaluation reinforces spurious paths into stable failure patterns. We exploit a key asymmetry: generating a correct chain is hard, but verifying a single step is easy. Many high-stakes domains admit a deterministic, queryable oracle, a physics simulator or rule engine over codified constraints. It checks asserted steps without teacher-level ability and abstains beyond its rules; it can check what the model asserts, never replace it. This enables CoEvo, an oracle-grounded self-evolution framework where a single model alternates between Proposer and Solver. As Solver, the model generates competing chains; intra-group debate exposes disagreement steps, a proxy for the capability boundary, and the oracle adjudicates them into process-level supervision. As Proposer, the same model constructs progressively harder scenarios inside oracle constraints, steering the curriculum toward deep multi-hop chains. Both roles are updated jointly, so training pressure co-evolves with the model. On industrial, clinical, and legal multi-step causal reasoning benchmarks, CoEvo enables an 8B LLM to sustain self-evolution, surpassing distillation baselines and the strongest proprietary reference on path correctness (82.1% vs. 71.4%). The trained model generalizes to unseen categories and systems, preserving root-cause accuracy.

</details>


### [291] [Certified Mechanistic Interpretability: Lifting Single-Input Findings to Bounded Neighbourhoods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26112)
*Zhen Zhang,Yanliang Huang,Peng Xie,Wenyuan Wu,Amr Alanwar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于受限多项式-zonotope（CPZ）传播的框架，将机械可解释性从单输入观察提升为有界扰动集合上的可证明陈述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机械可解释性方法逐输入逆向分析transformer电路，仅能给出单点观察，无法在输入的有界邻域内提供保证。

Method: 采用受限多项式-zonotope（CPZ）传播；将三个内部注意力查询（top-k稳定性、证据质量、注意力熵）表述为注意力权重单纯形上的可处理程序；证明CPZ通过transformer块时能精确保持softmax单纯形与LayerNorm零均值恒等式；通过递归Jacobian zonotope构造将证书扩展至多层深度，对块堆叠在输入处线性化并避免逐层生成器增长。

Result: 在transformer注意力上实例化该框架，所得证书可强化单输入检查无法解决的可解释性陈述。

Conclusion: 该框架为机械可解释性提供有界邻域内的认证保证，可指导经验启发式可能产生误导的下游决策。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability reverse-engineers transformer circuits one input at a time, leaving observed mechanisms without guarantees over bounded input neighbourhoods. We address this gap with a framework based on constrained polynomial-zonotope (CPZ) propagation that lifts mechanistic-interpretability observations from a single input to certified statements over a bounded set of perturbations. Three internal-attention queries (top-$k$ stability, evidence mass, and attention entropy) are formulated as tractable programs over the simplex of attention weights, and CPZ propagation through transformer blocks is shown to preserve the softmax simplex and the LayerNorm zero-mean identity exactly. A recursive Jacobian zonotope construction extends the same certificates across layer depth by linearising the block stack at the input and avoids per-layer generator growth. We instantiate the framework on transformer attention; the resulting certificates offer a way to sharpen mechanistic statements that single-input inspection cannot resolve on its own, and to inform downstream decisions in regimes where empirical heuristics may be misleading.

</details>


### [292] [From Risk Scoring to Risk Allocation: A Density-Driven Framework for Diverse Monitoring in Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26146)
*Zhaohui Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将多智能体系统风险监控从“逐状态打分选Top-K”范式转为“风险分配（组合子集选择）”，提出“拥挤悖论”：密度p(x)而非异常分才是有效风险信号（P(risk|x)∝p(x)），并将监控建模为带λ风险—多样性权衡的QUBO问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统常用的逐状态风险打分+Top-K选择在“拥挤”情形下（大量智能体共享相同脆弱性）会选出风险高度相关、彼此冗余的告警，即“监控中的羊群效应”，导致监控失效。

Method: 1）识别Crowding Paradox：P(risk|x)∝p(x)而非1/p(x)，用密度而非异常分作为脆弱性信号；2）在密度脆弱性分数基础上，把监控重铸为相互依赖状态上的组合子集选择，映射为带λ控制风险—多样性权衡的QUBO目标；3）用学习到的λ策略逼近oracle网格搜索。

Result: 密度评分在金融数据5d/10d/20d崩盘窗口AUROC≥0.94，而五个异常基线均<0.80；Pareto前沿包含MMR、k-DPP等固定工作点；相对贪心增益随规模单调上升（n=15时+24%，n=200时+66%）；学习λ策略达到oracle网格搜索目标的99.5%；方法可迁移到交通与多智能体强化学习；QUBO实例可原样在Rigetti超导QPUs上运行（Ankaa-3、Cepheus-1-108Q，经Amazon Braket）。

Conclusion: 监控应从风险打分转向风险分配；密度作为风险信号更有效，QUBO组合优化能刻画风险—多样性权衡并随规模扩大获得更大增益；量子硬件执行仅作为该形式的兼容性性质报告，而非在现有规模上声称量子优势。

Abstract: Risk monitoring in multi-agent systems is commonly built on a per-state primitive that scores each state independently and selects the top K. Under crowding, where many agents share the same fragility, this approach picks redundant alerts whose risks are jointly correlated, a pattern we describe as ``herding in monitoring.'' We propose a paradigm shift from risk scoring to risk allocation, supported by two contributions. First, we identify the Crowding Paradox, namely that P(risk | x) $\propto$ p(x) rather than 1/p(x), so density rather than anomaly score is the operative risk signal; on financial data, density-based scoring reaches AUROC $\geq$ 0.94 at 5d/10d/20d crash horizons, while five anomaly baselines all fall below 0.80. Second, given a density-derived fragility score, we recast monitoring as combinatorial subset selection over interdependent states and map it to a QUBO objective with a $λ$-controlled risk--diversity tradeoff. The resulting Pareto frontier contains standard diverse-subset methods (MMR, k-DPP) as fixed operating points; the gain over greedy grows monotonically with scale, from +24% at n=15 to +66% at n=200; a learned $λ$ policy reaches 99.5% of an oracle grid-search objective; and the formulation transfers to traffic and multi-agent reinforcement learning. The same QUBO instances execute without modification on Rigetti superconducting QPUs (Ankaa-3 and Cepheus-1-108Q via Amazon Braket), which we report as a compatibility property of the formulation rather than a claim of quantum advantage at this scale.

</details>


### [293] [Block-Level Weight-Space Structure Persists Under Post-Training: An Empirical Study Across LLM Families](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26147)
*Zhaohui Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文实证研究了后训练如何改变大模型权重空间几何：后训练会扰动每个张量，但保留块级结构；基于此提出LinkerLLM惰性加载器，通过共享块实现显存节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代LLM以从共享预训练权重派生的后训练变体族形式部署（base、instruct、chat、code）。作者希望理解后训练如何改变权重空间几何，从而利用这种结构特性来降低多变体共驻时的显存开销。

Method: 在四个架构族（Qwen2.5、Llama-3.1/3.2、Mistral、Gemma-2）共八个配置上做实证研究，比较张量级与块级相似度（余弦相似度、相对Frobenius距离），并与独立训练的特化模型对比。基于发现构建LinkerLLM——一个跨共驻变体别名共享块的惰性加载器。

Result: 发现"粒度差距"：291-339个张量中无一字节完全相同（哈希去重0%节省），但块级结构被保留（平均余弦相似度>0.99，相对Frobenius距离<0.13）。独立训练的特化模型（如Qwen2.5-Coder）与通用base余弦相似度仅约0.64，处于不连通区域。扰动幅度随模型规模、架构族与后训练配方系统性变化。LinkerLLM实现18-48%显存节省，可在单张24GB消费级GPU上运行最多五个7B变体；八个配置中五个在MMLU、ARC-Challenge、HellaSwag、WinoGrande上保留≥94%质量，其余三个各有一个基准低于阈值（87-91%）。

Conclusion: 后训练表现为结构化扰动：改变每个参数但保持块级几何不变，该性质随训练来源不同而失效。块共享是可在不显著损失质量前提下节省显存的有效实用技术，但需透明报告个别低于阈值的基准，而非以单阈值决定共享决策。

Abstract: Modern LLMs are deployed as families of post-trained variants (base, instruct, chat, code) derived from a shared set of pre-trained weights. We present an empirical study of how post-training transforms weight-space geometry, covering eight configurations across four architecture families (Qwen2.5, Llama-3.1/3.2, Mistral, Gemma-2). We identify a granularity gap: post-training modifies every tensor (zero of 291-339 tensors remain byte-identical, so hash-based deduplication achieves 0% savings), yet preserves block-level structure (mean cosine similarity exceeds 0.99 and relative Frobenius distance stays below 0.13). Post-training therefore acts as a structured perturbation that shifts every parameter while leaving block-level geometry intact. The property is not universal: independently trained specializations (for example, Qwen2.5-Coder) attain cosine similarity around 0.64 with the general base, indicating a disconnected region of weight space. Perturbation magnitude varies systematically with model scale, architecture family, and post-training recipe. As a practical application, we build LinkerLLM, a lazy loader that aliases shareable blocks across co-resident variants, achieving 18-48% GPU memory savings and enabling up to five 7B-parameter variants on a single 24 GB consumer GPU. Five of eight configurations retain at least 94% of the unshared variant's quality on MMLU, ARC-Challenge, HellaSwag, and WinoGrande; the remaining three (Mistral-7B, Gemma-2-2B, Llama-3.2-1B) have one below-threshold benchmark each (87-91%), which we report transparently rather than gate the block-sharing decision on a single threshold.

</details>


### [294] [Spectral Tail Interventions in Decoder-Only Language Models: Reasoning-Sensitive Weight Structure from Controlled Surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26165)
*Ibne Farabi Shihab,Sanjida Akhter,Md Najmus Swaqeeb,Abu Sa-Adat Mohamed Moon-Im Al Ahsan,Anuj Sharma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过受控干预研究解码器 Transformer 中集中的上谱尾部，建立其与语言模型行为的计算关联，而非仅停留在相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 权重空间结构与语言模型行为常相关，但相关性不足以证明计算参与。本文旨在通过受控干预建立计算层面的因果联系。

Method: 在固定相对偏移下推导有限宽度条件界，将平方奇异值的逆参与率与中心 pre-softmax logit 峰度关联；定义逐点 QK 乘积尾部目标，对比独立因子手术与保留原生注意力计算的乘积目标分解；在三个基础检查点、五个推理基准及一个指令微调 Phi 检查点上验证，并使用五个固定谱匹配 Haar 对照；另进行逆参与率与准确率转变的研究及尾部感知 LoRA。

Result: 在全部 20 个模型-任务单元中，学习尾部编辑比五个固定谱匹配 Haar 对照均值更具破坏性；Holm 校正后 18 个配对对比显著，2 个方向性但不结论；乘积目标因子获得更高的留出尾部子空间比例；组件隔离识别出 QK、value-output 和 MLP 块贡献；逆参与先于准确率转变，尾部感知 LoRA 比标准 LoRA 和 PiSSA 更早达标但最终分数区间重叠。

Conclusion: 结论仅限所评估的检查点、层、任务、干预与对照，为上谱尾部涉及语言模型计算提供了受控证据。

Abstract: Weight-space structure often correlates with language-model behavior, but correlation alone does not establish computational involvement. We study concentrated upper spectral tails in decoder-only transformers through controlled interventions. At a fixed relative offset, we derive a finite-width conditional bound linking the inverse participation ratio of squared singular values to central pre-softmax logit kurtosis. We then define a pointwise query--key ($QK$) product-tail target and compare independent factor surgery with a product-targeted factorization that preserves native attention computation. Across three base checkpoints and five reasoning benchmarks, plus an instruction-tuned Phi checkpoint analyzed separately, the learned-tail edit is more damaging than the mean of five fixed spectrum-matched Haar controls in all 20 model--task cells. Eighteen paired contrasts remain significant after Holm correction, while two are directional but inconclusive. Product-targeted factors attain higher held-out tail-subspace fractions, providing an empirical bridge between product- and factor-level interventions. Component isolation identifies contributions from $QK$, value--output, and multilayer-perceptron blocks, although the theorem covers only $QK$. In separate studies, inverse participation precedes pooled accuracy transitions under a matched crossing rule, and residualized tail-aware low-rank adaptation (LoRA) reaches targets earlier than standard LoRA and PiSSA while final-score intervals overlap. Conclusions are restricted to the evaluated checkpoints, layers, tasks, interventions, and controls.

</details>


### [295] [Activation-Energy Pruning for Spiking Neural Networks: Unsupervised Personalization via Spike-Count Saliency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26167)
*Joseph Bingham*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将面向常规DNN的"激活能剪枝"准则迁移到脉冲神经网络（SNN），发现梯度类剪枝在SNN上严重失效，而激活能剪枝在高稀疏度下反而优于源模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在常规深度网络中，激活能剪枝（去除"权重大小×突触前脉冲累计计数"低于阈值的权重）已被证明是有效的无监督个性化策略。作者想探究：在SNN中，激活能不再只是启发式指标，而是与每个突触代谢成本成正比的物理量，同一准则是否仍然有效。

Method: 将激活能剪枝准则应用于脉冲神经网络，并与梯度类剪枝方法（SNIP、GraSP、幅度剪枝）在多种架构和数据集上进行系统对比，考察不同稀疏度下的表现；同时在神经形态基准（N-MNIST）上测试激活能剪枝的效果。

Result: 第一，梯度类剪枝在SNN上持续劣于常规网络，在σ=0.2稀疏度时所有测试架构与数据集均退化至接近随机猜测的准确率，作者将其归因于代理梯度显著性估计与二值脉冲序列表示之间的系统性不兼容，但不排除替代代理梯度或超参设置可部分缓解。第二，激活能剪枝在神经形态基准上于高稀疏度下反而优于源模型（N-MNIST上σ=0.8时98.4±0.4% vs. 97.2±0.7%），这一现象在常规网络中没有对应。

Conclusion: 作者将该提升现象解释为与经验依赖的皮层特化相一致：去除仅对非目标类别活跃的连接可能降低跨类干扰、形成更干净的目标表示；但强调这只是解释性类比，而非机制性证明。整体而言，SNN中的激活能剪枝与梯度剪枝呈现与常规网络截然不同的行为。

Abstract: Activation-energy pruning -- removing weights whose product of magnitude and cumulative pre-synaptic spike count falls below a threshold -- was established as an effective unsupervised personalization strategy for conventional deep neural networks~\citep{BINGHAM2025101242}. This paper asks what happens when the same criterion is applied to spiking neural networks (SNNs), where activation energy is not merely a useful heuristic but a literal physical quantity proportional to the metabolic cost of each synapse. The answer is surprising on three counts.
  First, gradient-based pruning methods that perform competitively on conventional networks (SNIP, GraSP, magnitude pruning) consistently underperform on SNNs, collapsing to near-chance accuracy by $σ= 0.2$ sparsity across all tested architectures and datasets. We trace this to a systematic incompatibility between surrogate-gradient saliency estimation and the binary spike-train representation, though we cannot rule out that alternative surrogate choices or hyperparameter settings might partially mitigate the effect.
  Second, activation-energy pruning applied to a neuromorphic benchmark \emph{improves} over the source model at high sparsity ($98.4 \pm 0.4\%$ vs.\ $97.2 \pm 0.7\%$ at $σ= 0.8$ on N-MNIST), a phenomenon with no counterpart in the conventional network setting. We interpret this result as consistent with experience-dependent cortical specialisation: removing connections active only for non-target classes may reduce cross-class interference and produce a cleaner target representation, though we note this is an interpretive analogy rather than a mechanistic demonstration.

</details>


### [296] [Component Type, Not Reconstruction Error, Predicts Attention Quantization Sensitivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26173)
*Kasun Dewage,Marianna Pensky,Suranadi De Silva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过9个开源语言模型、3808次测量，实证检验注意力投影量化中重建误差能否预测下游功能影响，发现重建误差解释力弱，投影类型与层身份更重要，V投影最敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多训练后量化（PTQ）方法基于逐层重建、二阶代理目标或激活感知变换来降低量化误差，但这类误差信号能否预测量化单个注意力投影的下游功能影响，此前未被直接刻画。本文旨在实证检验重建误差作为敏感性指标的可靠性。

Method: 扫描9个开源权重语言模型（1.3B–8B；OPT、GPT-J、LLaMA-1/2/3、Mistral、Qwen 2.5），在RTN下逐次量化单个注意力投影，并对其中7个模型在3-bit和4-bit下使用GPTQ；记录重建误差、困惑度变化及各投影的激活加权量化误差，共3808次测量。

Result: (1) RTN下同组件类型内（Q/K/V/O）重建误差仅解释困惑度敏感性方差不足10%，36例中27例如此，中位R²=0.044；(2) 组件类型与层身份解释的方差均超过重建误差，9个模型中7个以层身份为最强预测因子，2个以组件类型最强；(3) V投影最常成为主导组件，9个模型中7个其占总正向ΔPPL的38–51%；(4) RTN与GPTQ间主导组件大体保持一致（7例中5例）；(5) 对V投影，激活加权量化误差作为组件内预测因子略优于重建误差（中位R²为0.20 vs 0.06）。

Conclusion: 仅凭相对权重重建误差不足以支持敏感性感知的比特分配，V投影在混合精度方案中值得专门考虑；组件类型与层身份应被纳入敏感性判断依据。

Abstract: Many post-training quantization (PTQ) methods use layer-wise reconstruction, second-order proxy objectives, or activation-aware transformations to reduce quantization-induced error. Whether that error signal predicts the downstream functional impact of quantizing an individual attention projection has not been directly characterized. We sweep nine open-weight language models (1.3B--8B parameters; OPT, GPT-J, LLaMA-1/2/3, Mistral, Qwen 2.5) and quantize one attention projection at a time under round-to-nearest (RTN) and, for seven models, GPTQ at 3 and 4 bits, recording reconstruction error, perplexity change, and per-projection activation-weighted quantization error for 3,808 distinct measurements. We find: (1) within a given component type (Q, K, V, or O), reconstruction error explains less than 10% of the variance in perplexity sensitivity in 27 of 36 cases under RTN, with median R^2 = 0.044; (2) both component type and layer identity explain more variance than reconstruction error in all 9 models, with layer identity the strongest predictor in 7 of 9 models and component type strongest in the remaining 2; (3) value (V) projections are the most commonly dominant component, accounting for 38--51% of total positive Delta PPL in seven of nine models; (4) the dominant component is broadly preserved between RTN and GPTQ (5 of 7 cases); and (5) activation-weighted quantization error is a moderately better within-component predictor than reconstruction error for V projections specifically (median R^2 of 0.20 vs. 0.06). These findings indicate that relative weight reconstruction error alone is insufficient for sensitivity-aware bit allocation, and that V projections merit dedicated consideration in mixed-precision schemes.

</details>


### [297] [Partially Observed Sparse Graphs: The Unknown Sampling Rate is a Tail Index](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26199)
*Jian Xu,Delu Zeng,John Paisley,Qibin Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究在采样率s未知但总体规模已知时稀疏图的全局边数估计问题，证明其等价于尾指数估计，并给出模块化估计器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大图常只能获取部分（如爬取预算停止、面板、部分转储）。当采样比例s由设计已知时可用\hat e=e_s/s^2估计总边数，但s未知时该法失效。作者希望在s未知、总体规模已知的情形下估计全局边基数。

Method: 在稀疏可交换图（graphex）模型下，推导非孤立节点比例满足n_s/n_1→s^{1+σ}，从而可由尾指数σ估计采样率，代回得估计量e_s(n_1/n_s)^{2/(1+σ)}；并界定替代法的有限规模误差，证明归约对尾指数估计器具有模块性，用已发表的闭式估计器进行实证。

Result: σ=0时即二次graphon估计器，其260%中位误差对27%，因等式失败而非拟合失败；替代法配合闭式尾指数估计器在13个网络、39个采样预算上达到21.7%误差且无需拟合；拟合完整graphex还可返回任意规模的度分布与生成对象。两类极限精确：秩一graphex的传递性由度分布决定，高聚类图不在类内；在雪球或随机游走爬取下所有方法均失效（7.8%至588%）。

Conclusion: 在稀疏图中估计全局边基数在期望意义上等价于尾指数估计；该归约模块化、可零拟合应用，并揭示二次graphon估计器是σ=0特例及其失败机理，同时界定方法的适用与失效边界。

Abstract: A large graph is often available only in part: a crawl stopped by its budget, a panel, a partial dump. When the sampled fraction $s$ is known by design the total edge count follows from $\hat e=e_s/s^2$ and no model is needed. We treat the case where $s$ is unknown and the population size is known. Our main result is a reduction: under a sparse exchangeable (graphex) model the expected non-isolated fraction obeys $n_s/n_1\to s^{1+σ}$, so the sampling rate becomes estimable once the tail index $σ$ is, and substituting it back gives $e_s(n_1/n_s)^{2/(1+σ)}$ -- the same estimator, with the design quantity inferred. Estimating global edge cardinality in a sparse graph is therefore, in expectation, tail-index estimation, and the quadratic graphon estimator is the case $σ=0$: it fails by an identity rather than by a fit ($260\%$ median error against $27\%$). We bound the finite-size error of the substitution and show the reduction is \emph{modular} in the tail-index estimator --- filled with a published closed-form one it reaches $21.7\%$ over $13$ networks and $39$ sampling budgets with no fitting at all. Fitting a full graphex additionally returns the degree distribution at any size and a generative object, in a representation where sparsity is a coordinate and the interpolation path is dictated rather than chosen. Two limits are exact: rank-one graphexes have transitivity fixed by the degree profile, so high-clustering graphs lie outside the class; and under snowball or random-walk crawls every method here fails, the design-based oracle worst of all ($7.8\%$ to $588\%$).

</details>


### [298] [Bridging the Data Gap: Digital Twin as a New Paradigm for AI-based Radio Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26214)
*Éloi Sainte-Beuve,Guillaume Larue,Louis-Adrien Dufrène,Quentin Lampin,Ali Al Khansa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出以3D数字孪生驱动射线追踪生成信道冲激响应序列，训练序列神经网络实现无设备定位，并主张将感知视为环境条件化的学习问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现大规模无线电感知通常依赖大量真实数据与带标签目标，成本高且难以覆盖多样场景。借助数字孪生作为世界模型，可低成本生成大规模、可控的合成数据，从而绕开无源目标仪器的限制。

Method: 以环境3D数字孪生为核心，提供几何、材质与收发机布局，交由射线追踪引擎生成大量合理场景（人员与物体移动、布局变化、季节天气等）下的时间序列信道冲激响应（CIR）。利用这些合成序列训练序列神经网络，将CIR时间序列映射为空间占用估计，实现无设备定位。并提出将感知视为环境条件化问题，训练或微调针对特定站点数字孪生的局部模型，而非单一全局模型。

Result: 作为首个实验，引入了新的状态空间模型（SSM）架构，并在多种房间几何结构上训练与评估，定位性能验证了该方法的潜力。

Conclusion: 数字孪生可支撑大规模无线电感知与无设备定位，环境条件化的局部模型思路具有可行性，后续可扩展至更多场景与任务。

Abstract: We present a methodology that places a 3D digital twin (DT) of the environment as the main enabler behind the development of radio sensing at scale. The DT acts as a world model, providing geometry, materials, and transmitter/receiver placements to a ray-tracing engine that generates time-indexed channel impulse responses (CIRs) for large numbers of plausible scenes (moving people and objects, layout variants, seasonal/weather conditions, etc). From these synthetic sequences, we train a sequential neural network that maps CIR time series to spatial occupancy estimates, enabling device-free localization (DFL) without instrumented targets. We posit that sensing is best approached as an environment-conditioned learning problem: rather than seeking a single global model, we advocate training or fine-tuning local models specialized to a site-specific DT. As a first experiment, we introduce a novel State Space Model architecture, trained and evaluated across multiple room geometries. The localization performances obtained demonstrate the potential of the approach.

</details>


### [299] [Beyond Imitation: Auditing the Recoverability of Reasoning in Distilled Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26216)
*Ruitong Li,Binjie Guo,Aisheng Mo,Guowei Su,Han Wang,Jie Li,Ru Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出"前缀恢复"（prefix recovery）作为评估知识蒸馏中师生兼容性的诊断指标，并将其与交叉熵和反向KL梯度间的余弦冲突联系起来，揭示了蒸馏效果依赖于学生容量的规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正确的教师解答只有在学生能够继续其推理时才能成为有效监督。作者希望找到一个可量化的指标来衡量这种师生兼容性，从而指导成本高昂的分布级蒸馏方法的选择。

Method: 提出前缀恢复指标：在展示已验证解答的25%、50%或75%后，测试学生能否正确完成剩余部分。同时计算交叉熵与反向KL梯度在整个词表上的余弦冲突。实验使用Qwen3相邻规模的师生配对（0.6B至8B参数），并采用独立的目标准则干预进行验证。

Result: 反向KL蒸馏在2B以下的两个学生上取得最一致的数学与代码提升。在1000条轨迹的固定队列上，学生规模从0.6B增至4B时，平均前缀恢复率由71.0%升至91.9%，鲁棒-脆弱恢复差距由46.0个百分点缩小至14.4个百分点。教师固定为8B时，冲突分离度从0.993降至0.233。目标准则干预在脆弱轨迹上表现出最大的反向KL挽救效果。

Conclusion: 三项测量共同指向同一容量依赖的迁移机制：当正确轨迹的恢复性不均时，分布匹配具有最大的提升空间。前缀恢复为选择高成本的分布级蒸馏提供了实用诊断工具。

Abstract: A correct teacher solution becomes useful supervision when the receiving student can continue its reasoning. We measure this compatibility with prefix recovery: after revealing 25%, 50%, or 75% of a verified solution, we test whether the student completes it correctly. We connect recovery to the cosine conflict between cross-entropy and reverse-KL gradients over the full vocabulary. Across adjacent Qwen3 teacher-student pairs from 0.6B to 8B parameters, reverse-KL distillation delivers its most consistent mathematical and code improvements for the two students below 2B parameters. On a fixed cohort of 1,000 trajectories, average prefix recovery rises from 71.0% to 91.9% as student size increases from 0.6B to 4B, and the robust-fragile recovery gap contracts from 46.0 to 14.4 percentage points. With the teacher fixed at 8B, conflict separation falls from 0.993 to 0.233. An independent objective intervention finds the largest reverse-KL rescue on fragile trajectories. The three measurements locate the same capacity-dependent transfer regime: distribution matching has the greatest headroom when correct traces remain unevenly recoverable. Prefix recovery provides a practical diagnostic for selecting costly distribution-level distillation.

</details>


### [300] [MSA-CITE: A Co-Adapted LoRA Specialist Ecology for Fixed-Budget Small-Model Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26217)
*Ruitong Li,Binjie Guo,Aisheng Mo,Guowei Su,Jie Li,Ru Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将后训练中被丢弃的多个检查点（LoRA分支）作为可组合资源部署：每个分支各生成一次，用校准先验对等价答案类打分并选择代表，在数学基准上取得小幅度但一致的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧凑语言模型通常只保留单一后训练检查点并重复采样，这种做法浪费了后训练过程中产生的其他检查点。作者希望验证：来自不同后训练轨迹的多个（原本被丢弃的）检查点，即使没有联合训练，是否也能作为可组合的部署资产共同带来收益。

Method: 以单一Qwen3-4B为骨干，保留四个冻结的LoRA分支，每个分支来自不同的后训练轨迹。在固定的四次生成预算下，从每个分支各采样一个完成结果（而非从单一分支采样四次）。提出MSA-CITE：将各分支的最终答案归入等价类，用校准得到的来源先验求和为每个类打分，并在确定性平局规则下选出代表答案。读出阶段不利用评测结果学习，也不额外引入生成、验证器或重排序步骤。

Result: 在200道留出数学题上，四路径组合达到65.5%准确率，最强单分支基线为62.0%；在100题学科不相交的分布偏移上为42.0%对40.0%。在分布内条件下，相对同质SFT和Online-OPD重复的改进是稳健的；但相对最强基线的结果以及在偏移条件下并不具结论性。

Conclusion: 后训练分支即使未经联合训练，也可以在部署时被集体利用并带来收益，这是一个范围有限但具体的贡献；但在更强基线和分布偏移下的优势尚不确定，仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Compact language models are typically deployed by retaining a single post-training checkpoint and sampling it repeatedly. In this work, we challenge this practice by treating multiple discarded checkpoints as composable assets for deployment. Starting from a single Qwen3-4B backbone, we preserve four frozen LoRA branches, each derived from a different post-training trajectory. stead of drawing four generations from one branch, we allocate a fixed four-generation budget by sampling one completion from each branch. Our method, Multi-path Specialist Adaptation with Calibrated Inference-Time Evidence (MSA-CITE), processes the resulting portfolio by grouping terminal answers into equivalence classes, scoring each class via summed calibration-derived source priors, and selecting a representative under deterministic tie-breaking rules. The readout stage does not learn from evaluation results, nor does it introduce additional generations, verifiers, or reranking steps. On 200 held-out mathematics items, the four-path portfolio achieves 65.5% accuracy, compared with 62.0% for the strongest single-branch baseline. On a 100-item subject-disjoint shift, it attains 42.0% versus 40.0%. Under in-distribution conditions, the improvements over homogeneous SFT and Online-OPD repetition are robust; results against the strongest baseline and under shifted conditions are not conclusive. Our findings offer a narrow but concrete contribution: post-training branches, even without co-training, can be collectively beneficial for deployment.

</details>


### [301] [High-Order Liquid Evidence Modeling for Continuous and Subtle GNSS Spoofing Detection in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26231)
*Muhammad Ayub Sabir,Junbiao Pang,Fatima Ashraf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将持续且隐蔽的GNSS欺骗检测建模为因果序列证据建模问题，提出高阶液态证据检测器（HO-LLN），在AV-GPS数据集上取得AUROC 0.9932、AUPRC 0.9843的优异表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续隐蔽的GNSS欺骗对自动驾驶汽车构成严重威胁：伪造位置在局部看似合理，却逐渐与非GNSS车载传感器观测的运动不一致。现有检测器多依赖残差阈值或特征级分类，对微弱"GNSS-运动不一致性"如何随时间发展与持续缺乏建模。

Method: 将欺骗检测表述为因果序列证据建模问题。首先比较连续GNSS位置位移与车载运动观测推断位移之差，转化为经不确定性归一化的残差证据；再以因果弱证据表示当前不一致性、局部演化、超常水平、累积持续性及位移有效性；将这些线索映射为瞬时、演化、持续潜状态，经有界Kirchhoff式对称交换对齐，并通过显式三阶交互组合；最后用二阶液态动态追踪其记忆与演化以估计因果欺骗概率，经阈值与持续参数转化为确认告警。

Result: 在AV-GPS数据集族上展现出强受控与外推泛化能力及清晰的序列告警行为。Dataset-1上AUROC为0.9932，AUPRC为0.9843，并在基于学习的基线中取得最低假阳性率。

Conclusion: 该高阶液态证据检测器通过显式建模隐蔽欺骗证据的时序演化与协同支持，有效提升了自动驾驶场景下隐蔽GNSS欺骗检测的性能，并具备良好的泛化性与低误报特性。

Abstract: Continuous and subtle GNSS spoofing poses a serious threat to autonomous vehicles because forged positions may remain locally plausible while gradually becoming inconsistent with vehicle motion observed by non-GNSS onboard sensors. Existing AV-oriented detectors commonly rely on residual thresholds or feature-level classification and provide limited modeling of how weak GNSS--motion inconsistency develops and persists over time. This paper formulates subtle GNSS spoofing detection as a causal sequential evidence-modeling problem and proposes a high-order liquid evidence detector. The method first compares the displacement implied by consecutive GNSS positions with that inferred from independent onboard motion observations and converts their difference into uncertainty-normalized residual evidence. It then represents the current inconsistency, its local evolution, excess above the normal level, accumulated persistence, and displacement validity as causal weak evidence. These cues are mapped into instantaneous, evolutionary, and persistent latent states, aligned through a bounded Kirchhoff-inspired symmetric exchange, and combined through an explicit third-order interaction to capture their coordinated support for spoofing. To model how this coordinated evidence develops over time, second-order liquid dynamics track its memory and evolution to estimate causal spoofing probabilities, which are converted into confirmed alarms using validation-selected threshold and persistence parameters. Experiments on the AV--GPS dataset family demonstrate strong controlled and external generalization, together with clear sequential alarm behavior. On Dataset-1, the proposed detector achieves an AUROC of 0.9932 and an AUPRC of 0.9843, while obtaining the lowest false-positive rate among the learning-based baselines. Code: https://github.com/pangjunbiao/HO-LLN-Spoofing.

</details>


### [302] [Can You Delete a Year of Market Data? Machine Unlearning Against Exact Retraining Oracles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26242)
*Junyi Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文对时间序列预测模型中的机器遗忘进行基准测试，提出应以"未经被删时间段训练的oracle模型"为近似遗忘目标，而非追求被删期的低预测精度；仅TSMixer+hinge方法在全部滚动折中稳定贴近oracle。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据许可到期后，仅删除存储记录无法消除已训练预测器中编码的影响，而重训练成本高昂。现有机器遗忘研究缺乏针对时间序列、特别是存在制度性记忆（如危机年份）场景的系统基准，也缺少明确的评估目标。

Method: 在S&P 500波动率面板上构建3,200组配对参照（全数据训练）与oracle（剔除指定时间段重训练）。网格覆盖5种架构、4个滚动折、5个可删年份、3种删除强度；比较部署型与非部署型模型，并评估多种遗忘方法与审计统计（含窗口级损失比较与独立性假设检验）。

Result: 2020年COVID危机年对所有架构产生最大记忆差距；删除它使三种可部署模型在所有折中均改善，2022年熊市中提升最大，而两种不可部署模型反应不一致。某Transformer单元中，未训练2020的oracle仍以IC=0.51预测该期（参照为0.55），将预测推向噪声反而降低测试能力。12组可部署架构-方法对中，仅TSMixer+hinge在每个折都接近oracle，弥合74-118%的reference-to-oracle差距且无可测测试能力损失。方法排名随架构与滚动窗口变化；审计分离度随先前记忆度上升，但精确删除后仍可能很小；窗口级损失比较最高仅0.69，将股票级窗口视为独立会使t统计量绝对值中位数被夸大1.9倍。

Conclusion: 时间序列遗忘需要明确的删除范围定义、针对特定架构与窗口的oracle验证，以及考虑统计功效的审计设计；评估应锚定oracle而非被删期低误差。

Abstract: When a data license expires, deleting stored records does not remove influence encoded in a trained forecaster. Machine unlearning seeks to remove this influence without retraining. We benchmark temporal unlearning with 3,200 paired references trained on all data and oracles retrained without the requested period. The grid covers five architectures, four rolling folds, five deletable years, and three experimental deletion levels on an S&P 500 volatility panel. The 2020 COVID crisis year produces the largest memorization gap for every architecture. Removing it improves all three deployable models in every fold, with the largest improvement in the 2022 bear market, while the two non-deployable models respond inconsistently. The target for approximate unlearning is the oracle, not low predictive accuracy on the deleted period. In one Transformer cell, an oracle that never trained on 2020 still predicts it at an information coefficient of 0.51, compared with 0.55 for the reference; pushing predictions toward noise reduces test skill. Across twelve deployable architecture-method pairs, only TSMixer with the hinge method remains near the oracle in every fold, closing 74-118% of the reference-to-oracle gap without a measurable loss of test skill. Method rankings vary across architectures and rolling windows. Audit separation rises with prior memorization but can remain small after exact deletion. The window-level loss comparison reaches at most 0.69, and treating stock-level windows as independent inflates the absolute t-statistic by a median factor of 1.9. These results call for an explicit deletion scope, oracle validation for the relevant architecture and window, and power-aware auditing.

</details>


### [303] [Information-Theoretic Decoupled Prompt Tuning for Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26257)
*Yunfei Zhang,Wen Wen,Tieliang Gong,Weizhan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 DPT4CL，将 CLIP 文本提示解耦为任务共享提示分布与类特定提示，以缓解检索依赖和分类器偏置，在持续学习基准上取得 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习需在顺序数据上增量获取知识并避免灾难性遗忘；现有提示调优范式普遍存在检索依赖和分类器偏置，导致模型适配对提示选择敏感、预测偏向新到类别。

Method: 提出解耦提示调优 DPT4CL：将 CLIP 文本提示解耦为任务共享提示分布与类特定提示。任务共享提示分布通过优化信息瓶颈目标得到，以促进跨任务知识迁移并缓解分类器偏置；类特定提示增强类间可分性且不依赖显式提示检索。同时建立统一的超额风险界。

Result: 在标准持续学习基准上的大量实验表明，DPT4CL 达到最先进性能；理论分析为其鲁棒泛化与遗忘缓解提供了支持。

Conclusion: DPT4CL 通过提示解耦与信息瓶颈优化，有效缓解检索依赖和分类器偏置，兼顾理论保障与实验性能，为持续学习中的提示调优提供了新范式。

Abstract: Continual learning (CL) aims to incrementally acquire knowledge from sequential data while avoiding catastrophic forgetting. Recently, prompt tuning has attracted increasing attention as an efficient approach for adapting pre-trained models to CL tasks. However, existing prompt design paradigms commonly suffer from retrieval dependence and classifier bias, which make model adaptation sensitive to prompt selection and bias predictions toward newly arrived classes. To address these challenges, we propose Decoupled Prompt Tuning for Continual Learning (DPT4CL), which decouples the CLIP textual prompt into a task-shared prompt distribution and class-specific prompts. The task-shared prompt distribution is derived by optimizing an Information Bottleneck objective to facilitate cross-task knowledge transfer and alleviate classifier bias, while class-specific prompts enhance inter-class separability without relying on explicit prompt retrieval. Furthermore, we establish a unified excess risk bound from an information-theoretic perspective, providing theoretical support for the robust generalization and forgetting mitigation of the proposed framework. Extensive experiments on standard CL benchmarks demonstrate that DPT4CL achieves state-of-the-art performance. The source code is available at https://github.com/Cloudfly-Z/DPT4CL

</details>


### [304] [Mode Collapse Is Cheap to Detect: A Ground-Truth-Free Pre-Flight Check for Neural Samplers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26272)
*Jian Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种低成本"预检"方法，用于检测神经采样器在训练中是否遗漏了目标分布的部分质量，仅利用能量函数及其一、二阶导数即可估计遗漏质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经采样器针对未归一化目标训练且无真实样本，常用诊断（如自归一化ESS、ELBO）都基于模型自身采样，只能局限在模型支撑集内，无法发现被遗漏的模式。作者举例说明某采样器ESS达0.99却遗漏87%的目标质量，因此需要一种能检测遗漏质量的独立诊断手段。

Method: 作者论证"检测"遗漏质量比"采样/修正"更容易：检测只需每个被遗漏盆地一个点加一个局部曲率估计，而修正需要重训。据此构造预检方法，只消耗少量训练预算，仅使用E、∇E、∇²E。方法包含一个自诊断机制，在无真实标签时估计结果保守偏向安全方向。

Result: 在高斯混合、Many-Well及旋转各向异性Many-Well（有精确可算真值）上，该检查在2.7%训练成本下估计遗漏质量误差在10⁻³内，而调好的退火SMC需70%–280%训练成本且更差。方法同样适用于无解析密度的受控SDE采样器。自诊断在60种配置中通过16种，其中15种精度达10⁻²或更好。在真实物理景观LJ-13上映射了方法边界并报告失败原因。

Conclusion: 该检查能廉价地提供遗漏质量的证据，有时也能提供搜索已稳定的证据，但不能认证一次训练，其阈值是启发式的。

Abstract: Neural samplers are trained against an unnormalised target $\tildeπ=e^{-E}$ with no samples from $π$, which leaves the practitioner with no way to tell whether an expensive training run has silently dropped part of the target. The diagnostics in common use are computed from the model's own draws and are therefore confined to the model's support: we exhibit a sampler whose self-normalised effective sample size is $0.99$ while it misses $87\%$ of the target mass. We argue that \emph{detecting} missing mass is a strictly easier problem than sampling it: detection needs one point per missed basin plus a local curvature estimate, whereas correction needs the sampler retrained. We turn this into a pre-flight check that consumes a few percent of the sampler's own training budget and uses only $E$, $\nabla E$ and $\nabla^2 E$. On Gaussian-mixture, Many-Well and rotated anisotropic Many-Well targets with exactly computable ground truth, the check estimates the missing mass to within $10^{-3}$ at $2.7\%$ of training cost, where a tuned annealed SMC reference needs $70$--$280\%$ of training cost to do worse. It also applies unchanged to a controlled-SDE sampler that has no tractable density, where ESS and the ELBO cannot be formed at all. The estimator carries a \emph{self-diagnostic} that, without ground truth, is conservative in the safe direction: across $60$ configurations it clears $16$, of which $15$ are accurate to $10^{-2}$ or better. We are explicit about what this does and does not license: the check cheaply produces evidence of missing mass, and sometimes evidence that the search has stabilised, but it cannot certify a run, and its thresholds are heuristic. We then map the boundary of the method on a real physical landscape, LJ-13, and report where it fails and why.

</details>


### [305] [JAMPR+/L2D: scalable neural heuristic for constrained vehicle routing problems in dynamic environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26275)
*Andrew Soroka,Alex Meshcheryakov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文探讨将JAMPR+/L2D强化学习模型应用于带实际约束的车辆路径问题（CPDPTW），并在CVRP/VRPTW基准上验证其泛化性与高效微调策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统精确与启发式方法在中小规模（N≲100）问题上有效，但面对更大规模物流任务时缺乏适应性与可扩展性；同时实际场景中距离矩阵常发生较大变化，需要兼具精度与快速推理的方法。

Method: 采用JAMPR+/L2D深度强化学习模型求解CPDPTW，并考察图距离矩阵发生显著变化时的表现；对更大距离矩阵变化，提出对JAMPR+进行快速微调，而非全模型重训练。

Result: 在CVRPLIB基准的中等规模CVRP与VRPTW问题上，JAMPR+/L2D在超过85%的实例中优于当前最优启发式HGS，并改善了目标间隙；在CPDPTW上训练的模型对更简单约束任务、不同问题规模及适度距离矩阵变化具有良好泛化能力；在ORTEC数据上快速微调可显著降低目标间隙。

Conclusion: JAMPR+/L2D在CPDPTW及相关路径问题上具有较强泛化与竞争力，快速微调策略可在距离矩阵变化的实际路由场景中兼顾精度与快速推理。

Abstract: The vehicle routing problems with real-world constraints (we consider vehicles capacity limits, time windows constrains, pickup-and-delivery multi-depo --- CPDPTW) pose significant computational challenges. While classical exact and heuristic methods remain effective to solve problems of small/medium size ($N\lesssim100$), they often lack adaptability and scalability for larger logistics tasks. In this work, we show how JAMPR+/L2D RL deep learning model, proposed in to solve large CPDPTW problems can be adopted in the case of substantial changes of graph distance matrix. We test performance of JAMPR+/L2D model for medium-sized CVRP and VRPTW problems on CVRPLIB benchmarks: JAMPR+/L2D outperforms the state-of-the-art heuristic HGS in over 85\% of instances, achieving improvement in objective gap. We show that the JAMPR+/L2D model trained on CPDPTW problem, generalizes well for tasks with simpler constraints (CVRP, VRPTW), for different problem sizes and for moderate changes in distance matrixes. For more substantial changes in distance matrixes, we propose here to make fast finetuning of JAMPR+: on ORTEC data (for CPDPTW) the proposed strategy remarkably reduces the objective gap without full model retraining, what will give both accuracy and rapid inference of the model in the practical routing scenarios with distance matrix changes.

</details>


### [306] [On the Effect of Bit-Level Parameter Perturbations in Machine Learning and Deep Learning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26280)
*Akanksha Raghapur,Mark Stamp*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文比较经典机器学习模型（HMM、SVM）与深度学习模型（MLP、LSTM）对参数微小改动的敏感性和隐写容量，发现经典模型脆弱且隐写容量有限，神经网络则具有参数冗余和高隐写容量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究不同类别学习模型对参数微小、针对性修改的响应差异，并理解参数敏感性与隐写容量之间的关系，为鲁棒性和隐藏信息嵌入提供框架。

Method: 在Drebin安卓恶意软件数据集上，对经典模型（HMM、SVM）与神经网络模型（MLP、LSTM）进行参数修改实验，并开展隐写容量实验，衡量可被覆盖而不显著影响性能的参数比特比例。

Result: 经典模型表现脆弱，少量选定参数即可显著改变模型行为；其隐写容量有限，源于参数紧凑、高效且敏感。神经网络具有参数冗余，可将修改分散到众多参数上，隐写容量更高且性能影响最小。

Conclusion: 经典模型与神经模型在参数变化响应上存在明显差异，对鲁棒性和隐藏信息嵌入均有启示，本文提供了理解不同学习模型参数敏感性与隐写容量的框架。

Abstract: In this chapter, we investigate how classical machine learning models respond to small, targeted modifications in their parameters. We compare and contrast these results to analogous experiments on deep learning models. For classical learning models, we consider Hidden Markov Models (HMM) and Support Vector Machines (SVM), and for comparison, we conduct analogous experiments involving Multilayer Perceptrons (MLP) and Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) networks. When applied to the Drebin Android malware dataset, our results show that classical models are brittle, in the sense that a limited set of selected parameters can have a dramatic effect on model behavior. In a related set of experiments, we investigate the steganographic capacity of these same learning models, that is, the proportion of bits in model parameters that can be overwritten without having a significant adverse affect on a model. We find that classical models offer limited steganographic capacity due to their compact, parameter-efficient, and relatively sensitive parameter structure. In contrast, neural networks are parameter-redundant, enabling higher steganographic capacity, where modifications can be distributed across many parameters with minimal impact on performance. These results highlight differences in how classical and neural models respond to parameter changes, with clear implications for both robustness and hidden information embedding. Overall, this work provides a framework for understanding parameter sensitivity and steganographic capacity across different classes of learning models.

</details>


### [307] [Quantifying Protocol-Induced Uncertainty in Comparative Predictive-Model Evaluation: Evidence from Large-Scale Daily PM10 Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26288)
*Rafael da Silva,Kiersten Monahan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文将评估协议差异形式化为"协议诱导的排序不确定性"，提出PSS指标并引入协议内全重拟合参照，通过欧洲425站与美国365站PM10日预测实验证明：切换协议导致的模型排序变动远大于协议内扰动，模型选择结论会显著依赖合法的评估选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测模型比较研究通常以排序作结，但排序依赖评估协议，而协议本身对排序的影响很少被视为不确定性来源。作者希望量化这种被忽视的效应。

Method: 将问题形式化为协议诱导的排序不确定性；构建框架比较"切换协议"与"固定协议内常规选择"引起的排序位移；以协议敏感性得分（PSS）和协议内全重拟合参照进行量化。在PM10日预测研究中比较静态划分与滚动起点评估，覆盖欧洲425个背景站与365个美国EPA监测点，并扩展到3至9个候选模型与冻结协议下的留出站点检验。

Result: 切换协议产生平均PSS为0.801（欧洲）与0.772（美国），分别有35.3%与31.5%的站点改变选中模型。欧洲协议内扰动（评分目标相同）PSS为0.072与0.230，胜者互换率仅0.8%与4.9%，远小于协议间位移。候选集从3个增至9个时，欧洲协议间胜者互换率升至60.2%。该模式在冻结协议的留出站点上依然存在。

Conclusion: 模型选择结论会实质性依赖合法的评估协议选择；建议在声称模型优越性的同时，报告在一小组可辩护的协议内扰动下的排序稳定性。

Abstract: Comparative studies of predictive models often end by ranking candidate models, yet these rankings depend on evaluation protocols whose influence is rarely treated as a source of uncertainty. We formalize this problem as protocol-induced ranking uncertainty and introduce a framework that compares ranking displacement caused by switching protocols with displacement produced by conventional choices within a fixed protocol. We quantify these effects using the Protocol Sensitivity Score (PSS) and a full-refit intraprotocol reference.
  We validate the framework in a large-scale sequential prediction study of daily PM10. Static-split and rolling-origin evaluation are compared across 425 European background stations and 365 US EPA monitors. Switching protocols produces mean PSS values of 0.801 and 0.772 and changes the selected model at 35.3% and 31.5% of stations, respectively. In Europe, intraprotocol perturbations with identical scored targets produce PSS values of 0.072 and 0.230, with winner-swap rates of 0.8% and 4.9%. Between-protocol displacement is therefore substantially larger than the selected within-protocol references. Expanding the candidate set from three to nine models increases the between-protocol winner-swap rate to 60.2% in Europe. The pattern also persists under a frozen protocol applied to held-out background and non-background stations. These results show that model-selection conclusions can depend materially on legitimate evaluation choices. We recommend reporting ranking stability under a small set of defensible intraprotocol perturbations alongside claims of model superiority.

</details>


### [308] [CompKV: Compensation-Aware KV Selection for Long-Context LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26300)
*Zhen Huang,Ruizhe Yao,Danyi Liu,Xinrui Chen,Shuwei Li,Siru Zhong,Zijian Cao,Yushan Lai,Mingming Guo,Weijie Zheng,Haohuan Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 CompKV，一种补偿感知的稀疏注意力框架，通过将 token 分块并显式优化选择以适应下游补偿机制，提升长上下文 LLM 推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在长上下文推理中受限于 KV 缓存内存流量。现有稀疏注意力方法先按注意力质量选择 token，再对未选 token 进行粗粒度补偿，这种解耦设计忽略了选择与补偿之间的相互作用，未能优先保留遗漏后补偿误差最大的 token。

Method: CompKV 将 token 划分为块，显式针对下游补偿机制优化选择。理论分析表明块级均值补偿的残差由块注意力质量和块内 logit 变化共同决定，作者用紧凑的块级统计量近似该残差，得到可部署的选择准则，并设计了高效的异步实现。

Result: 在 RULER 和 LongBench-Pro 上，CompKV 在所评估的稀疏基线中表现最佳，相比完整注意力实现最高达 6.85 倍的自注意力加速。

Conclusion: 通过将选择与补偿机制耦合优化，CompKV 有效提升了稀疏注意力的质量与效率，为长上下文 LLM 推理提供了一种补偿感知的加速方案。

Abstract: Despite their strong performance, large language models (LLMs) are bottlenecked by KV cache memory traffic during long-context inference. Sparse attention is widely used to accelerate LLM inference by computing exact attention over a selected subset of tokens. To recover the contribution of tokens excluded from exact attention, recent methods apply coarse-grained compensation to the omitted attention tail. However, existing methods typically select tokens based on attention mass and only then compensate for the unselected tokens. This decoupled design overlooks their interaction: selection should prioritize tokens that would leave the largest compensation error if omitted. To address this limitation, we introduce CompKV, the first compensation-aware sparse attention framework that divides tokens into blocks and explicitly optimizes selection for the downstream compensation mechanism. Our theoretical analysis shows that the residual left by block-level mean compensation is governed by both block attention mass and within-block logit variation. We approximate this residual using compact block-level statistics, yielding a deployable selection criterion. We further develop an efficient asynchronous implementation. Experiments on RULER and LongBench-Pro show that CompKV performs best among the evaluated sparse baselines while delivering up to a $6.85\times$ self-attention speedup over full attention.

</details>


### [309] [PreGS: A Parameter-Transfer-Based Multi-Expert Graph Neural Network for Node Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26310)
*Zhicong Cai,Yinglong Zhang,Xiaoying Hong,Xuewen Xia,Xing Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PreGS框架，通过参数迁移将预训练多头GAT的线性变换权重迁移给多个冻结的GraphSAGE专家，形成互补结构分支，并与MLP、GAT logits融合；进一步提出PreGSv2引入源级加权与结构门控机制，在八个公开图数据集上取得竞争性表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单一聚合机制难以刻画不同图数据集中的多样结构模式，而独立训练多个结构分支会带来大量开销且未必产生稳定的节点表示，因此需要一种高效且稳定的多专家图神经网络方案。

Method: 先预训练多头图注意力网络（GAT），将其第一层注意力头的线性变换权重迁移到多个GraphSAGE专家并冻结，作为互补结构分支；将原始节点特征、GAT各头表示与GraphSAGE专家表示融合后输入MLP，输出再与预训练GAT logits融合。在此基础上提出PreGSv2，加入源级加权与结构门控机制实现自适应多源特征集成。

Result: 在八个公开图数据集上，PreGS与PreGSv2相较代表性图神经网络基线取得竞争性性能；消融、参数迁移、敏感性、聚合器、可视化及训练时间等分析验证了框架的有效性与稳定性。

Conclusion: 基于参数迁移的多专家框架能够以较低额外开销获得多样且稳定的结构表示，PreGSv2的加权与门控进一步提升了多源特征融合效果，代码与数据集已开源。

Abstract: Graph neural networks have achieved strong performance in node classification by aggregating information from graph neighborhoods. However, a single aggregation mechanism may be insufficient to capture diverse structural patterns across graph datasets. Moreover, independently training multiple structural branches can introduce substantial overhead without necessarily producing stable node representations. To address these issues, this paper proposes PreGS, a parameter-transfer-based multi-expert graph neural network framework. PreGS first pretrains a multi-head graph attention network (GAT) and transfers the linear transformation weights of its first-layer attention heads to multiple GraphSAGE experts. The transferred experts are frozen and used as complementary structural branches. The fused raw node features, GAT head representations, and GraphSAGE expert representations are fed into a multilayer perceptron (MLP), whose output is further fused with the pretrained GAT logits. Based on PreGS, we further develop PreGSv2, which introduces source-level weighting and a structural gating mechanism for adaptive multi-source feature integration. Experiments on eight public graph datasets show that PreGS and PreGSv2 achieve competitive performance against representative graph neural network baselines. Ablation, parameter-transfer, sensitivity, aggregator, visualization, and training-time analyses further validate the effectiveness and stability of the proposed framework. The code and datasets are available at https://github.com/LH-Czc/PreGS.

</details>


### [310] [Disaggregated Quantization: Specializing LLM Prefill and Decode](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26333)
*Andrei Panferov,Maximilian Kleinegger,Sweta Priyadarshi,Tijmen Blankevoort,Dan Alistarh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"解耦量化"(DQ)，针对预填充与解码两个阶段分别特化计算格式、权重与存储布局，在不增加推理成本的前提下提升精度与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预填充和解码对量化的需求不同：低精度算术加速提示处理，而紧凑权重减少生成时的内存流量。现有方法未针对这两阶段做专门化处理。

Method: 提出解耦量化(DQ)：在解码阶段移除激活量化；训练独立的计算原生预填充权重；提出卸载式解耦预填充(ODP)，从SSD流式加载权重以摊销加载开销。

Result: 在Qwen 3和Gemma 3上，解码阶段去除激活量化提升了重解码任务精度且不增加成本；训练NVFP4预填充器使1-bit精度在MMLU-Pro提升32.5点、MMMU-Pro提升35.3点；ODP在27B模型8K提示下实现1.78倍首token加速。并在vLLM及最大2.8T参数模型上完成验证。

Conclusion: 针对预填充与解码阶段解耦量化与存储布局，可同时提升精度与推理效率，并具备扩展到超大规模模型的可行性。

Abstract: Prefill and decode reward different approaches to quantization: low-precision arithmetic accelerates prompt processing, while compact weights reduce memory traffic during generation. We propose "disaggregated quantization" (DQ), which specializes computation formats, weights and storage placement to both of these phases. On Qwen 3 and Gemma 3, removing activation quantization specifically on decode improves accuracy on decode-heavy tasks without increasing inference cost. Training separate compute-native prefill weights accelerates prompt processing relative to weight-only inference while matching or exceeding its accuracy at 2-3-bit decode on both decode-heavy and prefill-heavy tasks. With released Qwen3.8-27B GGUF decoders, training an NVFP4 prefiller improves 1-bit accuracy by 32.5 points on MMLU-Pro and 35.3 on MMMU-Pro without modifying the decode checkpoint. To accommodate the additional checkpoint on a single device, offloaded disaggregated prefill (ODP) streams its weights from SSD, amortizing loading over prompt length. On the same 27B model, ODP delivers a 1.78x time-to-first-token speedup over the weight-only baseline at 8K prompt length in llama.cpp. We evaluate accuracy under disaggregated serving in vLLM and further validate shared-weight format disaggregation through post-training quantization on models up to 2.8T parameters.

</details>


### [311] [Geometry-Aware Hyperbolic Residual Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26342)
*Alessio Colombo,Melika Ayoughi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出几何感知的双曲残差量化方法，解决双曲空间中残差聚合不一致与梯度估计忽略几何的问题，在层次化任务上提升了稳定性与结构组织。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 残差向量量化（RVQ）可将连续表示转为离散多层级令牌序列，但现有方法多基于欧氏空间，忽略了所得码本由粗到细的结构以及许多数据域中固有的潜在层次性。双曲几何天然适合层次表示，然而直接做双曲扩展会带来几何不一致：双曲加法非结合性使残差聚合无法保持一致性，标准直通梯度估计又忽略了潜在空间的几何结构。

Method: 提出几何感知的双曲残差量化，在前向与反向传播中同时处理上述问题。前向传播中，Hyperbolic Residual Aggregation 在庞加莱球上恢复残差量化的"望远镜式"（逐级抵消）行为；反向传播中，采用 discounted Hyperbolic Straight-Through Estimator，将重建梯度作为单一几何块通过量化器传导，避免跨残差阶段的递归梯度传输不稳定。

Result: 在层次预测、推荐、图像分词和神经音频编码等任务上的评估表明，相比朴素双曲基线，该方法提升了双曲残差码的稳定性与结构组织性。同时观察到明显的"结构—压缩"权衡：纯压缩场景下欧氏残差量化仍更优，而几何感知双曲量化在层次化组织的离散潜在空间中最有价值。

Conclusion: 几何感知双曲残差量化在前后向中修复了双曲扩展的几何不一致问题，适合层次化离散潜在空间；但纯压缩任务仍以欧氏 RVQ 为宜，方法价值取决于数据结构是否具备层次性。

Abstract: Residual Vector Quantization turns continuous representations into discrete, multi-level token sequences. Yet most methods operate in Euclidean space, despite the coarse-to-fine structure of the resulting codes and the latent hierarchies present in many data domains. Hyperbolic geometry offers a natural alternative for hierarchical representations, but naive hyperbolic extensions introduce geometric inconsistencies: non-associative hyperbolic addition prevents consistent residual aggregation, while standard straight-through gradient estimation ignores the geometry of the latent space. We propose a geometry-aware hyperbolic residual quantization that addresses these issues in both the forward and backward passes. In the forward pass, Hyperbolic Residual Aggregation restores the telescoping behavior of residual quantization on the Poincare ball. In the backward pass, a discounted Hyperbolic Straight-Through Estimator routes the reconstruction gradient through the quantizer as a single geometric block, avoiding unstable recursive gradient transport across residual stages. Evaluations on hierarchical prediction, recommendation, image tokenization, and neural audio coding tasks show that our method improves the stability and structural organization of hyperbolic residual codes over naive hyperbolic baselines. At the same time, we observe a clear structure-compression trade-off: Euclidean residual quantization remains preferable for pure compression, while geometry-aware hyperbolic quantization is most useful for hierarchically organized discrete latent spaces.

</details>


### [312] [PACT: From Credit Assignment to Critic Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26355)
*Jiayan Fu,Hang Xu,Yong Zhang,Zhaokai Luo,Yao Hu,Dongyan Zhao,Mu Chuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出token级信用分配的三个正则条件，证明其唯一确定token级信用，并据此提出PACT训练方法，在数学推理和SWE-bench上超越GRPO和PPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习已成为大语言模型后训练的核心组件，但token级信用（token-level credit）缺乏公认的数学定义，导致其与常用训练信号之间的关系不清晰。本文希望建立统一的理论基础来厘清这一关系，并指导更优的actor-critic训练流程设计。

Method: 作者提出三个正则条件——完备性（Completeness）、前缀一致性（Prefix Consistency）和中立性（Neutrality），并证明它们唯一确定token级信用。基于该刻画，分析了on-policy蒸馏中理想教师作为隐式评论家、RLOO信号与token级信用的关系，并在有界结果奖励下建立近似信用稀疏性，指出GAE中间评论家误差的影响。据此提出PACT（Policy Aligned Critic Training），采用"先Actor后Critic"的更新顺序，对评论家训练施加重要性采样校正，使其更好对齐更新后的策略。

Result: 在智能体数学推理任务中，PACT在四个基准上平均准确率达72.87%，分别超越GRPO和PPO 8.80和13.16个百分点。在SWE-bench Verified上，PACT通过率达67.4%，分别超越PPO、GRPO和SAO 2.4、2.0和3.8个百分点。

Conclusion: 通过正则条件唯一刻画token级信用，本文为现有算法现象提供了统一解释框架，并据此设计出PACT方法。PACT在数学推理与软件工程任务上均取得优于主流方法的性能，验证了理论指导训练流程改进的有效性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has become a central component of large language model (LLM) post-training, yet token-level credit lacks a generally accepted mathematical definition, leaving its relationship to commonly used training signals unclear. We formulate three regularity conditions, namely Completeness, Prefix Consistency, and Neutrality, and prove that they uniquely determine token-level credit. This characterization provides a unified basis for explaining phenomena across existing algorithms and guides the development of an improved actor-critic training procedure. Through this lens, an ideal teacher in On-Policy Distillation (OPD) acts as an implicit critic, yielding an expected policy gradient proportional to that induced by token-level credit. Response-level REINFORCE Leave-One-Out (RLOO) signals match the expected policy-gradient contribution of token-level credit despite their coarser granularity. We further establish approximate credit sparsity under bounded outcome rewards and show how intermediate critic errors in Generalized Advantage Estimation (GAE) can become comparable to the underlying credit. These motivate Policy Aligned Critic Training (PACT), which adopts an Actor-then-Critic update order to apply importance sampling correction to critic training and better align the critic with the updated policy. In agentic mathematical reasoning, PACT achieves 72.87% average accuracy across four benchmarks, outperforming GRPO and PPO by 8.80 and 13.16 percentage points, respectively. On SWE-bench Verified, PACT achieves a pass rate of 67.4%, outperforming PPO, GRPO, and SAO by 2.4, 2.0, and 3.8 percentage points, respectively.

</details>


### [313] [FairMean: Promoting Fairness in Distributed Learning under Label Poisoning Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26377)
*Huigan Zheng,Jiaojiao Zhang,Yongxiang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FairMean方法，用有界非减的损失函数对客户端梯度加权，在公平性与标签投毒鲁棒性之间取得平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公平性感知的分布式学习倾向于给损失大的客户端更高权重以减少性能差异，但标签投毒会人为制造大损失，从而引发公平性与鲁棒性的冲突。

Method: FairMean对客户端梯度使用基于本地损失的有界非减函数进行加权：递增的权重优先高损失客户端以促进公平；权重上界则防止投毒客户端梯度被过度放大。

Result: 无标签投毒时，最小化FairMean目标比最小化标准平均损失目标更有利于解公平性；存在投毒时，建立了平均稳定性界，其攻击相关项与投毒客户端比例的平方成正比。实验显示FairMean通过降低准确率方差提升公平性，同时改善最差客户端准确率。

Conclusion: FairMean在理论上和实验上均能有效缓解公平性与鲁棒性之间的冲突。

Abstract: Fairness-aware distributed learning prioritizes clients with large losses to reduce performance disparities, but label poisoning can create large losses, thereby inducing a fairness--robustness conflict. We propose FairMean to manage this conflict. FairMean weights client gradients using a bounded, nondecreasing function of local loss. The increasing weights prioritize high-loss clients to promote fairness, while the upper bound prevents excessive loss-induced amplification of poisoned-client gradients. In the absence of label poisoning, we show that minimizing the FairMean objective is more conducive to solution fairness than minimizing the standard average-loss objective. Under label poisoning, we establish an average-stationarity bound whose attack-dependent term is proportional to the square of the poisoned-client fraction. Experiments show that FairMean promotes fairness by reducing accuracy variance while improving worst-client accuracy.

</details>


### [314] [Learning to Defer with Guidance on Real World Medical Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26384)
*Emma Sun,Joshua Strong,Alison Noble*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次在真实医学影像数据（含人类标注）上评估两阶段学习推迟（L2D），并提出"带引导的L2D"新设置，在三个胸部X光数据集上验证其优于经典L2D及各类基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学影像解读量大耗时，AI可减轻负荷，但完全自主部署存在安全隐患，且低特异性可能反而增加临床医生工作量。L2D虽已有理论保证，却尚未在含人类读者标注的真实医学数据集上验证其性能。

Method: 在Collab-CXR（每例多人类标注的多标签胸片数据集）上评估两阶段L2D的"预测器-拒绝器"形式，其中AI预测器固定、路由模型可训练。提出新设置"带引导的L2D"，将决策空间扩展为三种：自主预测、推迟给人类专家、或推迟并附AI引导。比较多种拒绝器架构、损失函数与不同输入特征可用性，并在VinDr-CXR和CheXpert上复现。

Result: 带引导的两阶段L2D优于经典两阶段L2D，也优于人类单独、AI单独及AI引导人类等基线。值得注意的是，该性能是用比现有文献中形式化L2D替代损失更简单的损失函数实现的。

Conclusion: 本研究首次在真实医学影像数据（含人类标注）背景下考察L2D，证明带引导的L2D能有效提升人机协作性能，且实现方式相对简单，具备临床应用潜力。

Abstract: Medical image interpretation is high-volume and time-consuming, and while AI interpretation can reduce workload, fully autonomous deployment carries potential safety concerns and low specificity may in practice lead to increased clinician workload. Learning to Defer (L2D) addresses this by selectively routing cases between autonomous prediction and human experts by learning from input features and AI model and human performance.
